--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./main.py ---
import os

from telegram.error import TimedOut, NetworkError
from telegram.ext import Application

from bot.exchange.market_data import market_data
from bot.exchange.paper_exchange import paper
from bot.core.state import bot_state
from bot.core.remote_state import ensure_branch
from bot.services.reports import build_report, build_audit_html
from bot.services.info import info_full_text, generate_whitepaper
from bot.strategy.shadow import shadow
from bot.strategy.scanner import SCAN_SUMMARY, FILTERED_BY_NEWS, get_thresholds

    format_coin, sl_emoji, grade_badge, mode_emoji,
    usd, pnl_emoji, weight_emoji, fmt_price, fmt_pct, fmt_sym
)

_app = None
WEBHOOK_PATH = "/telegram-webhook"


def _is_authorized(chat_id) -> bool:
    admin_chat_id = str(os.getenv("TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID", "")).strip()
    if not admin_chat_id:
        return True
    if str(chat_id) != admin_chat_id:
        logger.warning(f"🚨 Попытка взлома! Заблокирован доступ от чата: {chat_id}")
        return False
    return True


async def health_handler(request):
    return web.Response(status=204)


async def log_download_handler(request):
    """Прямое скачивание файла лога через браузер."""
    src = Path("logs/bot.log")
    if not src.exists():
        return web.Response(text="Файл лога пока пуст.", status=404)
    name = f"log_{datetime.now().strftime('%H%M%S')}.txt"
    return web.Response(
        body=src.read_bytes(),
        content_type="text/plain; charset=utf-8",
        headers={"Content-Disposition": f'attachment; filename="{name}"'}
    )


async def whitepaper_download_handler(request):
    """Прямое скачивание документации Whitepaper."""
    text = generate_whitepaper()
    return web.Response(
        body=text.encode("utf-8"),
        content_type="text/plain; charset=utf-8",
        headers={"Content-Disposition": 'attachment; filename="CaptainRost_Whitepaper.txt"'}
    )


async def fix_telegram_handler(request):
    """
    Сервисная веб-страница: восстанавливает Telegram Webhook прямо из браузера пользователя
    (в обход исходящего фаервола Render) и позволяет управлять ботом в 1 клик.
    """
    token = os.getenv("TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN", "").strip()
    public_url = os.getenv("RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL", "https://captain-rost-bot.onrender.com").rstrip("/")
    webhook_url = f"{public_url}{WEBHOOK_PATH}"

    action = request.query.get("action", "")
    action_result = ""
    if action == "resetstats":
        action_result = await action_resetstats()
    elif action == "resetlearn":
        action_result = await action_resetlearn()
    elif action == "start":
        bot_state.fresh_start()
        action_result = "🤖 Капитан Рост: торговля запущена!"
    elif action == "pause":
        action_result = await action_pause()
    elif action == "resume":
        action_result = await action_resume()

    html = f"""<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="ru">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
<title>Капитан Рост — Восстановление связи Telegram</title>
<style>
  body {{ background:#0f1218; color:#e6edf3; font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Segoe UI",Roboto,sans-serif; padding:24px; max-width:720px; margin:0 auto; }}
  .card {{ background:#171c26; border:1px solid #262f40; border-radius:10px; padding:20px; margin-bottom:16px; }}
  h2 {{ margin-top:0; color:#00e5ff; }}
  .btn {{ display:inline-block; text-decoration:none; color:#fff; background:#2962ff; padding:10px 16px; border-radius:6px; font-weight:600; margin:6px 6px 6px 0; border:none; cursor:pointer; font-size:14px; }}
  .btn-green {{ background:#00c853; }}
  .btn-orange {{ background:#ff9100; }}
  .status-box {{ padding:12px; border-radius:8px; background:#1d2432; border:1px solid #334155; margin-top:12px; font-family:monospace; font-size:13px; white-space:pre-wrap; }}
</style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="card">
    <h2>🔧 Авто-восстановление Telegram Webhook</h2>
    <p>Эта страница автоматически прописывает адрес вебхука <code>{webhook_url}</code> напрямую через твой браузер.</p>
    <button class="btn btn-green" onclick="registerWebhook()">🔄 Привязать Webhook Telegram сейчас</button>
    <div id="wh-status" class="status-box">Проверка и привязка вебхука...</div>
  </div>

  {"<div class='card' style='border-color:#00e676;'><b>✅ Выполнено:</b><br>" + action_result + "</div>" if action_result else ""}

  <div class="card">
    <h2>🎛 Прямое управление ботом (работает всегда)</h2>
    <a class="btn btn-green" href="/fix-telegram?action=start">🚀 Запустить торговлю (/start)</a>
    <a class="btn btn-orange" href="/fix-telegram?action=resetstats">📊 Сбросить статистику ($1,000)</a>
    <a class="btn btn-orange" href="/fix-telegram?action=resetlearn">🧠♻️ Сбросить опыт ИИ</a>
    <a class="btn" href="/fix-telegram?action=pause">⏸ Пауза</a>
    <a class="btn" href="/fix-telegram?action=resume">▶️ Возобновить</a>
    <hr style="border:0; border-top:1px solid #262f40; margin:16px 0;">
    <a class="btn" href="/audit?hours=48">📊 Открыть дашборд /audit (48ч)</a>
    <a class="btn" href="/download-log">📥 Скачать лог (bot.log)</a>
  </div>

<script>
const TOKEN = {json.dumps(token)};
const WEBHOOK_URL = {json.dumps(webhook_url)};

async function registerWebhook() {{
  const box = document.getElementById('wh-status');
  box.innerHTML = '⏳ Отправка запроса в api.telegram.org...';
  try {{
    const setUrl = `https://api.telegram.org/bot${{TOKEN}}/setWebhook?url=${{encodeURIComponent(WEBHOOK_URL)}}&drop_pending_updates=true`;
    const r = await fetch(setUrl);
    const data = await r.json();
    const infoR = await fetch(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${{TOKEN}}/getWebhookInfo`);
    const info = await infoR.json();
    if (data.ok) {{
      box.style.borderColor = '#00e676';
      box.innerHTML = '✅ ВЕБХУК УСПЕШНО УСТАНОВЛЕН! Теперь все команды в Telegram (/status, /resetstats, /chart и др.) работают мгновенно!\\n\\n' + JSON.stringify(info.result, null, 2);
    }} else {{
      box.style.borderColor = '#ff5252';
      box.innerHTML = '⚠️ Ответ Telegram: ' + JSON.stringify(data, null, 2);
    }}
  }} catch (e) {{
    box.style.borderColor = '#ff5252';
    box.innerHTML = '⚠️ Браузер не смог достучаться до api.telegram.org (включи VPN в браузере и нажми кнопку выше): ' + e;
  }}
}}

window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', registerWebhook);
</script>
</body>
</html>"""
    return web.Response(text=html, content_type="text/html")


async def audit_handler(request):
    try:
        tz = ZoneInfo(os.getenv("TIMEZONE", "Europe/Moscow"))
        public_url = os.getenv("RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL", "https://captain-rost-bot.onrender.com")
        try:
            hours = int(request.query.get("hours", 48))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            hours = 48
        html_page = await build_audit_html(tz, public_url=public_url, hours=hours)
        compressed_html = gzip.compress(html_page.encode("utf-8"))
        return web.Response(
            body=compressed_html,
            content_type="text/html",
            headers={
                "Content-Encoding": "gzip",
                "Vary": "Accept-Encoding"
            }
        )
    except Exception as e:
        logger.exception(f"audit_handler error: {e}")
        return web.Response(text=f"Ошибка генерации дашборда /audit: {e}", status=500)


async def chart_handler(request):
    symbol = request.query.get("symbol")
    interval_str = request.query.get("interval", "60")

    if not symbol:
        return web.Response(text="Укажите тикер, например ?symbol=LINKUSDT", status=400)
    symbol = symbol.upper()
    if not symbol.endswith("USDT"):
        symbol += "USDT"

    valid_intervals = ["1", "3", "5", "15", "30", "60", "120", "240", "D", "W"]
    if interval_str not in valid_intervals:
        interval_str = "60"

    def make_chart():
        try:
            from bot.utils.visualizer import TradeVisualizer
            viz = TradeVisualizer(log_path="logs/bot.log", symbol=symbol, interval=interval_str)
            fig = viz.build_chart(show=False)
            if fig is None:
                return None

            raw_html = fig.to_html(include_plotlyjs="cdn", full_html=True)

            buttons_html = (
                '<div style="position: absolute; top: 15px; left: 50%; transform: translateX(-50%); z-index: 1000; '
                'background: rgba(30, 30, 30, 0.85); padding: 8px 15px; border-radius: 8px; '
                'border: 1px solid #444; font-family: Arial, sans-serif; '
                'box-shadow: 0 4px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.3); display: flex; align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 4px;">'
                '<a href="/audit?hours=48" style="text-decoration: none; color: #00e5ff; background: rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.14); '
                'border: 1px solid rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.4); padding: 5px 10px; margin-right: 6px; border-radius: 4px; '
                'font-size: 13px; font-weight: bold;">📊 Аудит 48ч</a>'
                '<button id="chart-shot-btn" onclick="takeChartScreenshot()" '
                'style="cursor: pointer; color: #00e676; background: rgba(0, 230, 118, 0.16); '
                'border: 1px solid rgba(0, 230, 118, 0.45); padding: 5px 10px; margin-right: 10px; border-radius: 4px; '
                'font-size: 13px; font-weight: bold;">📸 Скриншот</button>'
                '<span style="color: #ccc; margin-right: 6px; font-size: 14px;">ТФ:</span>'
            )

            for tf in valid_intervals:
                bg_color = "#2962ff" if tf == interval_str else "#444"
                text_color = "#fff" if tf == interval_str else "#ccc"
                hover_style = "this.style.background='#555'" if tf != interval_str else ""
                out_style = f"this.style.background='{bg_color}'"

                buttons_html += (
                    f'<a href="?symbol={symbol}&interval={tf}" '
                    f'style="text-decoration: none; color: {text_color}; background: {bg_color}; '
                    f'padding: 5px 10px; margin: 0 2px; border-radius: 4px; font-size: 13px; font-weight: bold; transition: 0.2s;" '
                    f'onmouseover="{hover_style}" onmouseout="{out_style}">{tf}</a>'
                )

            buttons_html += "</div>"

            shot_script = f"""
            <script>
              async function takeChartScreenshot() {{
                const btn = document.getElementById('chart-shot-btn');
                const orig = btn.innerHTML;
                btn.innerHTML = '⏳ Снимок...';
                btn.disabled = true;
                try {{
                  const gd = document.querySelector('.js-plotly-plot');
                  const dataUrl = await Plotly.toImage(gd, {{
                    format: 'png',
                    width: 1600,
                    height: 950,
                    scale: 2
                  }});
                  const a = document.createElement('a');
                  const nowStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 16).replace('T', '_').replace(':', '-');
                  a.href = dataUrl;
                  a.download = 'CaptainRost_{symbol}_{interval_str}_' + nowStr + '.png';
                  document.body.appendChild(a);
                  a.click();
                  a.remove();
                }} catch (e) {{
                  alert('Ошибка скриншота: ' + e);
                }} finally {{
                  btn.innerHTML = orig;
                  btn.disabled = false;
                }}
              }}
            </script>
            """

            patched = raw_html.replace(
                "<body>",
                f"<body style='margin:0; padding:0; background-color:#111;'>\n{buttons_html}"
            )
            return patched.replace("</body>", f"{shot_script}\n</body>")

        except Exception as e:
            return str(e)

    try:
        html_or_error = await asyncio.to_thread(make_chart)
        if html_or_error is None:
            return web.Response(text=f"Нет данных лога или свечей для {symbol}.", status=404)
        if not html_or_error.startswith("<"):
            return web.Response(text=f"Ошибка генерации: {html_or_error}", status=500)

        compressed_html = gzip.compress(html_or_error.encode("utf-8"))

        return web.Response(
            body=compressed_html,
            content_type="text/html",
            headers={
                "Content-Encoding": "gzip",
                "Vary": "Accept-Encoding"
            }
        )
    except Exception as e:
        return web.Response(text=f"Внутренняя ошибка сервера: {e}", status=500)


async def send_chat(text):
    chat = os.getenv("TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID")
    token = os.getenv("TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN")
    if not (chat and token):
        return

    url = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{token}/sendMessage"
    for attempt in range(2):
        try:
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=8.0) as client:
                r = await client.post(url, json={
                    "chat_id": chat,
                    "text": text,
                    "parse_mode": "HTML",
                    "disable_web_page_preview": True,
                })
                if r.status_code == 200:
                    return
                await client.post(url, json={
                    "chat_id": chat,
                    "text": text,
                    "disable_web_page_preview": True,
                })
                return
        except Exception as e:
            if attempt == 0:
                await asyncio.sleep(1.0)
            else:
                logger.debug(f"Фоновое уведомление Telegram отложено ({type(e).__name__})")


async def report_loop():
    tz = ZoneInfo(os.getenv("TIMEZONE", "Europe/Moscow"))
    last_sent = None
    while True:
        try:
            now = datetime.now(tz)
            if now.hour == 21 and now.minute < 5 and last_sent != now.date():
                last_sent = now.date()
                await send_chat(await build_report("daily", tz))
                if now.weekday() == 6:
                    await send_chat(await build_report("weekly", tz))
                if now.day == calendar.monthrange(now.year, now.month)[1]:
                    await send_chat(await build_report("monthly", tz))
        except Exception as e:
            logger.exception(f"report error: {e}")
        await asyncio.sleep(30)


async def action_pause(context=None):
    if bot_state.paused:
        return "⏸ Уже на паузе."
    orders = list(paper.orders)
    paper.orders = []
    paper.save()
    bot_state.pause(orders)
    return f"⏸ <b>Пауза</b>: ордеров снято {len(orders)}, позиции открыты."


async def action_resume(context=None):
    if not bot_state.paused:
        return "▶️ Не на паузе."
    orders = bot_state.resume()
    paper.orders.extend(orders)
    paper.save()
    return f"▶ <b>Возобновлено</b>: ордеров восстановлено {len(orders)}."


async def action_exitall(context=None):
    bot_state.trading_enabled = False
    prices = await market_data.get_tickers()
    results = paper.sell_all(prices)
    total = sum(r["pnl"] for r in results)
    return (
        f"🛑 <b>Торговля остановлена</b>\n"
        f"Позиций закрыто: {len(results)} · {pnl_emoji(total)} {total:+.2f}%\n"
        f"💰 Баланс: <b>{usd(paper.usdt)}</b>\n"
        f"🧠 Опыт обучения сохранён."
    )


async def action_resetlearn(context=None):
    learner.reset()
    shadow.reset()
    return "🧠♻️ <b>Опыт ИИ сброшен</b>\n• Веса возвращены к 1.0\n• Журнал автотюна очищен."


async def action_resetstats(context=None):
    paper.reset_stats()
    learner.reset_stats()
    return "📊 <b>Статистика сброшена</b>\n💰 Баланс: <b>$1,000.00</b>\n📦 Ордера и позиции очищены."


ACTIONS = {
    "pause": (action_pause, "поставить торговлю на паузу?"),
    "resume": (action_resume, "возобновить торговлю?"),
    "exitall": (action_exitall, "продать ВСЕ позиции и остановить торговлю?"),
    "resetlearn": (action_resetlearn, "сбросить опыт обучения (веса и историю)?"),
    "resetstats": (action_resetstats, "сбросить торговую статистику?"),
}


def _confirm_payload(key: str):
    _, question = ACTIONS[key]
    markup = {
        "inline_keyboard": [[
            {"text": "✅ Подтвердить", "callback_data": f"confirm:{key}"},
            {"text": "❌ Отмена", "callback_data": "cancel"},
        ]]
    }
    return f"⚠️ <b>Подтвердите:</b> {_html.escape(question)}", markup


async def _build_learn_text() -> str:
    wr, n = learner.winrate()
    a_stats = learner.get_grade_a_stats()
    lines = ["🧠 <b>Обучение бота (ИИ)</b>", ""]
    lines.append("📌 <b>Текущие параметры</b>")
    lines.append(
        f"🎯 Чистый Winrate: <b>{wr:.0%}</b> <i>(за {n} сдел.)</i> · "
        f"🛡🔒 БУ: <b>{learner.be_count}</b> · строгость: <b>{learner.threshold_adj:+.2f}</b>"
    )
    lines.append(
        f"💎 <b>Сетапы [💎]:</b> WR <b>{a_stats['wr']:.0%}</b> ({a_stats['decisive']} рез. + {a_stats['be']} 🛡🔒) · "
        f"ср. {a_stats['avg']:+.2f}% · серия {a_stats['streak']:+d} · вес <b>×{a_stats['mult']:.2f}</b>"
    )

    lines.append("\n🧭 <b>Где деньги</b>")
    if learner.sector_stats:
        rows = []
        for s, h in learner.sector_stats.items():
            dec = [p for p in h if not is_neutral_be(p)]
            if dec:
                rows.append((s, sum(1 for p in dec if p > 0) / len(dec), len(dec), sum(dec) / len(dec), learner.sector_bias(s)))
        rows.sort(key=lambda r: r[4], reverse=True)
        for s, swr, cnt, avg, bias in rows:
            lines.append(f"   {pnl_emoji(avg)} <i>{s}</i> · wr {swr:.0%} ({cnt}) · {avg:+.2f}% → {bias:+.2f}")

    lines.append("\n🎯 <b>Веса индикаторов контекста</b>")
    for k, v in sorted(learner.weights.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1], reverse=True):
        lines.append(f"   {weight_emoji(v)} <i>{k}</i> · {v:.2f} {'⚡' * max(1, int(round(v * 5)))}")

    lines.append("\n🧩 <b>Паттерны Price Action</b>")
    pat_names = {
        "pinbar": "🔨 Пин-бар выкупа (Spring)",
        "divergence": "🧲 Дивергенция RSI",
        "vcp": "📦 Сжатие волатильности (VCP)",
        "breakout": "🚀 Импульсный пробой",
        "engulfing": "⚡ Бычье поглощение"
    }
    if learner.pattern_stats:
        for p, h in sorted(learner.pattern_stats.items()):
            dec = [x for x in h if not is_neutral_be(x)]
            if dec:
                pwr = sum(1 for x in dec if x > 0) / len(dec)
                pavg = sum(dec) / len(dec)
                pmult = learner.pattern_multiplier(p)
                lines.append(f"   {pnl_emoji(pavg)} {pat_names.get(p, p)}: {len(dec)} сдел. · wr {pwr:.0%} · ср. {pavg:+.2f}% (×{pmult:.2f})")
    else:
        lines.append("   <i>(накапливается статистика по сетапам)</i>")

    lines.append("\n🧾 <b>Как выходим</b>")
    if learner.exit_stats:
        rows = sorted(
            [(t, sum(1 for p in h if p > 0) / len(h), len(h), sum(h) / len(h)) for t, h in learner.exit_stats.items() if h],
            key=lambda r: r[3],
            reverse=True
        )
        for t, twr, cnt, avg in rows:
            lines.append(f"   {pnl_emoji(avg)} {t} · {cnt} сдел. · wr {twr:.0%} · {avg:+.2f}%")

    mem = memory_stats()
    lines.append(f"\n🗂 <b>Память:</b> 📚 База {mem['base']} + выучено {mem['learned']} = <b>{mem['total']}</b>")
    lines.extend(shadow.learn_lines())
    return "\n".join(lines)


async def _build_funding_text() -> str:
    prices = await market_data.get_tickers()
    coins = getattr(paper, "funding_coins", {})
    usdt_fund = paper.funding

    lines = ["🏦 <b>Накопительный фонд (Funding Vault)</b>", ""]
    lines.append(f"💵 <b>Стейблкоины (USDT):</b> <b>{usd(usdt_fund)}</b>")
    lines.append("<i>Резерв 30% с профита альткоинов. Доступен для ручного вывода или экстренных нужд.</i>\n")

    active_coins = {s: q for s, q in coins.items() if q > 0.000001}
    if active_coins:
        coins_val = sum(qty * prices.get(s, {}).get("last", 0) for s, qty in active_coins.items())
        lines.append(f"🐋 <b>HODL-портфель китов (TOP20): ~{usd(coins_val)}</b>")
        for sym, qty in sorted(active_coins.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1] * prices.get(kv[0], {}).get("last", 0), reverse=True):
            last = prices.get(sym, {}).get("last", 0)
            val = qty * last
            coin_name = sym[:-4] if sym.endswith("USDT") else sym
            lines.append(f"• <b>{coin_name}</b>: {qty:.6f} (~{usd(val)}) · по ${fmt_price(last)}")
        lines.append("\n<i>Эти монеты не участвуют в торговле и накапливаются как чистый крипто-актив.</i>")
    else:
        lines.append("🐋 <b>HODL-портфель китов:</b> монет пока нет")

    return "\n".join(lines)


async def _build_news_text() -> str:
    from bot.news.cmc import get_stats as cmc_stats
    from bot.news.rss_news import get_stats as rss_stats
    cmc, rss = cmc_stats(), rss_stats()
    lines = ["📰 <b>Новостная аналитика</b>\n\n📡 <b>RSS-ленты</b>"]
    if rss["feeds_working"]:
        lines.append(f"   ✅ работают · {rss['items_count']} новостей · ⏱ {rss['cache_age_min']} мин назад")
        if rss["neg_examples"]:
            lines.append(f"   ⚠️ негатив: {_html.escape(rss['neg_examples'][0][:60])}…")
    else:
        lines.append("   ❌ ленты недоступны")
    lines.append(f"\n🏷 <b>CoinMarketCap:</b> {'✅' if cmc['api_key_set'] else '❌'} API · 📚 {cmc['sectors_learned']} сек · 🏆 {cmc['ranks_cached']} рангов")
    if FILTERED_BY_NEWS:
        lines.append("\n🚫 <b>Отфильтровано новостями:</b>")
        for i in FILTERED_BY_NEWS[-5:]:
            lines.append(f"   • {_html.escape(fmt_sym(i['symbol']))} · {i['neg_count']}")
    return "\n".join(lines)


async def _build_chart_text(args: list) -> str:
    import time
    arg = (args or [None])[0]
    public_url = os.getenv("RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL", "https://captain-rost-bot.onrender.com")

    if not arg:
        cutoff = int(time.time()) - 48 * 3600
        active_syms = {sym: p for sym, p in paper.positions.items()}
        for o in paper.orders:
            if o["symbol"] not in active_syms:
                active_syms[o["symbol"]] = o

        closed_trades = [t for t in paper.realized if not t.get("partial") and t.get("time", 0) >= cutoff]

        lines = [
            "📈 <b>Графики торгов и Аудит (48ч)</b>",
            f"🌐 <a href='{public_url}/audit?hours=48'><b>Открыть интерактивный дашборд инфографики (48ч)</b></a>\n"
        ]

        if not active_syms and not closed_trades:
            lines.append("<i>За последние 48 часов активности не было.</i>")
            return "\n".join(lines)

        if active_syms:
            curr_reg = bot_state.current_regime.upper()
            curr_icon = "🟢" if curr_reg == "BULL" else "🔴" if curr_reg == "BEAR" else "🟡"
            lines.append(f"📦 <b>В работе ({curr_icon} {curr_reg}):</b>")
            links = []
            for sym, data in sorted(active_syms.items()):
                m_icon = mode_emoji(data)
                links.append(f"{m_icon} <a href='{public_url}/chart?symbol={sym}'><b>{sym[:-4]}</b></a>{grade_badge(data)}")
            lines.append(" · ".join(links) + "\n")

        regimes_order = [
            ("bull", "🟢 <b>Бычий рынок (BULL)</b>"),
            ("neutral", "🟡 <b>Нейтральный рынок (NEUTRAL)</b>"),
            ("bear", "🔴 <b>Медвежий рынок (BEAR)</b>"),
            ("unknown", "⚪ <b>Архивные сделки</b>")
        ]

        for reg_key, reg_title in regimes_order:
            trades_in_reg = [t for t in closed_trades if t.get("regime", "unknown") == reg_key]
            if not trades_in_reg:
                continue

            lines.append(reg_title)

            be_list = [t for t in trades_in_reg if t.get("is_be") or is_neutral_be(t.get("pnl_pct", 0.0))]
            decisive_list = [t for t in trades_in_reg if t not in be_list]
            wins = [t for t in decisive_list if t["pnl"] > 0]
            losses = [t for t in decisive_list if t["pnl"] <= 0]

            if wins:
                win_links = []
                for t in sorted(wins, key=lambda x: x["pnl_pct"], reverse=True):
                    sym = t["symbol"][:-4]
                    win_links.append(f"{mode_emoji(t)} <a href='{public_url}/chart?symbol={t['symbol']}'><b>{sym}</b></a>{grade_badge(t)} ({t['pnl_pct']:+.1f}%)")
                lines.append("✅ " + " · ".join(win_links))

            if be_list:
                be_links = []
                for t in be_list:
                    sym = t["symbol"][:-4]
                    be_links.append(f"{mode_emoji(t)} <a href='{public_url}/chart?symbol={t['symbol']}'><b>{sym}</b></a> ({t['pnl_pct']:+.2f}%)")
                lines.append("🛡🔒 " + " · ".join(be_links))

            if losses:
                loss_links = []
                for t in sorted(losses, key=lambda x: x["pnl_pct"]):
                    sym = t["symbol"][:-4]
                    loss_links.append(f"{mode_emoji(t)} <a href='{public_url}/chart?symbol={t['symbol']}'><b>{sym}</b></a>{grade_badge(t)} ({t['pnl_pct']:+.1f}%)")
                lines.append("❌ " + " · ".join(loss_links))

            lines.append("")

        return "\n".join(lines).strip()

    sym = arg.upper() + ("USDT" if not arg.upper().endswith("USDT") else "")
    return (
        f"📈 <b>График торгов {sym}</b>\n\n"
        f"🌐 <a href='{public_url}/chart?symbol={sym}'>Открыть интерактивный график</a>\n"
        f"📊 <a href='{public_url}/audit?hours=48'>Открыть общий дашборд аудита (48ч)</a>"
    )


async def _build_status_text() -> str:
    prices = await market_data.get_tickers()
    eq = paper.equity(prices)

    locked_usdt = paper.effective_locked_usdt()
    available_usdt = max(0.0, paper.usdt - locked_usdt)
    free_pct = available_usdt / eq * 100 if eq else 0

    metrics_all = paper.get_metrics(prices)
    metrics_24h = paper.get_metrics(prices, hours=24)

    msg = ["📊 <b>Капитан Рост</b> · <i>тренировка</i> 🎓", ""]
    msg.append(f"💰 Доступно: <b>{usd(available_usdt)}</b> ({free_pct:.0f}%)")
    if locked_usdt > 0:
        msg.append(f"🔒 В ордерах: <b>{usd(locked_usdt)}</b>")
    msg.append(f"🏦 Накопления USDT: <b>{usd(paper.funding)}</b>")

    active_coins = {s: q for s, q in getattr(paper, "funding_coins", {}).items() if q > 0.000001}
    if active_coins:
        coins_val = sum(q * prices.get(s, {}).get("last", 0) for s, q in active_coins.items())
        msg.append(f"🐋 HODL (монеты): <b>~{usd(coins_val)}</b> ({len(active_coins)} шт · /funding)")

    msg.append(f"📈 Торговый капитал: <b>{usd(eq)}</b>")
    msg.append(f"💵 PnL (за всё время): {pnl_emoji(metrics_all['total_pnl'])} <b>{usd(metrics_all['total_pnl'])}</b>")
    msg.append("")

    if paper.positions:
        inv_pct = sum(p["qty"] * prices.get(s, {}).get("last", 0) for s, p in paper.positions.items()) / eq * 100 if eq else 0
        msg.append(f"📦 <b>Позиции ({len(paper.positions)})</b> · {inv_pct:.0f}% портфеля")
        for sym, pos in paper.positions.items():
            last = prices.get(sym, {}).get("last", 0)
            val, w = pos["qty"] * last, (pos["qty"] * last / eq * 100 if eq else 0)
            pnl_pct = (last - pos["avg"]) / pos["avg"] * 100 if pos["avg"] else 0

            tp1_status = " · 🔥 TP1" if pos.get("tp1_done") else ""
            msg.append(f"{format_coin(sym, pos)}{grade_badge(pos)} · {pnl_emoji(pnl_pct)} <b>{fmt_pct(pnl_pct)}</b>{tp1_status}")
            msg.append(f"   💼 {usd(val)} · {w:.1f}%")
            msg.append(f"   📥 {fmt_price(pos['avg'])} → 📊 {fmt_price(last)}")

            tp_pct = (pos["tp"] - pos["avg"]) / pos["avg"] * 100 if pos["avg"] else 0
            sl_pct = (pos["sl"] - pos["avg"]) / pos["avg"] * 100 if pos["avg"] else 0

            tp_icon = "TP🏃🏻‍➡️" if pos.get("tp1_done") else "🎯"
            sl_tag = sl_emoji(pos.get("sl_state", "SHIELD"))
            msg.append(f"   {tp_icon} {fmt_price(pos['tp'])} ({fmt_pct(tp_pct)}) · {sl_tag} <b>{fmt_price(pos['sl'])} ({fmt_pct(sl_pct)})</b>")
    else:
        msg.append("📦 <b>Позиции</b>: нет")
    msg.append("")

    if paper.orders:
        msg.append(f"📋 <b>Ордера ({len(paper.orders)})</b> · {usd(sum(o['qty'] * o['price'] for o in paper.orders))}")
        for o in paper.orders:
            val, w = o["qty"] * o["price"], (o["qty"] * o["price"] / eq * 100 if eq else 0)
            last_price = prices.get(o["symbol"], {}).get("last", 0)
            dist_str = f" ({(o['price'] - last_price) / last_price * 100:+.2f}%)" if last_price > 0 else ""

            msg.append(f"{format_coin(o['symbol'], o)}{grade_badge(o)} · {w:.1f}%")
            msg.append(f"   💼 {usd(val)} · 📥 {fmt_price(o['price'])}{dist_str}")
            msg.append(
                f"   🎯 {fmt_price(o['tp'])} ({fmt_pct((o['tp'] - o['price']) / o['price'] * 100 if o['price'] else 0)}) · "
                f"🛡📉 {fmt_price(o['sl'])} ({fmt_pct((o['sl'] - o['price']) / o['price'] * 100 if o['price'] else 0)})"
            )
    else:
        msg.append("📋 <b>Ордера</b>: нет")
    msg.append("")

    mode, _ = learner.risk_mode(metrics_24h["profit_factor"], metrics_24h["max_drawdown_pct"], metrics_24h["total_trades"])
    mode_emoji_map = {"NORMAL": "🟢", "CAUTIOUS": "🟡", "STRICT": "🔴", "AGGRESSIVE": "🚀"}
    m_em = mode_emoji_map.get(mode, "⚪")

    msg.append(f"📊 <b>Метрики (24ч)</b> · {m_em} {mode}")
    msg.append(
        f"🧾 {metrics_24h['total_trades']} (✅ {metrics_24h['win_count']} / ❌ {metrics_24h['loss_count']} / 🛡🔒 {metrics_24h['be_count']}) · "
        f"🔥TP1: {metrics_24h['partial_count']}"
    )

    a_stats = learner.get_grade_a_stats()
    if a_stats["total"] > 0:
        msg.append(
            f"💎 [💎] A+: WR <b>{a_stats['wr']:.0%}</b> ({a_stats['decisive']}) · "
            f"вес <b>×{a_stats['mult']:.2f}</b> (серия {a_stats['streak']:+d})"
        )

    pf = metrics_24h["profit_factor"]
    pf_text, pf_mark = ("—", "") if pf is None else ("∞", "🎯") if pf == float("inf") else (f"{pf:.2f}", "🎯" if pf >= 1.3 else ("⚠️" if pf >= 1.0 else "❌"))
    dd = metrics_24h["max_drawdown_pct"]
    dd_mark = "✅" if dd < 5 else ("⚠️" if dd < 15 else "🔴")

    if pf is None:
        msg.append(f"📈 PF: <b>—</b> · 📉 DD: <b>{dd:.1f}%</b> {dd_mark} (лимит 15%)")
    else:
        msg.append(f"📈 PF: <b>{pf_text}</b> {pf_mark} (цель ≥ 1.3) · 📉 DD: <b>{dd:.1f}%</b> {dd_mark} (лимит 15%)")

    exp = metrics_24h["expectancy"]
    rf = metrics_24h["recovery_factor"]
    exp_str = "—" if exp is None else f"{pnl_emoji(exp)} <b>{exp:+.2f}</b> {'🎯' if exp > 0 else '❌'}"
    rf_str = "—" if rf is None else "∞" if rf == float("inf") else f"{rf:.1f} {'🎯' if rf >= 2 else ('⚠️' if rf >= 1 else '❌')}"
    msg.append(f"💹 {exp_str} · 🔄 RF: <b>{rf_str}</b>")
    msg.append(f"⏱ PnL за 24 часа: {pnl_emoji(metrics_24h['total_pnl'])} <b>{usd(metrics_24h['total_pnl'])}</b>\n")

    regime = bot_state.current_regime
    thrs = SCAN_SUMMARY.get("thrs") or get_thresholds(regime)

    from bot.strategy.fundamental import get_fear_and_greed
    fng = get_fear_and_greed()
    fng_emoji = "🌋" if fng >= 75 else "🤑" if fng >= 55 else "😱" if fng <= 24 else "😨" if fng <= 45 else "😴"

    regime_str = {"bull": "🟢 BULL", "neutral": "🟡 NEUTRAL", "bear": "🔴 BEAR"}.get(regime, "⚪")
    btc_price = prices.get("BTCUSDT", {}).get("last", 0)

    msg.append(f"₿ <b>${fmt_price(btc_price)}</b> · {regime_str} · {fng_emoji} F&G: {fng}")
    msg.append(f"🎯 Пороги: 🚀 <b>{thrs['rocket']:.2f}</b> | 🏹 <b>{thrs['sniper']:.2f}</b> | 🧲 <b>{thrs['reversal']:.2f}</b>")

    if SCAN_SUMMARY.get("text"):
        msg.append(f"🔎 {SCAN_SUMMARY['text']}")
    wr, n = learner.winrate()
    top_txt = " · ".join(f"{k} {v:.2f}" for k, v in sorted(learner.weights.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1], reverse=True)[:3])
    msg.append(f"🧠 wr {wr:.0%} ({n}) · 🛡🔒 {learner.be_count} · топ: {top_txt} · строгость {learner.threshold_adj:+.2f}")

    return "\n".join(msg)


async def _dispatch_command(cmd: str, args: list):
    """Выполняет команду и возвращает (text, reply_markup_dict) для мгновенного ответа в Webhook."""
    public_url = os.getenv("RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL", "https://captain-rost-bot.onrender.com")

    if cmd == "start":
        bot_state.fresh_start()
        return "🤖 <b>Капитан Рост</b> на связи! Торговля запущена.", None

    if cmd == "help":
        return (
            "📖 <b>Мои команды</b>\n"
            "/status — метрики\n"
            "/chart — графики и веб-аудит (48ч)\n"
            "/info — инфо\n"
            "/learn — обучение\n"
            "/news — аналитика\n"
            "/pause, /resume, /exitall\n"
            "/resetstats, /resetlearn\n"
            "/log — лог"
        ), None

    if cmd in ACTIONS:
        return _confirm_payload(cmd)

    if cmd == "status":
        return await _build_status_text(), None

    if cmd == "learn":
        return await _build_learn_text(), None

    if cmd == "funding":
        return await _build_funding_text(), None

    if cmd == "news":
        return await _build_news_text(), None

    if cmd == "chart":
        return await _build_chart_text(args), None

    if cmd == "autotune":
        arg = (args or [None])[0]
        shadow.set_auto(True if arg in ("on", "вкл") else False if arg in ("off", "выкл") else not shadow.tuning["auto"])
        return shadow.stats_text() + "\n💡 переключение: /autotune on|off", None

    if cmd == "info":
        txt = info_full_text() + f"\n\n📥 <a href='{public_url}/whitepaper'><b>Скачать CaptainRost_Whitepaper.txt</b></a>"
        return txt, None

    if cmd == "log":
        return (
            f"📄 <b>Лог работы бота</b>\n\n"
            f"📥 <a href='{public_url}/download-log'><b>Скачать свежий файл лога (bot.log)</b></a>\n"
            f"📊 <a href='{public_url}/audit?hours=48'>Открыть веб-дашборд аудита (48ч)</a>"
        ), None

    return None, None


async def webhook_handler(request):
    """
    Обрабатывает входящие команды и кнопки от Telegram и возвращает ответ
    ПРЯМО В ТЕЛЕ HTTP-ОТВЕТА ВЕБХУКА (мгновенно и без исходящих запросов к api.telegram.org!).
    """
    try:
        data = await request.json()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"webhook json error: {e}")
        return web.Response(text="OK")

    try:
        if "callback_query" in data:
            cb = data["callback_query"]
            msg = cb.get("message") or {}
            chat_id = msg.get("chat", {}).get("id")
            message_id = msg.get("message_id")
            cb_data = cb.get("data", "")

            if not _is_authorized(chat_id):
                return web.Response(text="OK")

            if cb_data == "cancel":
                return web.json_response({
                    "method": "editMessageText",
                    "chat_id": chat_id,
                    "message_id": message_id,
                    "text": "❌ Отменено.",
                    "reply_markup": {"inline_keyboard": []},
                })

            if cb_data.startswith("confirm:"):
                key = cb_data.split(":", 1)[1]
                entry = ACTIONS.get(key)
                if entry:
                    fn, _ = entry
                    result = await fn(None)
                    return web.json_response({
                        "method": "editMessageText",
                        "chat_id": chat_id,
                        "message_id": message_id,
                        "text": f"✅ <b>Подтверждено</b>\n\n{result}",
                        "parse_mode": "HTML",
                        "disable_web_page_preview": True,
                        "reply_markup": {"inline_keyboard": []},
                    })

            return web.Response(text="OK")

        msg = data.get("message") or data.get("edited_message")
        if msg:
            chat_id = msg.get("chat", {}).get("id")
            text = (msg.get("text") or "").strip()

            if not _is_authorized(chat_id):
                return web.Response(text="OK")

            if text.startswith("/"):
                parts = text.split()
                cmd = parts[0][1:].split("@")[0].lower()
                args = parts[1:]

                reply_text, markup_dict = await _dispatch_command(cmd, args)
                if reply_text:
                    payload = {
                        "method": "sendMessage",
                        "chat_id": chat_id,
                        "text": reply_text,
                        "parse_mode": "HTML",
                        "disable_web_page_preview": True,
                    }
                    if markup_dict:
                        payload["reply_markup"] = markup_dict
                    return web.json_response(payload)

    except Exception as e:
        logger.exception(f"webhook_handler dispatch error: {e}")

    return web.Response(text="OK")


async def _init_telegram_forever(application):
    """Фоновая синхронизация меню команд и вебхука (НЕ удаляет вебхук и не блокирует бота)."""
    public_url = os.getenv("RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL", "https://captain-rost-bot.onrender.com")
    webhook_url = f"{public_url}{WEBHOOK_PATH}"
    attempt = 0

    while True:
        attempt += 1
        try:
            if not getattr(application, "_initialized", False):
                await application.initialize()
            if not getattr(application, "running", False):
                await application.start()

            await application.bot.set_webhook(
                url=webhook_url,
                drop_pending_updates=False,
                allowed_updates=["message", "edited_message", "callback_query"],
            )

            await application.bot.set_my_commands([
                BotCommand("start", "🚀 Запустить торговлю"),
                BotCommand("pause", "⏸ Пауза"),
                BotCommand("resume", "▶️ Возобновить"),
                BotCommand("status", "📊 Статус"),
                BotCommand("funding", "🏦 Накопительный фонд"),
                BotCommand("chart", "📈 Графики и Аудит 48ч"),
                BotCommand("learn", "🧠 Обучение"),
                BotCommand("news", "📰 Новости"),
                BotCommand("exitall", "🛑 Продать всё"),
                BotCommand("resetstats", "📊 Сброс статы"),
                BotCommand("resetlearn", "🧠♻️ Сброс ИИ"),
                BotCommand("log", "📄 Лог"),
                BotCommand("autotune", "🎛 Автотюн"),
                BotCommand("info", "📖 Инфо"),
                BotCommand("help", "📖 Справка"),
            ])
            logger.info("✅ Telegram Webhook и меню команд синхронизированы")
            return
        except (TimedOut, NetworkError, Exception) as e:
            wait_sec = min(120.0, 15.0 * attempt)
            logger.debug(f"Фоновая синхронизация Telegram API отложена на {wait_sec:.0f}с ({type(e).__name__})")
            await asyncio.sleep(wait_sec)


async def run_all(application):
    global _app
    _app = application

    web_app = web.Application()
    web_app.router.add_get("/", health_handler)
    web_app.router.add_get("/chart", chart_handler)
    web_app.router.add_get("/audit", audit_handler)
    web_app.router.add_get("/fix-telegram", fix_telegram_handler)
    web_app.router.add_get("/download-log", log_download_handler)
    web_app.router.add_get("/whitepaper", whitepaper_download_handler)
    web_app.router.add_post(WEBHOOK_PATH, webhook_handler)

    runner = web.AppRunner(web_app)
    await runner.setup()
    port = int(os.getenv("PORT", 10000))
    site = web.TCPSite(runner, "0.0.0.0", port)
    await site.start()
    logger.info(f"🌐 HTTP-сервер и прямой Webhook-диспетчер запущены на порту {port}")

    asyncio.create_task(_init_telegram_forever(application))
    asyncio.create_task(asyncio.to_thread(ensure_branch))

    from bot.core.event_bus import EventBus
    from bot.workers.market_data import MarketDataWorker
    from bot.workers.execution import ExecutionRiskWorker
    from bot.workers.scanner import ScannerWorker
    from bot.workers.order_manager import OrderManagerWorker
    from bot.workers.notification import NotificationWorker
    from bot.workers.position_manager import PositionManagerWorker
    from bot.workers.data_feeder import DataFeederWorker

    global_bus = EventBus()

    workers = [
        asyncio.create_task(MarketDataWorker(global_bus).run(), name="MarketData"),
        asyncio.create_task(ExecutionRiskWorker(global_bus).run(), name="Execution"),
        asyncio.create_task(ScannerWorker(global_bus).run(), name="Scanner"),
        asyncio.create_task(OrderManagerWorker(global_bus).run(), name="OrderManager"),
        asyncio.create_task(PositionManagerWorker(global_bus).run(), name="PositionManager"),
        asyncio.create_task(NotificationWorker(global_bus, send_chat_func=send_chat).run(), name="Notification"),
        asyncio.create_task(DataFeederWorker(global_bus).run(), name="DataFeeder"),
    ]

    def worker_callback(t: asyncio.Task):
        try:
            t.result()
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            pass
        except Exception as e:
            logger.exception(f"Воркер {t.get_name()} упал: {e}")

    for task in workers:
        task.add_done_callback(worker_callback)

    asyncio.create_task(report_loop())

    logger.info("⚡ EDA Core успешно запущено. (DIRECT WEBHOOK REPLY MODE)")

    try:
        while True:
            await asyncio.sleep(3600)
    except asyncio.CancelledError:
        pass
    finally:
        # Никогда не вызываем delete_webhook при остановке контейнера!
        try:
            if getattr(application, "_initialized", False):
                await application.stop()
                await application.shutdown()
        except Exception:
            pass
        await runner.cleanup()


def main():
    os.makedirs("logs", exist_ok=True)
    logger.add("logs/bot.log", rotation="5 MB", retention="7 days", enqueue=True, level="INFO")

    token = os.getenv("TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN")
    app = (
        Application.builder()
        .token(token)
        .connect_timeout(15.0)
        .read_timeout(15.0)
        .write_timeout(15.0)
        .pool_timeout(15.0)
        .build()
    )

    asyncio.run(run_all(app))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

import os
import time
import asyncio
import httpx
from loguru import logger

_cache = {
    "stablecoins": {"trend": "neutral", "ts": 0},
    "hot_sectors": {"data": [], "ts": 0},
    "fear_and_greed": {"value": 50, "ts": 0}
}

CACHE_TTL = 3600


async def _fetch_stablecoin_flows():
    """Анализирует приток/отток стейблкоинов (DefiLlama)."""
    try:
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=15) as c:
            r = await c.get("https://stablecoins.llama.fi/stablecoincharts/all")
        data = r.json()
        if len(data) >= 8:
            today = data[-1]["totalCirculatingUSD"]["peggedUSD"]
            week_ago = data[-8]["totalCirculatingUSD"]["peggedUSD"]
            change_pct = (today - week_ago) / week_ago * 100
            
            # Реальный макро-тренд фиксируем только при значимом сдвиге ликвидности (> 1.0% / < -1.5%)
            if change_pct >= 1.0:
                _cache["stablecoins"]["trend"] = "bull"
            elif change_pct <= -1.5:
                _cache["stablecoins"]["trend"] = "bear"
            else:
                _cache["stablecoins"]["trend"] = "neutral"
            
            logger.info(f"DefiLlama | Stablecoin Flow: {change_pct:+.2f}% -> {_cache['stablecoins']['trend']}")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug(f"DefiLlama API error: {e}")


async def _fetch_defillama_sectors():
    """Смотрит, куда перетекает TVL по чейнам/секторам."""
    try:
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=15) as c:
            r = await c.get("https://api.llama.fi/v2/chains")
        data = r.json()
        hot = []
        for chain in data:
            if chain.get("tvl", 0) > 100_000_000:
                chg = chain.get("tvlChange_7d", 0) or 0
                if chg > 10.0:  
                    name = chain["name"].upper()
                    if name in ("ETHEREUM", "SOLANA", "AVALANCHE", "SUI", "APTOS", "NEAR"):
                        hot.append("L1")
                    elif name in ("ARBITRUM", "OPTIMISM", "BASE", "POLYGON", "STARKNET"):
                        hot.append("L2")
        
        _cache["hot_sectors"]["data"] = list(set(hot))
        if hot:
            logger.info(f"DefiLlama | Hot Sectors (TVL Inflow): {', '.join(set(hot))}")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug(f"DefiLlama Chains API error: {e}")


async def _fetch_fear_and_greed():
    """Запрашивает индекс Страха и Жадности."""
    try:
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=10) as c:
            r = await c.get("https://api.alternative.me/fng/?limit=1")
        data = r.json()
        val = int(data["data"][0]["value"])
        _cache["fear_and_greed"]["value"] = val
        logger.info(f"Alternative.me | Fear & Greed Index: {val}")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug(f"Fear & Greed API error: {e}")


async def fetch_macro_data():
    await asyncio.gather(
        _fetch_stablecoin_flows(),
        _fetch_defillama_sectors(),
        _fetch_fear_and_greed()
    )

def get_macro_trend():
    return _cache["stablecoins"]["trend"]

def is_sector_hot(sector):
    return sector in _cache["hot_sectors"]["data"]

def get_fear_and_greed():
    return _cache["fear_and_greed"]["value"]


async def check_coinglass_liquidation_threat(symbol):
    api_key = os.getenv("COINGLASS_API_KEY")
    if not api_key:
        return False
        
    base = symbol[:-4] if symbol.endswith("USDT") else symbol
    try:
        headers = {"coinglassSecret": api_key}
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=10) as c:
            r = await c.get("https://open-api.coinglass.com/public/v2/indicator/long_short_ratio", 
                            params={"symbol": base, "time_type": "h1"}, headers=headers)
        data = r.json().get("data", [])
        if data and data[-1].get("longShortRatio", 1.0) > 3.0:
            return True 
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug(f"Coinglass API error: {e}")
    return False

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/strategy/indicators.py ---
def ema(values, period):
    if not values:
        return []
    k = 2 / (period + 1)
    result = [values[0]]
    for v in values[1:]:
        result.append(v * k + result[-1] * (1 - k))
    return result


def rsi(closes, period=14):
    if len(closes) < period + 1:
        return 50.0
    gains, losses = [], []
    for i in range(1, len(closes)):
        d = closes[i] - closes[i - 1]
        gains.append(max(d, 0))
        losses.append(max(-d, 0))
    avg_gain = sum(gains[:period]) / period
    avg_loss = sum(losses[:period]) / period
    for i in range(period, len(gains)):
        avg_gain = (avg_gain * (period - 1) + gains[i]) / period
        avg_loss = (avg_loss * (period - 1) + losses[i]) / period
    if avg_loss == 0:
        return 100.0
    rs = avg_gain / avg_loss
    return 100 - 100 / (1 + rs)


def atr(candles, period=14):
    if len(candles) < 2:
        return 0.0
    trs = []
    for i in range(1, len(candles)):
        h, l, pc = candles[i]["high"], candles[i]["low"], candles[i - 1]["close"]
        trs.append(max(h - l, abs(h - pc), abs(l - pc)))
    a = sum(trs[:period]) / min(period, len(trs))
    for tr in trs[period:]:
        a = (a * (period - 1) + tr) / period
    return a


def _dedup_levels(levels):
    if not levels:
        return []
    deduped = []
    for lvl in levels:
        match = next((d for d in deduped if abs(d[0] - lvl[0]) / lvl[0] <= 0.0055), None)
        if match:
            if lvl[1] > match[1]:
                deduped.remove(match)
                deduped.append(lvl)
        else:
            deduped.append(lvl)
    return deduped


def _count_spaced_touches(price_target: float, points: list, tol: float = 0.0055, is_res: bool = False) -> int:
    if price_target <= 0:
        return 0
    matched = [pt for pt in points if abs(pt[0] - price_target) / price_target <= tol]
    if not matched:
        return 0

    touches_weight = 0
    last_idx_by_tf = {}
    h1_touches_count = 0

    for item in sorted(matched, key=lambda x: (x[1], x[2])):
        p_val, tf_tag, idx, min_gap = item[0], item[1], item[2], item[3]
        w = item[4] if len(item) > 4 else 1
        prev_idx = last_idx_by_tf.get(tf_tag)
        if prev_idx is None or (idx - prev_idx) >= min_gap:
            touches_weight += w
            if tf_tag == "1h":
                h1_touches_count += 1
            last_idx_by_tf[tf_tag] = idx

    if h1_touches_count > 0 and "15m" in last_idx_by_tf:
        touches_weight = max(2, touches_weight - 1)

    if is_res:
        if touches_weight >= 6 and h1_touches_count < 3:
            touches_weight = 5
    else:
        if touches_weight >= 3 and h1_touches_count >= 1:
            touches_weight = max(touches_weight, 3)

    return touches_weight


def get_all_sr_levels(candles_15m, candles_1h=None, window_15m=120, window_1h=72):
    """
    Строит 48-часовую карту уровней Поддержки и Сопротивления (1H + 15m) с учётом:
    1. Зеркальных уровней (S/R Flip): пробитые вверх часовые и 15m максимумы становятся ступенями Поддержки под ценой.
    2. Часовых зон спроса (длинных нижних теней откупа на 1H).
    """
    if not candles_15m or len(candles_15m) < 15:
        return [], []

    last_price = float(candles_15m[-1]["close"])
    high_points = []
    low_points = []

    if candles_1h and isinstance(candles_1h, list) and len(candles_1h) >= 20:
        recent_1h = candles_1h[-window_1h:] if len(candles_1h) >= window_1h else candles_1h
        n1 = len(recent_1h)
        for i in range(2, n1 - 1):
            h = float(recent_1h[i]["high"])
            l = float(recent_1h[i]["low"])
            o = float(recent_1h[i]["open"])
            c = float(recent_1h[i]["close"])
            rng = h - l

            is_1h_high = (h >= recent_1h[i - 1]["high"] and h >= recent_1h[i - 2]["high"] and h >= recent_1h[i + 1]["high"])
            is_1h_low = (l <= recent_1h[i - 1]["low"] and l <= recent_1h[i - 2]["low"] and l <= recent_1h[i + 1]["low"])

            if is_1h_high:
                high_points.append((h, "1h", i, 3, 2))
            if is_1h_low:
                low_points.append((l, "1h", i, 3, 2))
            elif rng > 0 and (min(o, c) - l) / rng >= 0.45 and l <= min(recent_1h[i - 1]["low"], recent_1h[i + 1]["low"]):
                # Часовой выкуп тенью (зона спроса 1H)
                low_points.append((l, "1h", i, 3, 2))

    recent_15m = candles_15m[-window_15m:] if len(candles_15m) >= window_15m else candles_15m
    n15 = len(recent_15m)
    for i in range(3, n15 - 1):
        h = float(recent_15m[i]["high"])
        l = float(recent_15m[i]["low"])
        if all(h >= recent_15m[i - j]["high"] for j in (1, 2, 3)) and h >= recent_15m[i + 1]["high"]:
            high_points.append((h, "15m", i, 4, 1))
        if all(l <= recent_15m[i - j]["low"] for j in (1, 2, 3)) and l <= recent_15m[i + 1]["low"]:
            low_points.append((l, "15m", i, 4, 1))

    # Объединяем пики и впадины для расчёта зеркальных уровней (S/R Flip)
    all_pivots = high_points + low_points
    unique_prices = {pt[0] for pt in all_pivots if pt[0] > 0}

    recent_closes = [c["close"] for c in recent_15m[-36:]] if len(recent_15m) >= 36 else [c["close"] for c in recent_15m]
    max_recent_close = max(recent_closes) if recent_closes else 0.0

    clusters_res = []
    clusters_sup = []

    for p_lvl in unique_prices:
        if p_lvl > last_price * 1.0005:
            # Уровень ВЫШЕ текущей цены -> Сопротивление
            touches = _count_spaced_touches(p_lvl, high_points, tol=0.0055, is_res=True)
            if touches < 2:
                touches = _count_spaced_touches(p_lvl, all_pivots, tol=0.0050, is_res=True)
            if touches >= 6 and max_recent_close > p_lvl * 1.0025:
                touches = 4
            if touches >= 2:
                clusters_res.append((p_lvl, touches))
        elif p_lvl < last_price * 0.9995:
            # Уровень НИЖЕ текущей цены -> Поддержка (прямые впадины + зеркальные пробитые уровни)
            touches_low = _count_spaced_touches(p_lvl, low_points, tol=0.0055, is_res=False)
            touches_flip = _count_spaced_touches(p_lvl, all_pivots, tol=0.0050, is_res=False)
            touches = max(touches_low, touches_flip)
            if touches >= 2:
                clusters_sup.append((p_lvl, touches))

    clusters_res.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
    clusters_sup.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)

    return _dedup_levels(clusters_res), _dedup_levels(clusters_sup)


def find_sr_levels(candles_15m, candles_1h=None, window_15m=120, window_1h=72, window=None):
    if window is not None:
        window_15m = window

    if not candles_15m or len(candles_15m) < 15:
        return None, 0, None, 0

    last_price = candles_15m[-1]["close"]
    confirmed_res, confirmed_sup = get_all_sr_levels(candles_15m, candles_1h, window_15m, window_1h)

    res_above = [c for c in confirmed_res if c[0] > last_price * 1.0005]
    nearest_res = None
    res_touches = 0
    if res_above:
        res_above.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
        nearest_res, res_touches = res_above[0]

    sup_below = [c for c in confirmed_sup if c[0] < last_price * 0.9995]
    nearest_sup = None
    sup_touches = 0
    if sup_below:
        sup_below.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)
        nearest_sup, sup_touches = sup_below[0]

    return nearest_res, res_touches, nearest_sup, sup_touches

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/strategy/indicators.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/strategy/learner.py ---
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path

from loguru import logger

from bot.core.remote_state import download_state, upload_state
from bot.core.state import bot_state

STATE_FILE = Path("storage/learner.json")
REMOTE_PATH = "learner.json"

KEYS = ["ema50", "ema21", "impulse", "rsi", "volume", "chg24h", "indep", "news_pos", "hype"]

# Защита каркасных трендовых весов от деградации и перекоса в сторону indep
WEIGHT_FLOORS = {
    "ema50": 0.88,
    "ema21": 0.85,
    "impulse": 0.85,
    "volume": 0.78,
    "rsi": 0.75,
    "chg24h": 0.75,
    "indep": 0.70,
    "news_pos": 0.75,
    "hype": 0.75,
}

WEIGHT_CAPS = {
    "ema50": 1.35,
    "ema21": 1.35,
    "impulse": 1.35,
    "volume": 1.30,
    "rsi": 1.25,
    "chg24h": 1.25,
    "indep": 1.15,
    "news_pos": 1.20,
    "hype": 1.20,
}

PATTERN_NAMES = {
    "pinbar": "🔨 Пин-бар выкупа (Spring)",
    "divergence": "🧲 Дивергенция RSI",
    "vcp": "📦 Сжатие волатильности (VCP)",
    "breakout": "🚀 Импульсный пробой",
    "engulfing": "⚡ Бычье поглощение",
}

TIERS = ["TOP20", "MID", "SMALL", "MICRO"]
MODE_LADDER = ["STRICT", "CAUTIOUS", "NORMAL", "AGGRESSIVE"]

BE_LOW_PCT = -0.25
BE_HIGH_PCT = 0.25


def is_neutral_be(pnl_pct: float) -> bool:
    return BE_LOW_PCT <= float(pnl_pct) <= BE_HIGH_PCT


def clamp_weight(key: str, val: float) -> float:
    lo = WEIGHT_FLOORS.get(key, 0.75)
    hi = WEIGHT_CAPS.get(key, 1.30)
    return round(min(hi, max(lo, float(val))), 3)


class Learner:
    """Самообучение: защищённый каркас весов тренда + персональный рейтинг доверия к монетам (Coin Trust)."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.weights = {k: 1.0 for k in KEYS}
        self.results = []
        self.be_count = 0
        self.threshold_adj = 0.0
        self.current_risk_mode = "NORMAL"
        self._last_mode_change_ts = 0.0
        self.sector_stats = {}
        self.tier_stats = {}
        self.coin_stats = {}
        self.exit_stats = {}
        self.entry_stats = {}
        self.pattern_stats = {}
        self.grade_stats = {"A+": [], "B": []}
        self.grade_a_streak = 0
        self._last_upload = 0.0
        self._load()

    def _load(self):
        data = None
        try:
            if STATE_FILE.exists():
                data = json.loads(STATE_FILE.read_text())
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"learner load error: {e}")
        if data is None:
            data = download_state(REMOTE_PATH)
        if data:
            loaded_weights = data.get("weights", {})
            for k in KEYS:
                w_val = float(loaded_weights.get(k, 1.0))
                self.weights[k] = clamp_weight(k, w_val)

            self.results = data.get("results", [])
            self.be_count = int(data.get("be_count", 0))
            self.threshold_adj = float(data.get("threshold_adj", 0))
            self.current_risk_mode = data.get("current_risk_mode", "NORMAL")
            self._last_mode_change_ts = float(data.get("last_mode_change_ts", 0.0))
            self.sector_stats = data.get("sector_stats", {})
            self.tier_stats = data.get("tier_stats", {})
            self.coin_stats = data.get("coin_stats", {})
            self.exit_stats = data.get("exit_stats", {})
            if "🛡🔒" in self.exit_stats:
                old_be = self.exit_stats.pop("🛡🔒")
                for p in old_be:
                    target_k = "🛡💼" if p > BE_HIGH_PCT else "🛡🔒 БУ"
                    self.exit_stats.setdefault(target_k, []).append(p)
            self.entry_stats = data.get("entry_stats", {})
            self.pattern_stats = data.get("pattern_stats", {})
            self.grade_stats = data.get("grade_stats", {"A+": [], "B": []})
            self.grade_a_streak = int(data.get("grade_a_streak", 0))
            logger.info("learner: опыт загружен, веса тренда защищены (Structural Anchor)")

    def save(self):
        payload = {
            "weights": self.weights,
            "results": self.results[-200:],
            "be_count": self.be_count,
            "threshold_adj": self.threshold_adj,
            "current_risk_mode": self.current_risk_mode,
            "last_mode_change_ts": self._last_mode_change_ts,
            "sector_stats": {k: v[-50:] for k, v in self.sector_stats.items()},
            "tier_stats": {k: v[-50:] for k, v in self.tier_stats.items()},
            "coin_stats": {k: v[-30:] for k, v in self.coin_stats.items()},
            "exit_stats": {k: v[-50:] for k, v in self.exit_stats.items()},
            "entry_stats": {k: v[-50:] for k, v in self.entry_stats.items()},
            "pattern_stats": {k: v[-50:] for k, v in self.pattern_stats.items()},
            "grade_stats": {k: v[-100:] for k, v in self.grade_stats.items()},
            "grade_a_streak": self.grade_a_streak,
        }
        try:
            STATE_FILE.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            STATE_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False))
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"learner save error: {e}")
        if time.time() - self._last_upload > 3600:
            self._last_upload = time.time()
            upload_state(REMOTE_PATH, payload)

    def weight(self, key):
        return clamp_weight(key, self.weights.get(key, 1.0))

    def grade_a_trust_mult(self) -> float:
        hist = [p for p in self.grade_stats.get("A+", []) if not is_neutral_be(p)][-25:]
        if len(hist) >= 6:
            wr = sum(1 for p in hist if p > 0) / len(hist)
            if wr < 0.38:
                return 0.80
            if wr < 0.48:
                return 0.95
        if self.grade_a_streak <= -3:
            return 0.75
        if self.grade_a_streak == -2:
            return 0.85
        if self.grade_a_streak >= 3:
            return 1.15
        if self.grade_a_streak == 2:
            return 1.08
        return 1.0

    def get_grade_a_stats(self) -> dict:
        hist = self.grade_stats.get("A+", [])
        decisive = [p for p in hist if not is_neutral_be(p)]
        be_cnt = len(hist) - len(decisive)
        wins = [p for p in decisive if p > 0]
        wr = (len(wins) / len(decisive)) if decisive else 0.0
        avg = (sum(decisive) / len(decisive)) if decisive else 0.0
        return {
            "total": len(hist),
            "decisive": len(decisive),
            "be": be_cnt,
            "wr": wr,
            "avg": avg,
            "streak": self.grade_a_streak,
            "mult": self.grade_a_trust_mult(),
        }

    def record(self, keys, win, pnl_pct=0.0, sector=None, tier=None,
               exit_type=None, runner_bonus=0.0, soft=False,
               entry_mode=None, patterns=None, grade="B", symbol=None):
        g_key = "A+" if grade == "A+" else "B"
        gh = self.grade_stats.setdefault(g_key, [])
        gh.append(round(pnl_pct, 2))
        self.grade_stats[g_key] = gh[-100:]

        if symbol:
            ch = self.coin_stats.setdefault(symbol, [])
            ch.append(round(pnl_pct, 2))
            self.coin_stats[symbol] = ch[-30:]

        if is_neutral_be(pnl_pct):
            self.be_count += 1
            eh = self.exit_stats.setdefault("🛡🔒 БУ", [])
            eh.append(round(pnl_pct, 2))
            self.exit_stats["🛡🔒 БУ"] = eh[-50:]
            self.save()
            logger.info(f"learner: нейтральный выход 🛡🔒 БУ ({pnl_pct:+.2f}%) — веса и Winrate не затронуты")
            return

        real_win = pnl_pct > BE_HIGH_PCT
        self.results.append(1 if real_win else 0)
        self.results = self.results[-200:]

        if g_key == "A+":
            if real_win:
                self.grade_a_streak = max(1, self.grade_a_streak + 1)
            else:
                self.grade_a_streak = min(-1, self.grade_a_streak - 1)

        if sector:
            hist = self.sector_stats.setdefault(sector, [])
            hist.append(round(pnl_pct, 2))
            self.sector_stats[sector] = hist[-50:]
        if tier:
            hist = self.tier_stats.setdefault(tier, [])
            hist.append(round(pnl_pct, 2))
            self.tier_stats[tier] = hist[-50:]
        if exit_type:
            norm_exit = "🛡💼" if (exit_type == "🛡🔒" and real_win) else ("🛡📉" if (exit_type == "🛡🔒" and not real_win) else exit_type)
            eh = self.exit_stats.setdefault(norm_exit, [])
            eh.append(round(pnl_pct, 2))
            self.exit_stats[norm_exit] = eh[-50:]
        if entry_mode:
            em_h = self.entry_stats.setdefault(entry_mode, [])
            em_h.append(round(pnl_pct, 2))
            self.entry_stats[entry_mode] = em_h[-50:]

        if patterns:
            for p in patterns:
                ph = self.pattern_stats.setdefault(p, [])
                ph.append(round(pnl_pct, 2))
                self.pattern_stats[p] = ph[-50:]

        abs_pnl = abs(pnl_pct)
        if abs_pnl >= 3.0:
            delta = 0.045 if real_win else -0.040
        elif abs_pnl >= 1.0:
            delta = 0.025 if real_win else -0.025
        else:
            delta = 0.015 if real_win else -0.015
        if runner_bonus > 5.0:
            delta += 0.025
        if soft and not real_win:
            delta *= 0.5

        for k in KEYS:
            w_cur = self.weights.get(k, 1.0)
            if k in keys:
                # Базовые индикаторы тренда штрафуются в 2 раза мягче при стопе, чтобы не ослеплять сканер
                eff_delta = delta * 0.5 if (not real_win and k in ("ema50", "ema21", "impulse")) else delta
                w_cur += eff_delta
            w_reverted = w_cur * 0.96 + 1.0 * 0.04
            self.weights[k] = clamp_weight(k, w_reverted)

        self.save()
        logger.info(f"learner: win={real_win} pnl={pnl_pct:+.2f}% grade={g_key} delta={delta:+.3f} keys={keys}")

    def coin_bias(self, symbol: str) -> float:
        """
        Персональный рейтинг доверия к монете (от -1.50 до +0.60 баллов к скору):
        - 2 стопа подряд -> штраф -1.50 (защита от повторных сливов на ENA, TRUMP, PYTH).
        - Стабильные профиты -> бонус до +0.60 (приоритет для AVAX, NXPC, BSB, ARX).
        """
        raw_hist = self.coin_stats.get(symbol) or []
        decisive = [p for p in raw_hist if not is_neutral_be(p)]
        if not decisive:
            return 0.0

        if len(decisive) >= 2 and decisive[-1] < 0 and decisive[-2] < 0:
            return -1.50
        if len(decisive) == 1:
            return 0.20 if decisive[0] > 0.5 else (-0.45 if decisive[0] < -0.5 else 0.0)

        recent = decisive[-6:]
        wr = sum(1 for p in recent if p > 0) / len(recent)
        avg = sum(recent) / len(recent)
        bias = (wr - 0.5) * 0.9 + max(-0.6, min(0.4, avg * 0.22))
        if recent[-1] < 0:
            bias -= 0.25
        return round(max(-1.50, min(0.60, bias)), 2)

    def coin_trust_mult(self, symbol: str) -> float:
        """Множитель размера позиции по доверию к конкретной монете (0.65x .. 1.15x)."""
        if not symbol:
            return 1.0
        b = self.coin_bias(symbol)
        if b <= -1.0:
            return 0.65
        if b < -0.3:
            return 0.80
        if b >= 0.35:
            return 1.15
        if b >= 0.15:
            return 1.08
        return 1.0

    def pattern_multiplier(self, pattern_key: str) -> float:
        hist = [p for p in (self.pattern_stats.get(pattern_key) or []) if not is_neutral_be(p)]
        if len(hist) < 3:
            return 1.0
        wr = sum(1 for p in hist if p > 0) / len(hist)
        avg = sum(hist) / len(hist)
        mult = 1.0 + (wr - 0.5) * 0.5 + min(0.2, max(-0.2, avg * 0.05))
        return round(max(0.75, min(1.30, mult)), 2)

    def sector_bias(self, sector):
        hist = [p for p in (self.sector_stats.get(sector) or []) if not is_neutral_be(p)]
        if len(hist) < 3:
            return 0.0
        wr = sum(1 for p in hist if p > 0) / len(hist)
        avg = sum(hist) / len(hist)
        bias = (wr - 0.5) + max(-0.4, min(0.4, avg * 0.20))
        return round(max(-0.65, min(0.65, bias)), 2)

    def tier_bias(self, tier):
        hist = [p for p in (self.tier_stats.get(tier) or []) if not is_neutral_be(p)]
        if len(hist) < 5:
            return 0.0
        wr = sum(1 for p in hist if p > 0) / len(hist)
        avg = sum(hist) / len(hist)
        bias = (wr - 0.5) + max(-0.25, min(0.25, avg * 0.25))
        return round(max(-0.45, min(0.45, bias)), 2)

    def reset(self):
        self.weights = {k: 1.0 for k in KEYS}
        self.results = []
        self.be_count = 0
        self.threshold_adj = 0.0
        self.current_risk_mode = "NORMAL"
        self._last_mode_change_ts = 0.0
        self.sector_stats = {}
        self.tier_stats = {}
        self.coin_stats = {}
        self.exit_stats = {}
        self.entry_stats = {}
        self.pattern_stats = {}
        self.grade_stats = {"A+": [], "B": []}
        self.grade_a_streak = 0
        self.save()
        logger.info("learner: опыт сброшен")

    def reset_stats(self):
        self.results = []
        self.be_count = 0
        self.threshold_adj = 0.0
        self.current_risk_mode = "NORMAL"
        self._last_mode_change_ts = 0.0
        self.sector_stats = {}
        self.tier_stats = {}
        self.coin_stats = {}
        self.exit_stats = {}
        self.entry_stats = {}
        self.pattern_stats = {}
        self.grade_stats = {"A+": [], "B": []}
        self.grade_a_streak = 0
        self.save()
        logger.info("learner: статистика сброшена")

    def winrate(self):
        last = self.results[-20:]
        return (sum(last) / len(last), len(last)) if last else (0.0, 0)

    def _raw_target_mode(self, profit_factor, max_dd_pct, total_trades=0) -> tuple:
        adj = 0.0
        enough = total_trades >= 4
        if enough and profit_factor is not None:
            if profit_factor < 0.60:
                adj += 1.5
            elif profit_factor < 1.02:
                adj += 0.5
            elif profit_factor > 1.55 and (max_dd_pct or 0) < 6.0 and total_trades >= 6:
                adj -= 0.5
        if max_dd_pct and max_dd_pct > 12:
            adj += 1.0
        elif max_dd_pct and max_dd_pct > 8:
            adj += 0.5

        if adj >= 1.5:
            target = "STRICT"
        elif adj >= 0.5:
            target = "CAUTIOUS"
        elif adj <= -0.5:
            target = "AGGRESSIVE"
        else:
            target = "NORMAL"

        if getattr(bot_state, "current_regime", "neutral") == "bear" and target == "AGGRESSIVE":
            target = "NORMAL"
            adj = max(0.0, adj)

        return target, adj

    def risk_mode(self, profit_factor, max_dd_pct, total_trades=0):
        target_mode, _ = self._raw_target_mode(profit_factor, max_dd_pct, total_trades)
        cur_mode = self.current_risk_mode if self.current_risk_mode in MODE_LADDER else "NORMAL"
        cur_idx = MODE_LADDER.index(cur_mode)
        tgt_idx = MODE_LADDER.index(target_mode)

        now = time.time()
        if tgt_idx != cur_idx and (now - self._last_mode_change_ts >= 3600 or self._last_mode_change_ts == 0.0):
            step = 1 if tgt_idx > cur_idx else -1
            cur_idx = max(0, min(len(MODE_LADDER) - 1, cur_idx + step))
            cur_mode = MODE_LADDER[cur_idx]

        adj_map = {"STRICT": 1.2, "CAUTIOUS": 0.5, "NORMAL": 0.0, "AGGRESSIVE": -0.4}
        return cur_mode, adj_map.get(cur_mode, 0.0)

    def update_threshold(self, profit_factor, max_dd_pct, total_trades=0):
        old_mode = self.current_risk_mode if self.current_risk_mode in MODE_LADDER else "NORMAL"
        new_mode, dyn_adj = self.risk_mode(profit_factor, max_dd_pct, total_trades)
        if new_mode != old_mode:
            self.current_risk_mode = new_mode
            self._last_mode_change_ts = time.time()
            logger.info(f"learner: плавный переход режима риска {old_mode} -> {new_mode}")

        wr_adj = 0.0
        if total_trades >= 5:
            last = self.results[-20:]
            if last:
                wr = sum(last) / len(last)
                if wr < 0.35:
                    wr_adj = 0.20

        raw_adj = dyn_adj + wr_adj
        target_thr_adj = max(-0.20, min(0.20, raw_adj * 0.15))
        self.threshold_adj = round(0.6 * self.threshold_adj + 0.4 * target_thr_adj, 2)
        self.save()
        return self.threshold_adj

    def summary(self):
        wr, n = self.winrate()
        top = sorted(self.weights.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1], reverse=True)[:3]
        txt = ", ".join(f"{k} {v:.2f}" for k, v in top[:3])
        return f"wr {wr:.0%} ({n}) · 🛡🔒 БУ: {self.be_count} · топ: {txt} · режим: {self.current_risk_mode}"

    def kelly_multiplier(self, mode):
        hist = [p for p in (self.entry_stats.get(mode) or []) if not is_neutral_be(p)]
        if len(hist) < 10:
            return None
        wins = [p for p in hist if p > 0]
        losses = [p for p in hist if p <= 0]
        if not wins or not losses:
            return None
        p = len(wins) / len(hist)
        avg_win = sum(wins) / len(wins)
        avg_loss = abs(sum(losses) / len(losses))
        b = avg_win / avg_loss if avg_loss else 0
        if b <= 0:
            return 0.0
        kelly = (b * p - (1 - p)) / b
        return round(max(0.0, min(kelly * 0.5, 1.0)), 3)


learner = Learner()

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/strategy/patterns.py ---
from bot.strategy.indicators import rsi, ema


def detect_patterns(candles_15m: list, a15: float, e50: float, e21: float, recent_high: float) -> list:
    if not candles_15m or len(candles_15m) < 25:
        return []

    patterns = []
    curr = candles_15m[-1]
    closed = candles_15m[-2]
    prev2 = candles_15m[-3]
    closes = [c["close"] for c in candles_15m]

    vols = [c["volume"] for c in candles_15m]
    avg_vol = sum(vols[-22:-2]) / 20 if len(vols) >= 22 else (sum(vols) / max(1, len(vols)))
    vol_ratio_curr = (curr["volume"] / avg_vol) if avg_vol > 0 else 1.0
    vol_ratio_closed = (closed["volume"] / avg_vol) if avg_vol > 0 else 1.0
    r = rsi(closes)

    local_drop_pct = ((recent_high - curr["close"]) / recent_high * 100) if recent_high > curr["close"] else 0.0
    waterfall_stabilized = (
        local_drop_pct <= 9.0 or
        (closed["low"] >= prev2["low"] * 0.9985 and curr["low"] >= closed["low"] * 0.999 and closed["close"] > closed["open"])
    )

    # 1. SPRING PINBAR (строго по закрытой 15m свече + подтверждение зелёным стартом следующей свечи)
    closed_range = closed["high"] - closed["low"]
    if closed_range > 0:
        closed_body_low = min(closed["open"], closed["close"])
        closed_lwick = closed_body_low - closed["low"]
        is_closed_pin = (
            (closed_lwick / closed_range) >= 0.50 and
            closed["close"] >= closed["open"] and
            closed["close"] >= closed["low"] + 0.60 * closed_range and
            vol_ratio_closed >= 1.25 and
            curr["close"] >= closed["close"] * 0.999 and
            curr["low"] >= closed_body_low * 0.999
        )
        if is_closed_pin:
            pin_wick_pct = (closed_lwick / closed_range) * 100
            wick_entry_price = round(closed_body_low, 8)
            if (r <= 45 or curr["close"] <= e50 * 1.003) and waterfall_stabilized:
                patterns.append({
                    "key": "pinbar",
                    "name": f"Пин-бар выкупа на дне (хвост {pin_wick_pct:.0f}%, vol x{vol_ratio_closed:.1f})",
                    "mode": "reversal",
                    "base_bonus": 2.8,
                    "wick_entry_price": wick_entry_price,
                })
            elif e21 >= e50 and curr["close"] >= e21 * 0.998:
                patterns.append({
                    "key": "pinbar",
                    "name": f"Молот по тренду (хвост {pin_wick_pct:.0f}%, vol x{vol_ratio_closed:.1f})",
                    "mode": "sniper",
                    "base_bonus": 2.0,
                    "wick_entry_price": wick_entry_price,
                })

    # 2. BULLISH DIVERGENCE
    rsi_now = r
    past_closes = closes[:-6]
    rsi_past = rsi(past_closes)
    low_now = min(c["low"] for c in candles_15m[-6:])
    low_past = min(c["low"] for c in candles_15m[-18:-6])
    turning_up = (closed["close"] > closed["open"] and curr["close"] >= closed["close"])

    if waterfall_stabilized and low_now <= low_past * 1.003 and rsi_past <= 36.0 and rsi_now > (rsi_past + 4.0) and rsi_now <= 48 and turning_up:
        w_base_entry = round(min(curr["close"] * 0.998, max(low_now * 1.003, closed["open"])), 8)
        patterns.append({
            "key": "divergence",
            "name": f"Бычья дивергенция RSI ({rsi_past:.0f} -> {rsi_now:.0f})",
            "mode": "reversal",
            "base_bonus": 2.8,
            "wick_entry_price": w_base_entry,
        })

    # 3. VCP COMPRESSION (только над растущими EMA21/50 без признаков разворота вниз)
    e21_series = ema(closes, 21)
    e21_rising = len(e21_series) >= 5 and e21_series[-1] > e21_series[-4] * 1.0004

    if e50 > 0 and e21 > (e50 * 1.001) and e21_rising:
        box_candles = candles_15m[-5:-1]
        all_closed_above_e21 = all(c["close"] >= e21 * 0.999 for c in box_candles)
        curr_above_e21 = curr["close"] >= e21 and curr["close"] >= curr["open"]
        is_near_ema = 1.000 <= (curr["close"] / e50) <= 1.015
        recent_box = (max(c["high"] for c in candles_15m[-5:]) - min(c["low"] for c in candles_15m[-5:])) / curr["close"]
        lows_recent = min(c["low"] for c in candles_15m[-3:])
        lows_prev = min(c["low"] for c in candles_15m[-6:-3])
        higher_lows = lows_recent >= lows_prev * 1.000
        closed_4_vol = sum(c["volume"] for c in box_candles) / 4
        vcp_vol_ratio = (closed_4_vol / avg_vol) if avg_vol > 0 else 1.0
        is_dry_volume = vcp_vol_ratio <= 1.05

        if all_closed_above_e21 and curr_above_e21 and is_near_ema and higher_lows and recent_box <= 0.018 and is_dry_volume and closed["close"] >= closed["open"]:
            patterns.append({
                "key": "vcp",
                "name": f"Сжатие VCP над EMA21 (бокс {recent_box*100:.1f}%, vol x{vcp_vol_ratio:.1f})",
                "mode": "sniper",
                "base_bonus": 2.6,
            })

    # 4. CLEAN BREAKOUT
    if recent_high > 0 and a15 > 0 and e50 > 0:
        dist_from_ema50_atr = (curr["close"] - e50) / a15
        base_slice = candles_15m[-8:-2]
        pre_breakout_box = (
            (max(c["high"] for c in base_slice) - min(c["low"] for c in base_slice)) / curr["close"]
            if base_slice and curr["close"] > 0 else 0.0
        )
        not_exhausted = (dist_from_ema50_atr <= 2.5) and (pre_breakout_box <= 0.040) and (r <= 71)

        if not_exhausted:
            closed_green = closed["close"] > closed["open"]
            closed_body = abs(closed["close"] - closed["open"])
            closed_uwick = closed["high"] - max(closed["open"], closed["close"])
            closed_solid = (closed_body / closed_range) >= 0.58 if closed_range > 0 else False
            closed_clean = (closed_uwick / closed_range) <= 0.20 if closed_range > 0 else False

            brk_confirmed_closed = (
                closed["close"] > recent_high and closed_green and
                closed_solid and closed_clean and vol_ratio_closed >= 1.35 and
                curr["close"] >= closed["close"] * 0.999
            )

            curr_range = curr["high"] - curr["low"]
            curr_green = curr["close"] > curr["open"]
            curr_body = abs(curr["close"] - curr["open"])
            curr_uwick = curr["high"] - max(curr["open"], curr["close"])
            curr_solid = (curr_body / curr_range) >= 0.62 if curr_range > 0 else False
            curr_clean = (curr_uwick / curr_range) <= 0.18 if curr_range > 0 else False

            brk_live = (
                curr["close"] > recent_high * 1.001 and curr_green and
                curr_solid and curr_clean and vol_ratio_curr >= 1.55
            )

            if brk_confirmed_closed or brk_live:
                b_vol = vol_ratio_curr if brk_live else vol_ratio_closed
                patterns.append({
                    "key": "breakout",
                    "name": f"Истинный пробой базы ${recent_high:.4f} (vol x{b_vol:.1f})",
                    "mode": "rocket",
                    "base_bonus": 3.0,
                })

    # 5. БЫЧЬЕ ПОГЛОЩЕНИЕ НА РАЗВОРОТЕ ПРОСАДКИ (строго по закрытой 15m свече!)
    closed_engulf = (
        prev2["close"] < prev2["open"] and
        closed["close"] > closed["open"] and
        closed["close"] > prev2["high"] and
        (closed["close"] - closed["open"]) >= (prev2["open"] - prev2["close"]) * 1.10 and
        closed["volume"] >= prev2["volume"] * 1.15 and
        curr["close"] >= closed["close"] * 0.999 and
        curr["close"] >= curr["open"] * 0.9995 and
        r <= 62
    )
    if closed_engulf:
        patterns.append({
            "key": "engulfing",
            "name": "Бычье поглощение (разворот волны)",
            "mode": "sniper",
            "base_bonus": 2.2,
        })

    return patterns

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/strategy/patterns.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/strategy/risk.py ---
import time

}

TIER_TP_FLOOR = {

    "MID":   0.013,  # 1.3%
    "SMALL": 0.014,  # 1.4%
    "MICRO": 0.016,  # 1.6%

TIER_MAX_SL = {

    "MID":   2.2,
    "SMALL": 2.3,

    "SMALL": {"runner_dist": 0.015, "lock_trigger_pct": 2.05, "lock_dist": 0.007},
    "MICRO": {"runner_dist": 0.018, "lock_trigger_pct": 2.25, "lock_dist": 0.008},
}

TRADE_POLICIES = {

        "min_rr_sniper": 0.94,

        "min_rr_alt": 0.84,

def _round_px(val: float) -> float:
    v = float(val or 0.0)
    if v <= 0:
        return 0.0
    if v < 0.0001:
        return round(v, 10)
    return round(v, 8)


def get_trade_policy(risk_mode: str = "NORMAL") -> dict:
    return TRADE_POLICIES.get(risk_mode, TRADE_POLICIES["NORMAL"])


def calc_breakeven_price(avg_price: float, fee_pct: float = 0.10) -> float:
    if avg_price <= 0:
        return 0.0
    f = fee_pct / 100.0
    be = avg_price * (1.0 + f) / (1.0 - f) * 1.0005
    return _round_px(be)


def evaluate_setup_grade(cand: dict) -> str:
    pos_in_48h = cand.get("pos_in_48h", 0.5)
    corr = abs(cand.get("corr", 1.0))
    entry_mode = cand.get("entry_mode", "sniper")
    tier = cand.get("tier", "MICRO")
    sector = cand.get("sector", "Other")
    gate_1h = cand.get("gate_1h", "")
    sym = cand.get("symbol", "")

    if entry_mode == "reversal" and pos_in_48h > 0.36:
        return "B"
    if entry_mode == "sniper" and pos_in_48h > 0.62 and "breakout" not in cand.get("patterns", []):
        return "B"
    if corr > 0.85 and entry_mode != "reversal":
        return "B"
    if sector == "Other" and tier == "MICRO":
        return "B"
