    if sym and learner.coin_bias(sym) < -0.30:
        return "B"

    patterns = cand.get("patterns", [])
    strong_pats = [p for p in patterns if p in ("pinbar", "divergence", "vcp", "breakout", "engulfing")]
    sv = cand.get("signal_values", {})
    vol_ratio = sv.get("volume", 1.0)
    r_val = sv.get("rsi", 50.0)
    sup_touches = cand.get("sup_touches", 0)

    is_dip_turn = (gate_1h == "1H_DIP_BOUNCE" and sup_touches >= 2 and pos_in_48h <= 0.52)
    has_strong_slab_setup = (sup_touches >= 3 and pos_in_48h <= 0.52 and (vol_ratio >= 1.25 or r_val <= 42.0))

    if not strong_pats and not is_dip_turn and not has_strong_slab_setup:
        return "B"

    confluence = 2 if (strong_pats or is_dip_turn) else 1
    if strong_pats and sup_touches >= 3:
        confluence += 1
    if gate_1h == "1H_DIP_BOUNCE" or (gate_1h in ("1H_TREND_OK", "1H_SUPER_TREND") and pos_in_48h <= 0.58):
        confluence += 1
    elif gate_1h == "1H_SUPPORT_ONLY" and pos_in_48h <= 0.35 and sup_touches >= 3:
        confluence += 1

    if vol_ratio >= 1.30:
        confluence += 1

    if tier in ("TOP20", "MID") or corr < 0.35:
        confluence += 1

    return "A+" if confluence >= 4 else "B"


def calc_total_open_risk_pct(positions: dict, orders: list, equity: float) -> float:
    if equity <= 0:
        return 0.0
    total_risk_usdt = 0.0

    for pos in positions.values():
        avg = pos.get("avg", 0.0)
        sl = pos.get("sl", 0.0)
        qty = pos.get("qty", 0.0)
        if avg > sl > 0 and qty > 0:
            total_risk_usdt += (avg - sl) * qty

    for o in orders:
        price = o.get("price", 0.0)
        sl = o.get("sl", 0.0)
        qty = o.get("qty", 0.0)
        if price > sl > 0 and qty > 0:
            total_risk_usdt += (price - sl) * qty

    return round((total_risk_usdt / equity) * 100.0, 3)


def calc_initial_sl_tp(symbol: str, entry: float, tier: str, a: float,
                        sup: float, sup_touches: int, res: float, res_touches: int,
                        is_mom: bool = False, entry_mode: str = "sniper",
                        min_rr: float = None, recent_low_6: float = None,
                        ema50: float = None, e21_1h: float = None, e50_1h: float = None):
    tier = tier or "SMALL"
    dna = shadow.get_coin_dna(symbol)
    wave_mult = dna.get("wave_mult", 1.0)

    sl_floor_pct = TIER_SL_FLOOR.get(tier, 0.015)
    tp_floor_pct = TIER_TP_FLOOR.get(tier, 0.014)
    base_max_sl = TIER_MAX_SL.get(tier, 2.3)
    max_sl_allowed = round(base_max_sl * (1.12 if wave_mult > 1.1 else 1.0), 2)

    sl_tuning = shadow.sl_mult()
    tp_tuning = shadow.tp_mult()

    base_mult = 0.85 if is_mom else (1.22 if entry_mode == "reversal" else 1.15)
    raw_atr_sl_pct = (base_mult * a * sl_tuning / entry) * 100.0 if entry > 0 else 0.0

    if raw_atr_sl_pct > max_sl_allowed * 1.30:
        return None, None, False, f"Аномальная волатильность ATR (SL {raw_atr_sl_pct:.2f}% > {max_sl_allowed}%)"

    sl_dist = 0.0
    if sup and sup_touches >= 2 and sup < entry and (entry - sup) <= 1.8 * a:
        struct_sl = _round_px(sup * 0.997)
        candidate_dist = max(entry - struct_sl, entry * sl_floor_pct)
        if (candidate_dist / entry) * 100 <= max_sl_allowed:
            sl_dist = candidate_dist
            sl = _round_px(entry - sl_dist)

    if sl_dist <= 0:
        sl_dist_raw = max(base_mult * a * sl_tuning, entry * sl_floor_pct)
        sl_dist = min(sl_dist_raw, entry * (max_sl_allowed / 100.0))
        sl = _round_px(entry - sl_dist)

    if recent_low_6 and recent_low_6 > 0 and recent_low_6 < entry:
        wick_safe_sl = _round_px(recent_low_6 * 0.997)
        if wick_safe_sl < sl:
            wick_dist_pct = (entry - wick_safe_sl) / entry * 100
            if wick_dist_pct <= max_sl_allowed:
                sl = wick_safe_sl
                sl_dist = entry - sl

    if ema50 and ema50 > 0 and entry > ema50:
        ema50_safe_sl = _round_px(ema50 - 0.55 * a)
        if sl > ema50_safe_sl and abs(sl - ema50) <= 0.55 * a:
            ema_dist_pct = (entry - ema50_safe_sl) / entry * 100
            if ema_dist_pct <= max_sl_allowed:
                sl = ema50_safe_sl
                sl_dist = entry - sl

    if sl >= entry or (sl_dist / entry * 100) > max_sl_allowed:
        return None, None, False, "Недопустимая дистанция SL"

    if min_rr is None:
        min_rr = 0.92 if entry_mode == "sniper" else 0.84

    tp_atr_mult = 1.22 if entry_mode == "reversal" else (1.45 if is_mom else 1.28)
    rr_target = 1.02 if entry_mode == "reversal" else max(1.04, min_rr + 0.04)
    tp_dist_raw = max(tp_atr_mult * a * tp_tuning, sl_dist * rr_target, entry * tp_floor_pct)

    if not is_mom:
        max_tp1_cap = max(entry * (0.021 * wave_mult), sl_dist * max(1.00, min_rr + 0.02))
        tp_dist_raw = min(tp_dist_raw, max_tp1_cap)

    tp = _round_px(entry + tp_dist_raw)

    if entry_mode == "reversal" and e21_1h and e50_1h and e21_1h < e50_1h:
        if e21_1h > entry * 1.009:
            cap_e21 = _round_px(e21_1h * 0.9985)
            if cap_e21 < tp:
                tp = cap_e21
                min_rr = min(min_rr, (tp - entry) / sl_dist)
        elif e50_1h > entry * 1.010:
            cap_e50 = _round_px(e50_1h * 0.998)
            if cap_e50 < tp:
                tp = cap_e50
                min_rr = min(min_rr, (tp - entry) / sl_dist)

    if res and res > entry and res_touches >= 6:
        res_dist_pct = (res - entry) / entry * 100
        min_wall_dist = 0.18 if is_mom else 0.24
        if res_dist_pct < min_wall_dist:
            return None, None, False, f"Капитальная стена 1H в упор ({res_dist_pct:.2f}% < {min_wall_dist:.2f}%)"

        res_cap_tp = _round_px(res * 0.998)
        if res_cap_tp < tp:
            wall_rr = (res_cap_tp - entry) / sl_dist
            wall_gain_pct = (res_cap_tp - entry) / entry * 100
            if wall_rr >= 0.65 or wall_gain_pct >= 0.80:
                tp = res_cap_tp
                min_rr = min(min_rr, wall_rr)
            else:
                return None, None, False, f"Капитальная стена 1H слишком близко для TP1 ({wall_gain_pct:.2f}%)"

    rr = (tp - entry) / sl_dist if sl_dist > 0 else 0.0
    if (rr + 0.015) < min_rr or tp <= entry:
        return None, None, False, f"Недостаточный RR ({rr:.2f} < {min_rr:.2f})"

    return sl, tp, True, "OK"


def calc_runner_target(symbol: str, current_price: float, candles_15m: list, candles_1h: list = None, pos: dict = None) -> tuple:
    """
    Многоступенчатый Тейк Коридора (Multi-Step Wave Runner):
    - Держит планку TP🏃🏻‍➡️ на высоте импульсного шага (+0.75%..+1.35% от текущей цены).
    - Пока цена идёт ВЫШЕ 15m EMA21 (живой тренд, как на CAPUSDT перед ракетой +18%),
      НЕ прижимает TP вниз к цене, а шагает ступеньками ВВЕРХ вместе с волной!
    - Опускает ступеньку TP ниже под прокол только тогда, когда цена ушла ПОД 15m EMA21 и импульс реально затухает.
    """
    if not candles_15m or len(candles_15m) < 25:
        return _round_px(current_price * 1.010), True, "step_init"

    dna = shadow.get_coin_dna(symbol)
    wave_mult = dna.get("wave_mult", 1.0)
    wave_class = dna.get("wave_class", "mid_wave")

    closes = [c["close"] for c in candles_15m]
    e21_series = ema(closes, 21)
    e21 = e21_series[-1]
    r15 = rsi(closes)
    a15 = atr(candles_15m)

    pos = pos or {}
    old_tp = float(pos.get("tp", 0.0))
    tp1_ref = float(pos.get("tp1_price") or pos.get("initial_tp") or current_price)
    max_p = max(float(pos.get("max_price", current_price)), current_price)

    pierce_atr_mult = 1.20 if wave_class == "long_wave" else (0.78 if wave_class == "short_wave" else 0.95)
    pierce_step = max(a15 * pierce_atr_mult * wave_mult, current_price * 0.0075)
    pierce_step = min(pierce_step, current_price * 0.0160)

    # 1. Снижаем ступеньку TP под прокол ТОЛЬКО если цена провалилась ПОД 15m EMA21 и откатила от пика >= 0.65*ATR
    pullback_from_peak = max_p - current_price
    e21_falling = len(e21_series) >= 4 and e21 < e21_series[-3] * 0.9995

    if old_tp > current_price and current_price < e21 * 0.999 and pullback_from_peak >= 0.65 * a15 and e21_falling:
        lowered_tp = max(
            tp1_ref * 1.0045,
            current_price + max(0.75 * a15, current_price * 0.0065),
            e21 + 0.85 * a15
        )
        if lowered_tp < old_tp * 0.9980:
            return _round_px(lowered_tp), True, "step_down_for_pierce"

    # 2. Шаг ступеньки ВВЕРХ по волне
    ideal_pierce_tp = max(
        current_price + pierce_step,
        e21 + (1.50 * wave_mult) * a15
    )

    if old_tp <= current_price:
        return _round_px(ideal_pierce_tp), True, "step_up_pierce"

    # Если цена подошла к текущему TP (< 0.35*ATR) над растущей EMA21 — шагаем ступенькой вверх!
    if (old_tp - current_price) <= 0.35 * a15 and r15 >= 55 and current_price >= e21:
        stepped_up = max(old_tp * 1.0045, current_price + pierce_step * 0.85)
        return _round_px(stepped_up), True, "step_up_wave"

    return _round_px(old_tp), False, "step_hold"


def update_position_sl(pos: dict, max_p: float, last_price: float = None, candles_15m: list = None) -> tuple:
    """
    Частый Ступенчатый Стоп (Frequent Step-Wave SL):
    - ДО TP1: НЕ душит сделку на 20-й минуте в зоне шума (-0.35% — фикс JUP, POL, ARX)!
      Поджимает стоп только когда цена прошла >= 55% пути к TP1 (>= +0.68%) или при реальном сломе 15m EMA21 < EMA50 после 60 мин.
    - ПОСЛЕ TP1: плотными ступеньками идёт под растущей волной и зажимает стоп прямо под шпильку при каждом пробое TP🏃🏻‍➡️.
    """
    avg_p = pos.get("avg", 0.0)
    if avg_p <= 0:
        return False, pos.get("sl"), pos.get("sl_state", "SHIELD"), ""

    last_p = last_price or max_p
    sym = pos.get("symbol", "")
    dna = shadow.get_coin_dna(sym) if sym else {"wave_class": "mid_wave", "wave_mult": 1.0}
    wave_class = pos.get("wave_class") or dna.get("wave_class", "mid_wave")

    tier = pos.get("tier") or "SMALL"
    cfg = TIER_TRAIL_CONFIG.get(tier, TIER_TRAIL_CONFIG["SMALL"])

    current_sl = _round_px(pos.get("sl", 0.0))
    new_sl = current_sl
    current_state = pos.get("sl_state", "SHIELD")
    new_state = current_state

    breakeven = calc_breakeven_price(avg_p)
    peak_profit_pct = (max_p - avg_p) / avg_p * 100.0
    curr_profit_pct = (last_p - avg_p) / avg_p * 100.0
    tp_price = float(pos.get("tp", 0.0))
    init_tp = float(pos.get("initial_tp") or tp_price)
    tp_target_pct = ((init_tp - avg_p) / avg_p * 100.0) if init_tp > avg_p else 1.5

    closed_low = 0.0
    prev_closed_low = 0.0
    if candles_15m and len(candles_15m) >= 50:
        closes = [c["close"] for c in candles_15m]
        pos["_ema21"] = ema(closes, 21)[-1]
        pos["_ema50"] = ema(closes, 50)[-1]
        pos["_atr15"] = atr(candles_15m)
        pos["_rsi15"] = rsi(closes)
        closed_low = candles_15m[-2]["low"]
        prev_closed_low = min(candles_15m[-2]["low"], candles_15m[-3]["low"])

    e21 = pos.get("_ema21", 0.0)
    e50 = pos.get("_ema50", 0.0)
    a15 = pos.get("_atr15", 0.0)
    r15 = pos.get("_rsi15", 50.0)
    now_ts = int(time.time())
    pos_age = now_ts - int(pos.get("entry_time", now_ts))

    is_climax = (r15 >= 77) or (a15 > 0 and e21 > 0 and (last_p - e21) > 3.0 * a15)

    if pos.get("tp1_done"):
        tp1_ref = float(pos.get("tp1_price") or pos.get("initial_tp") or 0.0)
        tp1_pct = ((tp1_ref - avg_p) / avg_p * 100.0) if tp1_ref > avg_p else tp_target_pct
        tp1_age = now_ts - int(pos.get("tp1_time", now_ts))

        runner_floor = _round_px(max(breakeven, avg_p * 1.0035))

        if peak_profit_pct >= tp1_pct + 0.35:
            mid_lock = _round_px(avg_p + (tp1_ref - avg_p) * 0.62) if tp1_ref > avg_p else _round_px(avg_p * 1.0075)
            runner_floor = max(runner_floor, mid_lock)

        if tp1_ref > 0 and ((a15 > 0 and max_p >= tp1_ref + 0.65 * a15) or peak_profit_pct >= tp1_pct + 0.65):
            runner_floor = max(runner_floor, _round_px(tp1_ref * 1.0010))

        wave_step_sl = 0.0
        if a15 > 0 and e21 > 0 and e50 > 0:
            if is_climax:
                candle_anchor = (closed_low - 0.12 * a15) if closed_low > 0 else (last_p - 0.50 * a15)
                wave_step_sl = max(e21, candle_anchor, last_p * 0.992)
            elif tp1_age < 900 and wave_class == "long_wave" and peak_profit_pct < tp1_pct + 0.9:
                wave_step_sl = min(e50 - 0.35 * a15, last_p - 0.85 * a15)
            else:
                anchor = (0.35 * e50 + 0.65 * e21) if wave_class == "long_wave" else e21
                wave_step_sl = anchor - 0.32 * a15
                if prev_closed_low > wave_step_sl and last_p > prev_closed_low * 1.003:
                    wave_step_sl = prev_closed_low - 0.15 * a15

        dist_mult = 1.00 if wave_class == "long_wave" else (0.72 if wave_class == "short_wave" else 0.85)
        if peak_profit_pct >= tp1_pct + 1.0:
            dist_mult *= 0.78
        runner_trail = _round_px(max_p * (1.0 - cfg["runner_dist"] * dist_mult))

        raw_candidate = _round_px(max(breakeven, runner_floor, runner_trail, wave_step_sl))
        if raw_candidate >= last_p * 0.9945:
            raw_candidate = _round_px(last_p * 0.9935)

        if raw_candidate > new_sl * 1.0008 and last_p > raw_candidate * 1.0020:
            new_sl = raw_candidate

        if tp1_ref > 0 and new_sl >= tp1_ref * 0.9995:
            new_state = "ABOVE_TP1"
        elif new_sl > breakeven * 1.0025:
            new_state = "TRAILING_RUNNER"
        else:
            new_state = "BREAKEVEN"
    else:
        # ДО TP1: поджимаем стоп ТОЛЬКО когда цена уже прошла заметную часть пути к тейку (>= +0.68% или 55% пути к TP1),
        # чтобы не выбивать сделки на 20-й минуте в -0.37%..-0.54% (фикс JUP, POL, ARX)!
        shield_step_trigger = max(0.68, tp_target_pct * 0.55)
        if curr_profit_pct >= shield_step_trigger and a15 > 0 and e50 > 0:
            step_shield = _round_px(min(avg_p * 0.9980, max(e50 - 0.45 * a15, prev_closed_low - 0.25 * a15)))
            if step_shield > new_sl * 1.0015 and last_p > step_shield * 1.004:
                new_sl = step_shield
                new_state = "SHIELD"

        # Перевод в БУ (🛡🔒) до TP1 при достижении >= 0.88% (или 70% пути к TP1)
        be_trigger = max(0.88, tp_target_pct * 0.70)
        if curr_profit_pct >= be_trigger and breakeven > new_sl and last_p > breakeven * 1.0035:
            new_sl = breakeven
            new_state = "BREAKEVEN"

        # Если сделка висит >= 60 минут и тренд на 15m реально сломался (EMA21 < EMA50 и закрытие под EMA50) — поджимаем под свечу
        if pos_age >= 3600 and e21 > 0 and e50 > 0 and e21 < e50 * 0.998 and last_p < e50 * 0.998 and closed_low > 0 and a15 > 0:
            tight_broken_sl = _round_px(closed_low - 0.25 * a15)
            if tight_broken_sl > new_sl * 1.002 and last_p > tight_broken_sl * 1.003:
                new_sl = tight_broken_sl

        if is_climax and curr_profit_pct >= cfg["lock_trigger_pct"]:
            lock_sl = _round_px(max(breakeven, last_p * (1.0 - cfg["lock_dist"])))
            if lock_sl > new_sl and last_p > lock_sl * 1.0025:
                new_sl = lock_sl
                new_state = "PROFIT_LOCK"

    new_sl = _round_px(new_sl)

    if (new_sl > current_sl + 1e-9) or (new_state != current_state and new_sl >= current_sl):
        tag_desc = sl_emoji(new_state)
        return True, new_sl, new_state, tag_desc

    return False, current_sl, current_state, ""

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/strategy/risk.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/strategy/scanner.py ---
import os
import time
import asyncio

import httpx
from loguru import logger

from bot.exchange.market_data import market_data
from bot.exchange.paper_exchange import paper
from bot.strategy.indicators import ema, rsi, atr, find_sr_levels, get_all_sr_levels
from bot.strategy.learner import learner
from bot.strategy.shadow import shadow
from bot.strategy.patterns import detect_patterns
from bot.news.cmc import (get_coin_name, get_sectors_for_pool,
                          get_ranks_for_pool, tier_of, TIER_EMOJI, fetch_missing_names)
from bot.news.rss_news import fetch_news_cache, fetch_listings_cache, check_sentiment
from bot.strategy.fundamental import get_macro_trend, is_sector_hot, get_fear_and_greed, check_coinglass_liquidation_threat
from bot.utils.format import fmt_price

SCAN_SUMMARY = {"text": "", "thrs": {}, "ts": 0}
FILTERED_BY_NEWS = []

SCORE_MAX = 10.0

_instruments_cache = {"data": None, "ts": 0}

STABLE_BASES = {"USDC", "USDE", "DAI", "TUSD", "BUSD", "FDUSD", "USDP",
                "USD1", "USDD", "EUR", "EURT", "AEUR", "USDT",
                "RLUSD", "PYUSD", "EURI", "USDS", "USD0", "FRAX",
                "LUSD", "GUSD", "XUSD", "USDX", "CUSD", "SUSD",
                "PAXG", "XAUT"}

SYNTHETIC_BASES = {
    "AMZNX", "TSLAX", "NVDAX", "AAPLX", "COINX", "MSTRX",
    "GOOGLX", "METAX", "SPYX", "QQQX", "HOODX", "CRCLX",
    "MCDX", "NFLXX", "DFDVX", "MSFTX", "BRKX", "INTCX", "SPCXX",
}

BASE_THRESHOLDS = {
    "bull": {"rocket": 6.0, "sniper": 5.5, "reversal": 5.3},
    "neutral": {"rocket": 6.4, "sniper": 5.9, "reversal": 5.6},
    "bear": {"rocket": 6.8, "sniper": 6.2, "reversal": 6.0},
}

TIER_CORR_LIMITS = {
    "TOP20": 0.94,
    "MID":   0.90,
    "SMALL": 0.86,
    "MICRO": 0.80,
}

_regime_tracker = {
    "prev_regime": "neutral",
    "last_bear_ts": 0.0,
    "bull_start_ts": 0.0,
    "breadth_streak": 0,
}


def _init_regime_tracker():
    if _regime_tracker["last_bear_ts"] == 0.0 and hasattr(paper, "market_history") and paper.market_history:
        for h in reversed(paper.market_history):
            if h.get("regime") == "bear" and _regime_tracker["last_bear_ts"] == 0.0:
                _regime_tracker["last_bear_ts"] = float(h.get("ts", 0))
            if h.get("regime") == "bull" and _regime_tracker["bull_start_ts"] == 0.0:
                _regime_tracker["bull_start_ts"] = float(h.get("ts", 0))
        _regime_tracker["prev_regime"] = paper.market_history[-1].get("regime", "neutral")


def get_thresholds(regime):
    now = time.time()
    bull_start = _regime_tracker.get("bull_start_ts", 0.0)
    bull_age = (now - bull_start) if bull_start > 0 else 0

    if regime == "bull" and (bull_start == 0.0 or bull_age < 1800):
        base = BASE_THRESHOLDS["neutral"]
    else:
        base = BASE_THRESHOLDS.get(regime, BASE_THRESHOLDS["neutral"])

    adj = learner.threshold_adj
    t = shadow.tuning

    return {
        "rocket": round(max(0.0, min(SCORE_MAX - 0.5, base["rocket"] + adj + t.get("nudge_rocket", 0.0))), 2),
        "sniper": round(max(0.0, min(SCORE_MAX - 0.5, base["sniper"] + adj + t.get("nudge_sniper", 0.0))), 2),
        "reversal": round(max(0.0, min(SCORE_MAX - 0.5, base["reversal"] + adj + t.get("nudge_reversal", 0.0))), 2),
    }


def is_tradable(symbol):
    if not symbol.endswith("USDT"):
        return False
    base = symbol[:-4]
    if base in STABLE_BASES or base in SYNTHETIC_BASES:
        return False
    if base.endswith(("3L", "3S", "5L", "5S")):
        return False
    return True


def is_post_pump_dump(candles_15m: list) -> tuple:
    if not candles_15m or len(candles_15m) < 25:
        return False, ""
    recent = candles_15m[-20:]

    high_val = -1.0
    high_idx = -1
    for idx, c in enumerate(recent):
        if c["high"] > high_val:
            high_val = c["high"]
            high_idx = idx

    if high_idx >= 2:
        low_before = min(c["low"] for c in recent[:high_idx + 1])
        if low_before > 0:
            pump_magnitude = (high_val - low_before) / low_before * 100
            last_close = recent[-1]["close"]
            if pump_magnitude >= 4.5 and high_val > low_before:
                dump_depth = (high_val - last_close) / (high_val - low_before)
                drop_from_high_pct = (high_val - last_close) / high_val * 100
                closes_all = [c["close"] for c in candles_15m]
                e21_series = ema(closes_all, 21)
                e21_now = e21_series[-1]
                e21_falling = len(e21_series) >= 4 and e21_now < e21_series[-3] * 0.9992

                if dump_depth >= 0.26 and drop_from_high_pct >= 1.5 and (last_close < e21_now or e21_falling):
                    last_8 = recent[-8:]
                    box_8 = (max(c["high"] for c in last_8) - min(c["low"] for c in last_8)) / last_close if last_close > 0 else 1.0
                    if high_idx <= 8 and box_8 <= 0.022 and last_close >= e21_now and not e21_falling:
                        return False, ""
                    return True, f"пост-памп дамп 15m (+{pump_magnitude:.1f}%, откат {dump_depth*100:.0f}%)"

    return False, ""


def validate_1h_structure(candles_1h: list, nearest_sup: float, sup_touches: int, a15: float, candles_15m: list = None, patterns: list = None) -> tuple:
    """
    Среднесрочный 48ч фильтр фазы волны (Wave Phase Gatekeeper):
    - Блокирует покупки на хаях и на полнотелых красных свечах разворота.
    - Разрешает здоровые внутричасовые откаты к 15m EMA21 и откупы со дна часовых просадок (1H_DIP_BOUNCE / 1H_SUPPORT_ONLY).
    """
    if not candles_1h or len(candles_1h) < 50:
        return "1H_BAN", 0.0, 0.0, "недостаточно 1H истории (<48ч)", 0.0, 0.5

    closes_1h = [c["close"] for c in candles_1h]
    last_1h = closes_1h[-1]
    e21_series = ema(closes_1h, 21)
    e50_series = ema(closes_1h, 50)
    e21_1h = e21_series[-1]
    e50_1h = e50_series[-1]

    window_48h = candles_1h[-48:]
    open_48h = window_48h[0]["open"]
    chg_48h = ((last_1h - open_48h) / open_48h * 100) if open_48h > 0 else 0.0
    high_48h = max(c["high"] for c in window_48h)
    low_48h = min(c["low"] for c in window_48h)
    pos_in_48h = ((last_1h - low_48h) / (high_48h - low_48h)) if high_48h > low_48h else 0.5

    open_12h = candles_1h[-12]["open"] if len(candles_1h) >= 12 else open_48h
    chg_12h = ((last_1h - open_12h) / open_12h * 100) if open_12h > 0 else 0.0

    e21_3h_ago = e21_series[-4] if len(e21_series) >= 4 else e21_series[0]
    e50_6h_ago = e50_series[-7] if len(e50_series) >= 7 else e50_series[0]
    e50_12h_ago = e50_series[-13] if len(e50_series) >= 13 else e50_series[0]
    e50_24h_ago = e50_series[-25] if len(e50_series) >= 25 else e50_series[0]

    r_1h = rsi(closes_1h)
    a_1h = atr(candles_1h)

    drop_from_48h_high_pct = ((high_48h - last_1h) / high_48h * 100) if high_48h > 0 else 0.0
    if drop_from_48h_high_pct >= 14.0 and last_1h < e21_1h and e21_1h < e50_1h and pos_in_48h > 0.35:
        return "1H_BAN", e21_1h, e50_1h, f"1H крах с хая (-{drop_from_48h_high_pct:.1f}% под EMA21/50)", round(chg_48h, 1), round(pos_in_48h, 2)

    recent_24_1h = candles_1h[-24:] if len(candles_1h) >= 24 else candles_1h
    hi24_idx = max(range(len(recent_24_1h)), key=lambda i: recent_24_1h[i]["high"])
    max_h_24 = recent_24_1h[hi24_idx]["high"]
    bars_since_hi24 = (len(recent_24_1h) - 1) - hi24_idx
    min_before_hi24 = min(c["low"] for c in recent_24_1h[:hi24_idx + 1])
    wave_24_pct = ((max_h_24 - min_before_hi24) / min_before_hi24 * 100) if min_before_hi24 > 0 else 0.0
    drop_from_hi24_pct = ((max_h_24 - last_1h) / max_h_24 * 100) if max_h_24 > 0 else 0.0

    recent_10_1h = candles_1h[-10:] if len(candles_1h) >= 10 else candles_1h
    hi10_idx = max(range(len(recent_10_1h)), key=lambda i: recent_10_1h[i]["high"])
    bars_since_hi10 = (len(recent_10_1h) - 1) - hi10_idx

    curr_1h = candles_1h[-1]
    prev_1h = candles_1h[-2]
    prev2_1h = candles_1h[-3] if len(candles_1h) >= 3 else prev_1h

    curr_1h_red = curr_1h["close"] < curr_1h["open"]
    prev_1h_red = prev_1h["close"] < prev_1h["open"]
    curr_1h_red_body = max(0.0, curr_1h["open"] - curr_1h["close"])
    prev_1h_red_body = max(0.0, prev_1h["open"] - prev_1h["close"])

    # Считаем часовую свечу «сливной» только если её красное тело > 0.35*ATR_1h (а не микро-откат -0.1% внутри часа!)
    heavy_curr_1h_red = curr_1h_red and a_1h > 0 and (curr_1h_red_body >= 0.35 * a_1h)

    # --- 1. ЖЁСТКИЙ БАН ПОКУПОК НА РАЗВОРОТЕ С ХАЯ ---
    two_red_1h_from_top = (
        curr_1h_red and prev_1h_red and
        a_1h > 0 and (curr_1h_red_body + prev_1h_red_body) >= 0.50 * a_1h and
        (bars_since_hi10 in (1, 2, 3) or pos_in_48h >= 0.52)
    )
    red_reversal_at_high = (
        pos_in_48h >= 0.52 and bars_since_hi10 <= 3 and a_1h > 0 and
        ((prev_1h_red and prev_1h_red_body >= 0.42 * a_1h and last_1h <= prev_1h["close"] * 1.001) or
         (curr_1h_red and curr_1h_red_body >= 0.45 * a_1h))
    )
    if two_red_1h_from_top or red_reversal_at_high:
        return "1H_BAN", e21_1h, e50_1h, f"1H разворот вниз с локального хая (диап. {pos_in_48h*100:.0f}%)", round(chg_48h, 1), round(pos_in_48h, 2)

    big_1h_dump_candle = (
        (curr_1h_red_body > 0.65 * a_1h and curr_1h["close"] < e21_1h * 1.001) or
        (prev_1h_red_body > 0.70 * a_1h and last_1h <= prev_1h["open"] * 0.997 and last_1h < e21_1h * 1.001) or
        ((prev2_1h["open"] - prev2_1h["close"]) > 0.80 * a_1h and prev_1h_red_body > 0.55 * a_1h and last_1h < e21_1h)
    )
    if big_1h_dump_candle and bars_since_hi24 >= 1:
        return "1H_BAN", e21_1h, e50_1h, f"1H активный слив под EMA21 (-{drop_from_hi24_pct:.1f}% от пика)", round(chg_48h, 1), round(pos_in_48h, 2)

    has_breakout_pat = "breakout" in (patterns or [])
    if bars_since_hi24 >= 2 and wave_24_pct >= 4.8 and not has_breakout_pat:
        retrace_ratio = (max_h_24 - last_1h) / (max_h_24 - min_before_hi24) if max_h_24 > min_before_hi24 else 0.0
        e21_rolling_over = e21_1h <= e21_3h_ago * 1.0002
        lower_highs_1h = max(curr_1h["high"], prev_1h["high"]) < max_h_24 * 0.996
        if pos_in_48h > 0.50 and lower_highs_1h and (retrace_ratio >= 0.18 or e21_rolling_over or last_1h < e21_1h * 1.001):
            return "1H_BAN", e21_1h, e50_1h, f"1H остывание после пампа (+{wave_24_pct:.1f}%, ждём откат ниже 50%)", round(chg_48h, 1), round(pos_in_48h, 2)
        if retrace_ratio >= 0.30 and (last_1h < e21_1h * 0.998 or e21_rolling_over):
            return "1H_BAN", e21_1h, e50_1h, f"1H слом волны после пика (-{drop_from_hi24_pct:.1f}%)", round(chg_48h, 1), round(pos_in_48h, 2)

    e21_rising_1h = e21_1h > e21_3h_ago * 1.0003
    e50_rising_1h = e50_1h >= e50_6h_ago * 0.9996
    e50_falling_48h = (e50_1h < e50_12h_ago * 0.995) and (e50_1h < e50_24h_ago * 0.990)

    # --- АНАЛИЗ 15M СТРУКТУРЫ И РАЗВОРОТА СО ДНА ПРОСАДКИ ---
    healthy_15m_trend = True
    is_15m_dip_turn = False
    reversal_15m_confirmed = False

    if candles_15m and len(candles_15m) >= 35:
        c15_closes = [c["close"] for c in candles_15m]
        e12_15 = ema(c15_closes, 12)[-1]
        e21_15 = ema(c15_closes, 21)[-1]
        e50_15 = ema(c15_closes, 50)[-1]
        r_15 = rsi(c15_closes)
        last_15 = c15_closes[-1]
        curr_15 = candles_15m[-1]
        closed_15 = candles_15m[-2]
        prev_closed_15 = candles_15m[-3]
        low_last_3 = min(c["low"] for c in candles_15m[-3:])
        low_prev_6 = min(c["low"] for c in candles_15m[-8:-2])

        max_atr_dist = 2.00 if has_breakout_pat else 1.25
        max_rsi_15 = 72 if has_breakout_pat else 65
        if (
            e21_15 < e50_15 * 0.9985 or
            last_15 < e50_15 * 0.9975 or
            (a15 > 0 and (last_15 - e21_15) > max_atr_dist * a15) or
            r_15 > max_rsi_15 or
            (curr_15["close"] < curr_15["open"] and (curr_15["open"] - curr_15["close"]) > 0.42 * a15)
        ):
            healthy_15m_trend = False

        closed_rng_15 = closed_15["high"] - closed_15["low"]
        closed_lwick_15 = min(closed_15["open"], closed_15["close"]) - closed_15["low"]
        has_15m_pin = closed_rng_15 > 0 and (closed_lwick_15 / closed_rng_15) >= 0.42

        bottom_held = low_last_3 >= low_prev_6 * 0.9980
        green_turn_15m = (
            (closed_15["close"] > closed_15["open"] or has_15m_pin) and
            (curr_15["close"] >= curr_15["open"] * 0.9990 or last_15 >= closed_15["close"] * 0.9995) and
            last_15 >= e12_15 * 0.9985 and
            24 <= r_15 <= 60
        )
        if bottom_held and green_turn_15m:
            is_15m_dip_turn = True

        # Для разворота от плиты (reversal_15m_confirmed) не требуем last_15 >= e21_15 (на дне цена ниже EMA21),
        # достаточно возврата над быстрой EMA12 и удержания дна!
        if (
            bottom_held and
            last_15 >= e12_15 * 0.9985 and
            (closed_15["close"] > closed_15["open"] or has_15m_pin) and
            curr_15["close"] >= curr_15["open"] * 0.9990 and
            24 <= r_15 <= 58
        ):
            reversal_15m_confirmed = True

    # --- 2. РАННИЙ ВХОД НА ПРОСАДКЕ 1H («1H_DIP_BOUNCE») ---
    curr_1h_rng = curr_1h["high"] - curr_1h["low"]
    prev_1h_rng = prev_1h["high"] - prev_1h["low"]
    has_1h_bottom_wick = (
        (curr_1h_rng > 0 and (min(curr_1h["open"], curr_1h["close"]) - curr_1h["low"]) / curr_1h_rng >= 0.35) or
        (prev_1h_rng > 0 and (min(prev_1h["open"], prev_1h["close"]) - prev_1h["low"]) / prev_1h_rng >= 0.35)
    )
    near_1h_ema_support = (
        (e50_1h > 0 and -2.4 * a_1h <= (last_1h - e50_1h) <= 1.3 * a_1h and not e50_falling_48h) or
        (e21_1h > e50_1h and -1.4 * a_1h <= (last_1h - e21_1h) <= 0.9 * a_1h) or
        (nearest_sup and sup_touches >= 2 and 0 <= (last_1h - nearest_sup) <= 2.4 * a15) or
        (has_1h_bottom_wick and pos_in_48h <= 0.42)
    )
    if (
        is_15m_dip_turn and
        near_1h_ema_support and
        0.02 <= pos_in_48h <= 0.56 and
        -10.0 <= chg_48h <= 22.0 and
        not heavy_curr_1h_red
    ):
        return "1H_DIP_BOUNCE", e21_1h, e50_1h, f"1H откуп просадки на опоре ({chg_48h:+.1f}%, диап. {pos_in_48h*100:.0f}%)", round(chg_48h, 1), round(pos_in_48h, 2)

    # --- 3. ФИЛЬТР ПОТОЛКА ДЛЯ БОКОВИКОВ И ТРЕНДОВ ---
    if chg_48h >= 8.0 and wave_24_pct >= 5.0 and e21_rising_1h and e50_rising_1h and bars_since_hi24 <= 1 and not heavy_curr_1h_red:
        max_allowed_pos_48 = 0.80
    elif chg_48h >= 4.0 and wave_24_pct >= 3.8 and e21_rising_1h and e50_rising_1h and not heavy_curr_1h_red:
        max_allowed_pos_48 = 0.68
    else:
        max_allowed_pos_48 = 0.60

    is_uptrend_1h = (
        last_1h >= e21_1h * 0.998 and
        e21_1h >= e50_1h * 0.9995 and
        e21_rising_1h and
        not e50_falling_48h and
        not heavy_curr_1h_red and
        healthy_15m_trend and
        0.15 <= pos_in_48h <= max_allowed_pos_48 and
        0.5 <= chg_48h <= 28.0 and
        r_1h <= 70
    )

    is_fresh_1h_reversal = (
        last_1h >= e21_1h * 0.999 and
        last_1h >= e50_1h * 0.998 and
        e21_rising_1h and
        e50_rising_1h and
        not heavy_curr_1h_red and
        healthy_15m_trend and
        0.12 <= pos_in_48h <= 0.58 and
        0.5 <= chg_12h <= 14.0 and
        r_1h <= 66
    )

    is_breakout_1h = (
        has_breakout_pat and
        last_1h >= e21_1h * 1.001 and
        e21_1h >= e50_1h * 1.000 and
        e21_rising_1h and
        not curr_1h_red and
        healthy_15m_trend and
        bars_since_hi24 <= 1 and
        0.35 <= pos_in_48h <= 0.85 and
        2.0 <= chg_48h <= 30.0 and
        r_1h <= 72
    )

    is_super_trend_pullback = (
        last_1h >= e21_1h * 1.000 and
        e21_1h > e50_1h * 1.004 and
        e21_rising_1h and
        e50_rising_1h and
        not heavy_curr_1h_red and
        healthy_15m_trend and
        bars_since_hi24 <= 1 and
        0.35 <= pos_in_48h <= 0.80 and
        8.0 <= chg_48h <= 50.0 and
        r_1h <= 70
    )

    if is_uptrend_1h or is_fresh_1h_reversal or is_breakout_1h or is_super_trend_pullback:
        if is_super_trend_pullback and not is_uptrend_1h:
            return "1H_SUPER_TREND", e21_1h, e50_1h, f"1H супер-тренд волна (+{chg_48h:.1f}%, диап. {pos_in_48h*100:.0f}%)", round(chg_48h, 1), round(pos_in_48h, 2)
        if is_breakout_1h and not is_uptrend_1h:
            tag_1h = "1H пробой базы"
            ref_chg = chg_48h
        elif is_fresh_1h_reversal and not is_uptrend_1h:
            tag_1h = "1H разворот тренда"
            ref_chg = chg_12h
        else:
            tag_1h = "1H аптренд 48ч"
            ref_chg = chg_48h
        return "1H_TREND_OK", e21_1h, e50_1h, f"{tag_1h} ({ref_chg:+.1f}%, диап. {pos_in_48h*100:.0f}%)", round(chg_48h, 1), round(pos_in_48h, 2)

    if pos_in_48h > max_allowed_pos_48 or chg_48h > 22.0:
        return "1H_BAN", e21_1h, e50_1h, f"1H верх диапазона — риск хая ({chg_48h:+.1f}%, диап. {pos_in_48h*100:.0f}%)", round(chg_48h, 1), round(pos_in_48h, 2)

    has_strong_slab = bool(nearest_sup and sup_touches >= 2 and a15 > 0 and 0 <= (last_1h - nearest_sup) <= 2.2 * a15)
    is_1h_waterfall = (
        (heavy_curr_1h_red and prev_1h_red) or
        (curr_1h["low"] <= low_48h * 1.002 and curr_1h_red) or
        ((e21_3h_ago - e21_1h) > 0.65 * a_1h)
    )

    if is_1h_waterfall and not reversal_15m_confirmed:
        return "1H_BAN", e21_1h, e50_1h, f"1H падающий нож без разворота 15m ({chg_48h:+.1f}%)", round(chg_48h, 1), round(pos_in_48h, 2)

    if last_1h < e50_1h and e50_falling_48h:
        dist_below_e50_1h = (e50_1h - last_1h) / a_1h if a_1h > 0 else 0.0
        if has_strong_slab and reversal_15m_confirmed and dist_below_e50_1h <= 3.2 and pos_in_48h <= 0.38 and drop_from_48h_high_pct < 15.0:
            return "1H_SUPPORT_ONLY", e21_1h, e50_1h, f"1H плита + разворот 15m ({sup_touches}x, диап. {pos_in_48h*100:.0f}%)", round(chg_48h, 1), round(pos_in_48h, 2)
        return "1H_BAN", e21_1h, e50_1h, f"1H даунтренд ({chg_48h:+.1f}%)", round(chg_48h, 1), round(pos_in_48h, 2)

    if has_strong_slab and reversal_15m_confirmed and pos_in_48h <= 0.40:
        return "1H_SUPPORT_ONLY", e21_1h, e50_1h, f"1H опора на плиту ({sup_touches}x, диап. {pos_in_48h*100:.0f}%)", round(chg_48h, 1), round(pos_in_48h, 2)

    return "1H_BAN", e21_1h, e50_1h, f"1H нет точки входа ({chg_48h:+.1f}%)", round(chg_48h, 1), round(pos_in_48h, 2)


def raw_max_score(regime):
    m = sum(learner.weight(k) for k in ("ema50", "ema21", "impulse", "rsi", "volume", "chg24h"))
    m += max(learner.weight("indep"), learner.weight("news_pos"), learner.weight("hype"))
    m += 4.8
    return max(12.4, m)


def _returns(closes):
    return [(b - a) / a for a, b in zip(closes, closes[1:]) if a]


def _corr(a, b):
    n = min(len(a), len(b))
    if n < 10:
        return 0.0
    a, b = a[-n:], b[-n:]
    ma, mb = sum(a) / n, sum(b) / n
    cov = sum((x - ma) * (y - mb) for x, y in zip(a, b))
    va = sum((x - ma) ** 2 for x in a)
    vb = sum((y - mb) ** 2 for y in b)
    if va <= 0 or vb <= 0:
        return 0.0
    return cov / (va * vb) ** 0.5


def _corr_candles(candles_a: list, candles_b: list) -> float:
    if not candles_a or not candles_b or len(candles_a) < 15 or len(candles_b) < 15:
        return 0.0
    closed_a = candles_a[:-1]
    closed_b = candles_b[:-1]

    ret_a = {}
    for i in range(1, len(closed_a)):
        p0 = closed_a[i - 1]["close"]
        if p0 > 0:
            ret_a[closed_a[i]["ts"]] = (closed_a[i]["close"] - p0) / p0

    ret_b = {}
    for i in range(1, len(closed_b)):
        p0 = closed_b[i - 1]["close"]
        if p0 > 0:
            ret_b[closed_b[i]["ts"]] = (closed_b[i]["close"] - p0) / p0

    common_ts = sorted(set(ret_a.keys()) & set(ret_b.keys()))
    if len(common_ts) < 12:
        return _corr(_returns([c["close"] for c in closed_a]), _returns([c["close"] for c in closed_b]))

    common_ts = common_ts[-96:]
    return _corr([ret_a[t] for t in common_ts], [ret_b[t] for t in common_ts])


async def get_regime():
    now = time.time()
    _init_regime_tracker()

    candles = await market_data.get_kline("BTCUSDT", "60", 250)
    if len(candles) < 60:
        return _regime_tracker.get("prev_regime", "neutral"), {}
    closes = [c["close"] for c in candles]
    e21, e50, e200 = ema(closes, 21)[-1], ema(closes, 50)[-1], ema(closes, 200)[-1]
    last = closes[-1]

    if e21 > e50 > e200:
        raw_regime = "bull"
    elif e21 < e50 < e200:
        raw_regime = "bear"
    else:
        raw_regime = "neutral"

    breadth_bull = False
    tickers = await market_data.get_tickers()
    if tickers:
        tradable = [s for s, t in tickers.items() if is_tradable(s) and t["quote_volume"] >= 500_000]
        if tradable:
            green_alts = sum(1 for s in tradable if tickers[s]["change_pct"] > 3.0)
            breadth_pct = (green_alts / len(tradable)) * 100

            if breadth_pct >= 45.0:
                _regime_tracker["breadth_streak"] += 1
                if _regime_tracker["breadth_streak"] >= 2:
                    breadth_bull = True
                    if raw_regime == "neutral":
                        raw_regime = "bull"
            else:
                _regime_tracker["breadth_streak"] = 0

    stable_trend = get_macro_trend()
    fng = get_fear_and_greed()

    if stable_trend == "bull" and raw_regime == "neutral":
        raw_regime = "bull"
    elif stable_trend == "bear" and raw_regime == "bull" and not breadth_bull:
        raw_regime = "neutral"

    if fng < 35 and raw_regime == "bull":
        raw_regime = "neutral"
    elif fng > 78 and raw_regime == "bear":
        raw_regime = "neutral"

    regime = raw_regime

    if regime == "bear":
        _regime_tracker["last_bear_ts"] = now
        _regime_tracker["bull_start_ts"] = 0.0
    elif regime == "bull":
        time_since_bear = now - _regime_tracker["last_bear_ts"]
        if _regime_tracker["last_bear_ts"] > 0 and time_since_bear < 2700:
            regime = "neutral"
        else:
            if _regime_tracker["bull_start_ts"] == 0.0:
                _regime_tracker["bull_start_ts"] = now
    else:
        _regime_tracker["bull_start_ts"] = 0.0

    _regime_tracker["prev_regime"] = regime
    return regime, {"btc": last, "ema50": e50, "ema200": e200}


async def fetch_new_listings():
    now = time.time()
    if _instruments_cache["data"] is not None and now - _instruments_cache["ts"] < 3600:
        raw = _instruments_cache["data"]
    else:
        raw = []
        try:
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=15) as c:
                cursor = ""
                for page in range(5):
                    params = {"category": "spot", "limit": 1000}
                    if cursor:
                        params["cursor"] = cursor
                    r = await c.get("https://api.bybit.com/v5/market/instruments-info", params=params)
                    if r.status_code != 200:
                        break
                    data = r.json()
                    result = data.get("result", {})
                    items = result.get("list", [])
                    if not items:
                        break
                    raw += items
                    cursor = result.get("nextPageCursor", "") or ""
                    if not cursor:
                        break
            _instruments_cache["data"], _instruments_cache["ts"] = raw, now
        except Exception:
            raw = []

    now_ms = int(now * 1000)
    out = []
    for ins in raw:
        sym = ins.get("symbol", "")
        if not sym.endswith("USDT") or ins.get("status", "") != "Trading":
            continue
        try:
            launch = int(ins.get("launchTime", 0) or 0)
        except Exception:
            launch = 0
        if launch > 0:
            out.append((sym, (now_ms - launch) / 3600000))
    return out


def score_symbol(candles_15m, candles_1h, t, regime, is_open_pos=False, saved_patterns=None, saved_mode=None, saved_gate_1h=None):
    closes = [c["close"] for c in candles_15m]
    last = closes[-1]
    e21, e50 = ema(closes, 21)[-1], ema(closes, 50)[-1]
    e12, e26 = ema(closes, 12)[-1], ema(closes, 26)[-1]
    r = rsi(closes)
    a15 = atr(candles_15m)

    vols = [c["volume"] for c in candles_15m]
    base_vol = sum(vols[-22:-2]) / 20 if len(vols) > 22 else (sum(vols) / max(1, len(vols)))
    eff_vol = max(vols[-1], vols[-2]) if len(vols) >= 2 else vols[-1]
    vol_ratio = eff_vol / base_vol if base_vol else 1.0

    prior_candles = candles_15m[-26:-2] if len(candles_15m) >= 26 else candles_15m[:-1]
    recent_high = max(c["high"] for c in prior_candles) if prior_candles else last
    recent_low_2h = min(c["low"] for c in candles_15m[-8:]) if len(candles_15m) >= 8 else min(c["low"] for c in candles_15m)
    recent_low_6 = min(c["low"] for c in candles_15m[-6:]) if len(candles_15m) >= 6 else recent_low_2h

    w = shadow.signal_windows()
    score, reasons, keys = 0.0, [], []

    if last > e50:
        score += learner.weight("ema50")
        reasons.append("цена выше EMA50")
        keys.append("ema50")
    if e21 > e50:
        score += learner.weight("ema21")
        reasons.append("EMA21>EMA50")
        keys.append("ema21")
    if e12 > e26:
        score += learner.weight("impulse")
        reasons.append("импульс роста")
        keys.append("impulse")

    if r < 38:
        score += learner.weight("rsi")
        reasons.append(f"перепроданность RSI {r:.0f}")
        keys.append("rsi")
    elif 38 <= r <= w["rsi_hi"]:
        score += learner.weight("rsi")
        reasons.append(f"RSI {r:.0f}")
        keys.append("rsi")
    elif r > w["rsi_hi"]:
        score -= 1.5
        reasons.append(f"перегрев RSI {r:.0f}")

    last_candle = candles_15m[-1]
    prev_candle = candles_15m[-2]
    is_green = last_candle["close"] >= last_candle["open"] or (vols[-2] > vols[-1] and prev_candle["close"] >= prev_candle["open"])
    c_range = last_candle["high"] - last_candle["low"]
    lower_wick = min(last_candle["open"], last_candle["close"]) - last_candle["low"]
    is_panic_dip = (t["change_pct"] <= -6.0 or r < 38)

    if vol_ratio > w["vol_lo"]:
        has_bottom_wick = c_range > 0 and (lower_wick / c_range) >= 0.40
        if is_green or has_bottom_wick:
            score += learner.weight("volume")
            reasons.append(f"объём покупок x{vol_ratio:.1f}")
            keys.append("volume")
        elif not is_panic_dip:
            score -= 2.0
            reasons.append(f"сливной объём x{vol_ratio:.1f} (давление продаж)")

    recent_rejection = False
    for c in candles_15m[-3:]:
        c_rng = c["high"] - c["low"]
        c_w = c["high"] - max(c["close"], c["open"])
        if c_rng > 0 and (c_w / c_rng) > 0.42 and c["high"] >= recent_high * 0.996:
            recent_rejection = True
            break
    if recent_rejection and not is_open_pos:
        score -= 4.5
        reasons.append("🚫 отвержение на хаях (шпилька продаж)")

    if not is_open_pos and a15 > 0 and e21 > 0:
        dist_from_ema = last - e21
        if dist_from_ema > 1.35 * a15:
            score -= 4.0
            reasons.append(f"FOMO-перегрев (+{dist_from_ema/a15:.1f} ATR от EMA21)")

    detected_patterns = detect_patterns(candles_15m, a15, e50, e21, recent_high)
    matched_modes = []
    pattern_keys = []
    wick_entry_price = None

    for pat in detected_patterns:
        pkey = pat["key"]
        p_bonus = pat["base_bonus"] * learner.pattern_multiplier(pkey)
        score += p_bonus
        reasons.append(f"{pat['name']} (+{p_bonus:.1f})")
        pattern_keys.append(pkey)
        matched_modes.append(pat["mode"])
        if pat.get("wick_entry_price") and wick_entry_price is None:
            wick_entry_price = pat["wick_entry_price"]

    if saved_patterns:
        base_bonuses = {"pinbar": 2.8 if saved_mode == "reversal" else 2.0, "divergence": 2.8, "vcp": 2.6, "breakout": 3.0, "engulfing": 2.2}
        for sp in saved_patterns:
            if sp not in pattern_keys:
                p_bonus = base_bonuses.get(sp, 2.2) * learner.pattern_multiplier(sp)
                score += p_bonus
                pattern_keys.append(sp)
        if saved_mode and saved_mode not in matched_modes:
            matched_modes.append(saved_mode)

    local_swing_drop = (recent_high - last) / recent_high * 100 if recent_high > last else 0.0
    if 0 < t["change_pct"] < w["chg_hi"]:
        score += learner.weight("chg24h")
        reasons.append(f"24ч +{t['change_pct']:.1f}%")
        keys.append("chg24h")
    elif (t["change_pct"] <= -3.5 or local_swing_drop >= 2.2) and ("reversal" in matched_modes or r <= 48):
        score += learner.weight("chg24h")
        reasons.append(f"дисконт на просадке -{local_swing_drop:.1f}%")
        keys.append("chg24h")
    elif t["change_pct"] >= w["chg_hi"]:
        score -= 1.0
        reasons.append(f"памп +{t['change_pct']:.1f}% (уже поздно)")

    if t["quote_volume"] < 500_000:
        score -= 1.0

    nearest_res, res_touches, nearest_sup, sup_touches = find_sr_levels(candles_15m, candles_1h)

    if nearest_res and res_touches >= 6 and a15 > 0 and not is_open_pos:
        dist_to_res = nearest_res - last
        if dist_to_res < 0.4 * a15 or (dist_to_res / last * 100) < 0.24:
            score -= 3.5
            reasons.append(f"в упор к капитальной стене ${fmt_price(nearest_res)} ({res_touches}x)")

    sup_is_near = False
    closed_candle_holds_sup = False
    if nearest_sup and sup_touches >= 2 and a15 > 0:
        dist_to_sup = last - nearest_sup
        if 0 <= dist_to_sup <= 2.0 * a15:
            sup_is_near = True
            closed_candle_holds_sup = (
                (candles_15m[-2]["close"] >= nearest_sup * 0.9985) and
                (candles_15m[-2]["close"] >= candles_15m[-2]["open"] or
                 (candles_15m[-2]["close"] - candles_15m[-2]["low"]) >= 0.42 * (candles_15m[-2]["high"] - candles_15m[-2]["low"])) and
                last_candle["close"] >= last_candle["open"] * 0.9990
            )
            if not is_open_pos and closed_candle_holds_sup:
                sup_bonus = 2.2 if sup_touches >= 3 else 1.4
                score += sup_bonus
                reasons.append(f"опора на поддержку ${fmt_price(nearest_sup)} ({sup_touches}x, +{sup_bonus:.1f})")
                keys.append("support_bounce")
                if sup_touches >= 3 and "reversal" not in matched_modes and any(p in pattern_keys for p in ("pinbar", "divergence")):
                    matched_modes.append("reversal")

    gate_1h, e21_1h, e50_1h, gate_reason, chg_48h, pos_in_48h = validate_1h_structure(
        candles_1h, nearest_sup, sup_touches, a15, candles_15m=candles_15m, patterns=pattern_keys
    )

    if is_open_pos and saved_gate_1h == "1H_DIP_BOUNCE" and gate_1h == "1H_BAN" and pos_in_48h <= 0.58:
        gate_1h = "1H_DIP_BOUNCE"

    if chg_48h > 10.0 or pos_in_48h > 0.40:
        matched_modes = [m for m in matched_modes if m != "reversal"]

    if candles_1h and len(candles_1h) >= 50:
        closes_1h = [c["close"] for c in candles_1h]
        r_1h = rsi(closes_1h)

        if gate_1h == "1H_DIP_BOUNCE":
            missing_ema_comp = 0.0
            if last <= e50:
                missing_ema_comp += learner.weight("ema50")
            if e21 <= e50:
                missing_ema_comp += learner.weight("ema21")
            dip_bonus = round(missing_ema_comp + 2.2, 2)
            score += dip_bonus
            reasons.append(f"MTF: разворот со дна 1H просадки (+{dip_bonus:.1f})")
            keys.append("mtf_dip")
            if "ema50" not in keys:
                keys.append("ema50")
            if "impulse" not in keys:
                keys.append("impulse")
        elif gate_1h in ("1H_TREND_OK", "1H_SUPER_TREND") and (r_1h < 66 or abs(last - e21) <= 1.0 * a15) and e12 >= e26 * 0.999:
            score += 1.6
            reasons.append("MTF: выкуп отката в 1H аптренде (+1.6)")
            keys.append("mtf_dip")
        elif gate_1h == "1H_BAN" and not is_open_pos:
            score -= 5.0
            reasons.append(f"🚫 {gate_reason}")

        if gate_1h == "1H_SUPPORT_ONLY":
            matched_modes = [m for m in matched_modes if m == "reversal"]
            if not matched_modes and sup_is_near and sup_touches >= 2 and pos_in_48h <= 0.40 and closed_candle_holds_sup:
                matched_modes = ["reversal"]

    has_structure = (
        sup_is_near or
        len(pattern_keys) > 0 or
        ("support_bounce" in keys) or
        ("mtf_dip" in keys)
    )

    confirmed_res, confirmed_sup = get_all_sr_levels(candles_15m, candles_1h)

    signal_values = {
        "rsi": round(r, 1), "chg24h": round(t["change_pct"], 2), "volume": round(vol_ratio, 2),
        "nearest_res": nearest_res, "res_touches": res_touches,
        "nearest_sup": nearest_sup, "sup_touches": sup_touches,
        "all_res": confirmed_res,
        "all_sup": confirmed_sup,
        "recent_high": recent_high,
        "recent_low_2h": recent_low_2h,
        "recent_low_6": recent_low_6,
        "e21": e21, "e50": e50,
        "e21_1h": e21_1h, "e50_1h": e50_1h,
        "gate_1h": gate_1h,
        "reason_1h": gate_reason,
        "chg_48h": chg_48h,
        "pos_in_48h": pos_in_48h,
        "recent_rejection": recent_rejection,
        "wick_entry_price": wick_entry_price,
        "matched_modes": matched_modes,
        "patterns": pattern_keys,
        "has_structure": has_structure,
    }
    return score, reasons, keys, signal_values


def normalize(raw, regime):
    rm = raw_max_score(regime)
    if rm <= 0:
        return 0.0
    return round(max(0.0, min(SCORE_MAX, raw / rm * SCORE_MAX)), 2)


async def live_score(sym, t, regime, btc_ret, news_items=None, deriv_t=None, is_open_pos=False, saved_patterns=None, saved_mode=None, btc_candles=None, saved_gate_1h=None):
    candles_15m = await market_data.get_kline(sym, "15", 120)
    candles_1h = await market_data.get_kline(sym, "60", 250)

    if len(candles_15m) < 60 or len(candles_1h) < 60:
        return None, candles_15m

    raw, _, keys_live, sv_live = score_symbol(
        candles_15m, candles_1h, t, regime,
        is_open_pos=is_open_pos,
        saved_patterns=saved_patterns,
        saved_mode=saved_mode,
        saved_gate_1h=saved_gate_1h,
    )

    base = sym[:-4]
    sectors_map = await get_sectors_for_pool([base])
    ranks_map = await get_ranks_for_pool([base])
    sector = sectors_map.get(base, "Other")
    tier = tier_of(ranks_map.get(base))

    if btc_candles:
        corr = _corr_candles(candles_15m, btc_candles)
    elif isinstance(btc_ret, list) and btc_ret and isinstance(btc_ret[0], dict):
        corr = _corr_candles(candles_15m, btc_ret)
    else:
        corr = _corr(_returns([c["close"] for c in candles_15m[:-1]]), btc_ret[:-1] if btc_ret else [])

    tier_corr_limit = TIER_CORR_LIMITS.get(tier, 0.86)
    modes = sv_live.get("matched_modes", [])
    entry_mode_live = "reversal" if "reversal" in modes else ("rocket" if "rocket" in modes else "sniper")
    gate_1h_live = sv_live.get("gate_1h", "1H_BAN")

    if corr > tier_corr_limit and regime in ("neutral", "bear") and entry_mode_live != "reversal":
        raw -= 0.6
    elif abs(corr) < 0.25 and gate_1h_live in ("1H_TREND_OK", "1H_SUPER_TREND", "1H_DIP_BOUNCE"):
        raw += learner.weight("indep") * 1.15
    elif corr < 0.45:
        raw += learner.weight("indep")

    fng = get_fear_and_greed()
    if fng >= 80:
        raw -= 0.5
    elif fng <= 25 and entry_mode_live == "reversal":
        raw += 0.3

    if is_sector_hot(sector):
        raw += 0.4

    sb = learner.sector_bias(sector)
    if sb:
        raw += sb
    tb = learner.tier_bias(tier)
    if tb:
        raw += tb
    cb = learner.coin_bias(sym)
    if cb:
        raw += cb

    if news_items is not None:
        name = await get_coin_name(base)
        neg, pos_news, mentions, _ = check_sentiment(news_items, [base, name])
        if pos_news > neg:
            raw += learner.weight("news_pos")
        elif mentions >= 2:
            raw += learner.weight("hype")

    score10 = normalize(raw, regime)

    if deriv_t:
        funding = deriv_t.get("funding", 0)
        if funding > 0.05 and entry_mode_live == "rocket":
            score10 -= 0.5
        elif funding < -0.01:
            score10 += 0.5

    is_sqz = await check_coinglass_liquidation_threat(sym)
    if is_sqz:
        score10 -= 1.5

    score10 = round(max(0.0, min(SCORE_MAX, score10)), 2)
    return score10, candles_15m


async def scan(regime, tickers, deriv_tickers, limit=20):
    tradable = [s for s, t in tickers.items() if is_tradable(s) and t["quote_volume"] >= 450_000 and t["last"] > 0]

    by_vol = sorted(tradable, key=lambda s: tickers[s]["quote_volume"], reverse=True)[:45]
    by_chg = sorted([s for s in tradable if 0.5 < tickers[s]["change_pct"] < 28], key=lambda s: tickers[s]["change_pct"], reverse=True)[:35]
    by_dip = sorted([s for s in tradable if -12.0 <= tickers[s]["change_pct"] <= -1.5 and tickers[s]["quote_volume"] >= 700_000], key=lambda s: tickers[s]["change_pct"])[:15]

    pre_sem = asyncio.Semaphore(10)

    async def _check_mom_vol(sym):
        async with pre_sem:
            c1h, c15 = await asyncio.gather(
                market_data.get_kline(sym, "60", 250),
                market_data.get_kline(sym, "15", 60),
            )
            chg_7d = 0.0
            if len(c1h) >= 168 and c1h[-168]["close"] > 0:
                chg_7d = (c1h[-1]["close"] - c1h[-168]["close"]) / c1h[-168]["close"] * 100
            elif len(c1h) >= 100 and c1h[0]["close"] > 0:
                chg_7d = (c1h[-1]["close"] - c1h[0]["close"]) / c1h[0]["close"] * 100
            atr_pct_val = 0.0
            if len(c15) >= 30 and c15[-1]["close"] > 0:
                atr_pct_val = (atr(c15) / c15[-1]["close"]) * 100
            return sym, chg_7d, atr_pct_val

    pre_stats = await asyncio.gather(*[_check_mom_vol(s) for s in by_vol])
    by_momentum = [s for s, chg7, _ in sorted(pre_stats, key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True) if 4 < chg7 < 65][:15]
    by_volatility = [s for s, _, atrp in sorted(pre_stats, key=lambda x: x[2], reverse=True) if atrp > 2.2][:10]

    sources = await fetch_new_listings()
    try:
        rss = await fetch_listings_cache()
        now_ts = int(time.time())
        sources += [(l["symbol"], (now_ts - l["ts"]) / 3600) for l in rss]
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug(f"RSS listings unavailable: {e}")

    by_listings = []
    seen = set()
    for sym, age_h in sorted(sources, key=lambda x: x[1]):
        if sym in seen or not (24 <= age_h <= 336):
            continue
        seen.add(sym)
        if sym in tickers and is_tradable(sym) and tickers[sym]["quote_volume"] >= 650_000:
            by_listings.append((sym, age_h))
    by_listings = by_listings[:10]

    pool = list(dict.fromkeys(by_vol + by_chg + by_momentum + by_volatility + by_dip + [s for s, _ in by_listings]))
    pool_bases = list({s[:-4] for s in pool})

    sectors_map = await get_sectors_for_pool(pool_bases)
    ranks_map = await get_ranks_for_pool(pool_bases)
    await fetch_missing_names(pool_bases)

    news_items = await fetch_news_cache()

    btc_candles = await market_data.get_kline("BTCUSDT", "15", 120)

    raw_max = raw_max_score(regime)
    fng = get_fear_and_greed()

    if not paper.market_history or paper.market_history[-1].get("regime") != regime:
        paper.market_history.append({
            "ts": int(time.time()),
            "regime": regime,
            "mode": getattr(learner, "current_risk_mode", "NORMAL")
        })
        paper.market_history = paper.market_history[-500:]
        paper.save()

    sem = asyncio.Semaphore(10)

    async def _analyze_sym(sym):
        async with sem:
            candles_15m = await market_data.get_kline(sym, "15", 120)
            candles_1h = await market_data.get_kline(sym, "60", 250)

            if len(candles_15m) < 60 or len(candles_1h) < 60:
                return None

            is_post_pump, _ = is_post_pump_dump(candles_15m)
            if is_post_pump:
                return None

            a = atr(candles_15m)
            last_price = tickers[sym]["last"]
            atr_pct = (a / last_price) * 100 if last_price else 0
            if a <= 0 or atr_pct < 0.32:
                return None

            score, reasons, keys, signal_values = score_symbol(candles_15m, candles_1h, tickers[sym], regime)

            shadow.update_coin_dna(
                sym, candles_15m, candles_1h, atr_pct,
                signal_values.get("chg_48h", 0.0),
                signal_values.get("pos_in_48h", 0.5),
            )
            dna_info = shadow.get_coin_dna(sym)

            if dna_info["wave_amp_pct"] < 2.20 and atr_pct < 0.55 and signal_values.get("chg_48h", 0.0) < 5.0:
                return None

            gate_1h = signal_values.get("gate_1h", "1H_BAN")
            if gate_1h == "1H_BAN":
                return None

            if not signal_values.get("has_structure"):
                return None

            modes = signal_values.get("matched_modes", [])
            pats_now = signal_values.get("patterns", [])
            if gate_1h == "1H_SUPPORT_ONLY":
                if "reversal" not in modes:
                    return None
                entry_mode = "reversal"
            elif gate_1h == "1H_DIP_BOUNCE":
                entry_mode = "sniper"
            elif gate_1h == "1H_SUPER_TREND":
                entry_mode = "rocket" if "breakout" in pats_now else "sniper"
            else:
                if "rocket" in modes and ("breakout" in pats_now or signal_values.get("pos_in_48h", 0.5) > 0.72):
                    entry_mode = "rocket"
                elif "reversal" in modes and signal_values.get("pos_in_48h", 0.5) <= 0.38:
                    entry_mode = "reversal"
                elif "rocket" in modes:
                    entry_mode = "rocket"
                else:
                    entry_mode = "sniper"

            base = sym[:-4]
            sector = sectors_map.get(base, "Other")
            tier = tier_of(ranks_map.get(base))

            if sector == "Other" and tier == "MICRO" and tickers[sym]["quote_volume"] < 1_500_000:
                return None

            tactical_hold = False
            if candles_15m and len(candles_15m) >= 50:
                c15_closes = [c["close"] for c in candles_15m]
                e12_15 = ema(c15_closes, 12)[-1]
                e21_series_15 = ema(c15_closes, 21)
                e50_series_15 = ema(c15_closes, 50)
                e21_15 = e21_series_15[-1]
                e50_15 = e50_series_15[-1]
                closed_15 = candles_15m[-2]
                curr_15 = candles_15m[-1]

                if entry_mode in ("sniper", "rocket"):
                    if gate_1h != "1H_DIP_BOUNCE":
                        if e21_15 < e50_15 * 0.9985 or e50_15 < e50_series_15[-4] * 0.999:
                            tactical_hold = True
                    local_5_high = max(c["high"] for c in candles_15m[-5:])
                    if (local_5_high - last_price) > 1.5 * a and last_price < e21_15 and gate_1h != "1H_DIP_BOUNCE":
                        tactical_hold = True
                    if curr_15["close"] < curr_15["open"] and (curr_15["open"] - curr_15["close"]) > 0.42 * a:
                        tactical_hold = True
                elif entry_mode == "reversal":
                    if signal_values.get("pos_in_48h", 0.5) > 0.40:
                        tactical_hold = True
                    if curr_15["close"] < curr_15["open"] and (curr_15["open"] - curr_15["close"]) > 0.35 * a:
                        tactical_hold = True
                    if last_price < e12_15 * 0.9980:
                        tactical_hold = True
                    nsup = signal_values.get("nearest_sup")
                    if nsup and last_price < nsup * 0.996:
                        tactical_hold = True

            if regime == "bear":
                if entry_mode == "rocket":
                    if "breakout" not in pats_now or last_price < signal_values.get("e21_1h", 0) * 0.999 or signal_values.get("pos_in_48h", 1.0) > 0.85:
                        tactical_hold = True
                elif entry_mode == "sniper" and gate_1h != "1H_DIP_BOUNCE":
                    if last_price < signal_values.get("e21_1h", 0) * 0.998 or signal_values.get("pos_in_48h", 1.0) > 0.82:
                        tactical_hold = True

            if signal_values.get("recent_rejection") and entry_mode in ("sniper", "rocket"):
                tactical_hold = True

            corr = _corr_candles(candles_15m, btc_candles)
            tier_corr_limit = TIER_CORR_LIMITS.get(tier, 0.86)

            is_independent_mover = abs(corr) < 0.25 and gate_1h in ("1H_TREND_OK", "1H_SUPER_TREND", "1H_DIP_BOUNCE")

            if corr > tier_corr_limit and regime in ("neutral", "bear") and entry_mode != "reversal":
                score -= 0.6
                reasons.append(f"высокая связка с BTC ({tier}: corr {corr:.2f})")
            elif is_independent_mover:
                score += learner.weight("indep") * 1.15
                reasons.append(f"иммунитет от BTC (corr {corr:.2f})")
                keys.append("indep")
            elif corr < 0.45:
                score += learner.weight("indep")
                reasons.append(f"независима от BTC (corr {corr:.2f})")
                keys.append("indep")

            if fng >= 80:
                score -= 0.5
                reasons.append(f"F&G перегрев ({fng}): -0.5")
            elif fng <= 25 and entry_mode == "reversal":
                score += 0.3
                reasons.append(f"F&G страх ({fng}): +0.3")

            sb = learner.sector_bias(sector)
            if sb:
                score += sb
                reasons.append(f"сектор {sector}: {sb:+.2f}")

            if is_sector_hot(sector):
                score += 0.4
                reasons.append("TVL приток (DefiLlama): +0.4")

            tb = learner.tier_bias(tier)
            if tb:
                score += tb
                reasons.append(f"тир {TIER_EMOJI[tier]}: {tb:+.2f}")

            cb = learner.coin_bias(sym)
            if cb:
                score += cb
                reasons.append(f"доверие к монете: {cb:+.2f}")

            name = await get_coin_name(base)
            neg, pos_news, mentions, _ = check_sentiment(news_items, [base, name])

            is_toxic = neg >= 2 and neg >= (pos_news * 2) and neg >= (mentions * 0.33)
            if is_toxic:
                FILTERED_BY_NEWS[:] = [item for item in FILTERED_BY_NEWS if item["symbol"] != sym]
                FILTERED_BY_NEWS.append({"symbol": sym, "neg_count": f"{neg}/{mentions}", "time": int(time.time())})
                FILTERED_BY_NEWS[:] = FILTERED_BY_NEWS[-10:]
                return None

            if pos_news > neg:
                score += learner.weight("news_pos")
                reasons.append(f"позитивный новостной фон ({pos_news})")
                keys.append("news_pos")
            elif mentions >= 2:
                score += learner.weight("hype")
                reasons.append(f"медиа-хайп ({mentions} упом.)")
                keys.append("hype")

            score10 = (score / raw_max * SCORE_MAX) if raw_max > 0 else 0.0

            deriv_t = deriv_tickers.get(sym)
            if deriv_t:
                funding = deriv_t.get("funding", 0)
                if funding > 0.05 and entry_mode == "rocket":
                    score10 -= 0.5
                    reasons.append("перегрев лонгов Фьюч (-0.5)")
                elif funding < -0.01:
                    score10 += 0.5
                    reasons.append("шорт-сквиз потенциал Фьюч (+0.5)")

            is_sqz = await check_coinglass_liquidation_threat(sym)
            if is_sqz:
                score10 -= 1.5
                reasons.append("риск ликвидаций Coinglass (-1.5)")

            score10 = round(max(0.0, min(SCORE_MAX, score10)), 2)

            return {
                "symbol": sym, "score": score10, "reasons": reasons,
                "reason_keys": keys, "atr": a, "last": last_price,
                "liquidity": tickers[sym]["quote_volume"],
                "corr": round(corr, 2), "atr_pct": round(atr_pct, 2),
                "sector": sector, "tier": tier,
                "signal_values": signal_values,
                "patterns": signal_values.get("patterns", []),
                "is_momentum": (entry_mode == "rocket"),
                "entry_mode": entry_mode,
                "nearest_res": signal_values.get("nearest_res"),
                "res_touches": signal_values.get("res_touches", 0),
                "nearest_sup": signal_values.get("nearest_sup"),
                "sup_touches": signal_values.get("sup_touches", 0),
                "recent_high": signal_values.get("recent_high"),
                "recent_low_2h": signal_values.get("recent_low_2h"),
                "recent_low_6": signal_values.get("recent_low_6"),
                "e21": signal_values.get("e21"),
                "e50": signal_values.get("e50"),
                "e21_1h": signal_values.get("e21_1h"),
                "e50_1h": signal_values.get("e50_1h"),
                "gate_1h": gate_1h,
                "reason_1h": signal_values.get("reason_1h", ""),
                "chg_48h": signal_values.get("chg_48h", 0.0),
                "pos_in_48h": signal_values.get("pos_in_48h", 0.5),
                "wick_entry_price": signal_values.get("wick_entry_price"),
                "is_independent": is_independent_mover,
                "wave_class": dna_info["wave_class"],
                "wave_amp_pct": dna_info["wave_amp_pct"],
                "tactical_hold": tactical_hold,
                "size_mult": 1.0,
            }

    tasks = [_analyze_sym(sym) for sym in pool]
    results = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)

    all_structural = [r for r in results if r is not None]
    all_structural.sort(key=lambda c: (0 if c.get("tactical_hold") else 1, c["score"]), reverse=True)

    scored = [r for r in all_structural if not r.get("tactical_hold")]

    thrs = get_thresholds(regime)

    try:
        shadow.observe(all_structural, regime, thrs)
        shadow.tick(tickers)
        shadow.autotune()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"shadow error: {e}")

    public_url = os.getenv("RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL", "https://captain-rost-bot.onrender.com")
    parts_html, parts_plain = [], []
    for c in all_structural[:5]:
        chart_url = f"{public_url}/chart?symbol={c['symbol']}"
        mode = c.get("entry_mode", "sniper")
        thr = thrs.get(mode, 6.0)
        mode_icon = "🚀" if mode == "rocket" else "🧲" if mode == "reversal" else "🏹"

        parts_html.append(f"{mode_icon} <a href='{chart_url}'><b>{c['symbol'][:-4]}</b></a> {TIER_EMOJI.get(c['tier'], '🐭')} · <i>{c['sector']}</i> · {c['score']:.1f}/{thr:g} · ₿ {c['corr']:.2f}")
        parts_plain.append(f"{c['symbol']} {c['score']:.1f}/{thr:g} [{'+'.join(c['reason_keys'][:5])}] btc{c['corr']:.2f}")

    SCAN_SUMMARY["text"] = " | ".join(parts_html) or "сигналов нет"
    SCAN_SUMMARY["thrs"] = thrs
    SCAN_SUMMARY["ts"] = time.time()
    logger.info(f"Scan top: {' | '.join(parts_plain) or 'сигналов нет'}")

    new_set = set(s for s, _ in by_listings)
    candidates = []
    for c in scored:
        mode = c.get("entry_mode", "sniper")
        thr = thrs.get(mode, 6.0)
        if c["score"] < thr:
            continue
        c["thr"] = thr
        c["is_new"] = c["symbol"] in new_set
        candidates.append(c)

    return candidates[:limit]

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/strategy/scanner.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/strategy/shadow.py ---
import os
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path

from loguru import logger
from bot.core.remote_state import download_state, upload_state
from bot.strategy.learner import learner, clamp_weight

STATE_FILE = Path(os.getenv("STORAGE_DIR", "storage")) / "shadow.json"
REMOTE_PATH = "shadow.json"

OBS_GAP = 2.0
H1 = 3600
H4 = 4 * 3600
H24 = 24 * 3600
PUMP_PCT = 3.0
MAX_EPISODES = 300
COOLDOWN = 24 * 3600


def _band(score):
    if score < 5:
        return "0-5"
    if score < 6:
        return "5-6"
    if score < 7:
        return "6-7"
    if score < 8:
        return "7-8"
    return "8+"


class Shadow:
    """Теневой журнал + Память ДНК Монет (Coin DNA): калибрует пороги, глубину отката и цели под рынок."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.episodes = {}
        self.agg = {}
        self.cooldown = {}
        self.coin_dna = {}
        self.tuning = {
            "auto": True,
            "nudge_rocket": 0.0, "nudge_sniper": 0.0, "nudge_reversal": 0.0,
            "hunt": -0.0035, "near": -0.0020, "capture": +0.002,
            "sl_mult": 1.0, "tp_mult": 1.0,
            "signal_windows": {"rsi_hi": 90, "chg_hi": 30, "vol_lo": 1.3},
        }
        self._last_upload = 0.0
        self._last_tune = 0.0
        self._load()

    def _load(self):
        data = None
        try:
            if STATE_FILE.exists():
                data = json.loads(STATE_FILE.read_text())
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"shadow load error: {e}")
        if data is None:
            data = download_state(REMOTE_PATH)
        if data:
            self.episodes = data.get("episodes", {})
            self.agg = data.get("agg", {})
            self.cooldown = data.get("cooldown", {})
            self.coin_dna = data.get("coin_dna", {})
            self.tuning.update(data.get("tuning", {}))
            h_val = float(self.tuning.get("hunt", -0.0035))
            if h_val < -0.0055 or h_val > -0.0028:
                self.tuning["hunt"] = -0.0035
            logger.info(
                f"shadow: загружен ({len(self.episodes)} эпиз., ДНК {len(self.coin_dna)} монет, hunt={self.tuning['hunt']})"
            )

    def save(self):
        if len(self.coin_dna) > 400:
            sorted_dna = sorted(self.coin_dna.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1].get("ts", 0), reverse=True)[:350]
            self.coin_dna = dict(sorted_dna)

        payload = {
            "episodes": self.episodes,
            "agg": self.agg,
            "cooldown": self.cooldown,
            "coin_dna": self.coin_dna,
            "tuning": self.tuning,
        }
        try:
            STATE_FILE.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            STATE_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False))
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"shadow save error: {e}")
        if time.time() - self._last_upload > 3600:
            self._last_upload = time.time()
            upload_state(REMOTE_PATH, payload)

    def update_coin_dna(self, symbol: str, candles_15m: list, candles_1h: list, atr_pct: float, chg_48h: float, pos_in_48h: float):
        """Обновляет профиль волатильности, средней длины волны и глубины откатов монеты."""
        if not candles_1h or len(candles_1h) < 24:
            return

        recent_1h = candles_1h[-48:] if len(candles_1h) >= 48 else candles_1h
        swings = []
        chunk_size = 6
        for i in range(0, len(recent_1h) - 2, chunk_size):
            slc = recent_1h[i:i + chunk_size]
            lo = min(c["low"] for c in slc)
            hi = max(c["high"] for c in slc)
            if lo > 0:
                swings.append((hi - lo) / lo * 100.0)

        raw_wave_amp = (sum(swings) / len(swings)) if swings else max(2.2, atr_pct * 2.8)

        pullbacks = []
        if candles_15m and len(candles_15m) >= 30:
            for i in range(4, len(candles_15m) - 1, 4):
                w = candles_15m[i - 4:i + 1]
                hi_15 = max(c["high"] for c in w)
                lo_15 = min(c["low"] for c in w)
                if hi_15 > 0:
                    pullbacks.append((hi_15 - lo_15) / hi_15 * 100.0)
        avg_pullback = (sum(pullbacks) / len(pullbacks)) if pullbacks else max(0.5, atr_pct * 0.85)

        prev = self.coin_dna.get(symbol, {})
        hist_mfe = prev.get("hist_mfe", raw_wave_amp)
        prev_wave = prev.get("wave_amp_pct", raw_wave_amp)
        blended_wave = round(0.55 * raw_wave_amp + 0.25 * prev_wave + 0.20 * hist_mfe, 2)
        blended_atr = round(0.6 * atr_pct + 0.4 * prev.get("atr_pct", atr_pct), 3)

        if chg_48h >= 3.5 and pos_in_48h >= 0.45:
            med_trend = "bull"
        elif chg_48h <= -3.5 and pos_in_48h <= 0.45:
            med_trend = "bear"
        else:
            med_trend = "range"

        self.coin_dna[symbol] = {
            "wave_amp_pct": max(1.4, min(16.0, blended_wave)),
            "atr_pct": max(0.2, min(6.0, blended_atr)),
            "pullback_pct": round(max(0.28, min(3.2, avg_pullback)), 2),
            "chg_48h": round(chg_48h, 2),
            "pos_in_48h": round(pos_in_48h, 2),
            "med_trend": med_trend,
            "hist_mfe": round(hist_mfe, 2),
            "ts": int(time.time()),
        }

    def get_coin_dna(self, symbol: str) -> dict:
        dna = self.coin_dna.get(symbol) or {}
        wave_amp = float(dna.get("wave_amp_pct", 3.0))
        med_trend = dna.get("med_trend", "range")
        atr_pct = float(dna.get("atr_pct", 0.8))

        if wave_amp >= 4.0 or (wave_amp >= 3.3 and med_trend == "bull"):
            wave_class = "long_wave"
            tp1_ratio = 0.32
            wave_mult = 1.20
        elif wave_amp < 2.50 and med_trend != "bull":
            wave_class = "short_wave"
            tp1_ratio = 0.55
            wave_mult = 0.80
        else:
            wave_class = "mid_wave"
            tp1_ratio = 0.40
            wave_mult = 1.0

        return {
            "wave_amp_pct": wave_amp,
            "atr_pct": atr_pct,
            "pullback_pct": float(dna.get("pullback_pct", 0.75)),
            "med_trend": med_trend,
            "wave_class": wave_class,
            "tp1_ratio": tp1_ratio,
            "wave_mult": wave_mult,
        }

    def observe(self, scored, regime, thrs):
        from bot.exchange.paper_exchange import paper
        now = time.time()

        for c in scored:
            sym = c["symbol"]
            mode = c.get("entry_mode", "sniper")
            thr = thrs.get(mode, 6.0)
            obs_thr = thr - OBS_GAP

            if sym in self.episodes:
                ep = self.episodes[sym]
                if c["score"] > ep["max_score"]:
                    ep["max_score"] = c["score"]
                if sym in paper.positions:
                    ep["traded"] = True
                continue

            if c["score"] < obs_thr:
                continue
            if now < self.cooldown.get(sym, 0):
                continue
            if len(self.episodes) >= MAX_EPISODES:
                continue

            traded = (sym in paper.positions)

            self.episodes[sym] = {
                "ts": now, "price": c["last"], "last": c["last"],
                "max_score": c["score"],
                "regime": regime, "entry_mode": mode,
                "sector": c.get("sector"), "tier": c.get("tier"),
                "keys": c.get("reason_keys", []), "traded": bool(traded),
                "success": False,
                "hi": c["last"], "lo": c["last"], "p4": None,
                "lo_before_pump": c["last"], "pumped": False,
                "signal_values": c.get("signal_values", {}),
            }

    def mark_success(self, sym):
        if sym in self.episodes:
            self.episodes[sym]["success"] = True
            self.episodes[sym]["traded"] = True
            self.save()

    def tick(self, tickers):
        from bot.exchange.paper_exchange import paper
        now = time.time()
        for sym, ep in list(self.episodes.items()):
            t = tickers.get(sym)
            if not t:
                continue
            last = t["last"]
            ep["last"] = last
            ep["hi"] = max(ep["hi"], last)
            ep["lo"] = min(ep["lo"], last)
            if sym in paper.positions:
                ep["traded"] = True

            if not ep.get("pumped"):
                ep["lo_before_pump"] = min(ep.get("lo_before_pump", last), last)
                if last >= ep["price"] * (1 + PUMP_PCT / 100):
                    ep["pumped"] = True

            age = now - ep["ts"]
            if ep["p4"] is None and age >= H4:
                ep["p4"] = last
            if age >= H24:
                self._close(sym, ep, last)
        self.save()

    def _combined_agg(self) -> dict:
        merged = {}
        for k, v in self.agg.items():
            merged[k] = {
                "n": v.get("n", 0),
                "sum4": v.get("sum4", 0.0),
                "sum24": v.get("sum24", 0.0),
                "pumps4": v.get("pumps4", 0),
                "pumps24": v.get("pumps24", 0),
                "traded": v.get("traded", 0),
                "sum_mae": v.get("sum_mae", 0.0),
                "sum_mfe": v.get("sum_mfe", 0.0),
                "sum_pull": v.get("sum_pull", 0.0),
                "sum_pull_pump": v.get("sum_pull_pump", 0.0),
                "sum_mae_pump": v.get("sum_mae_pump", 0.0),
            }

        now = time.time()
        for sym, ep in self.episodes.items():
            age = now - ep.get("ts", now)
            dec = ep.get("price", 0.0)
            if age < H1 or dec <= 0:
                continue
            ref_last = ep.get("p4") or ep.get("last") or dec
            move_cur = (ref_last - dec) / dec * 100
            mae = (ep.get("lo", dec) - dec) / dec * 100
            mfe = (ep.get("hi", dec) - dec) / dec * 100
            pullback = (ep.get("lo_before_pump", ep.get("lo", dec)) - dec) / dec * 100
            did_pump = bool(ep.get("pumped") or mfe >= PUMP_PCT or move_cur >= PUMP_PCT)

            mode = ep.get("entry_mode", "sniper")
            key = f"{ep.get('regime', 'neutral')}|{mode}|{_band(ep.get('max_score', 6.0))}"
            a = merged.setdefault(key, {
                "n": 0, "sum4": 0.0, "sum24": 0.0, "pumps4": 0, "pumps24": 0,
                "traded": 0, "sum_mae": 0.0, "sum_mfe": 0.0, "sum_pull": 0.0,
                "sum_pull_pump": 0.0, "sum_mae_pump": 0.0,
            })
            a["n"] += 1
            a["sum4"] += move_cur
            a["sum24"] += move_cur
            a["sum_mae"] += mae
            a["sum_mfe"] += mfe
            a["sum_pull"] += mae
            if did_pump:
                a["pumps24"] += 1
                a["sum_pull_pump"] += pullback
                a["sum_mae_pump"] += mae
            if ep.get("success") or (ep.get("traded") and did_pump):
                a["traded"] += 1

        return merged

    def _close(self, sym, ep, last):
        dec = ep["price"]
        self.episodes.pop(sym, None)
        self.cooldown[sym] = time.time() + COOLDOWN
        if dec <= 0:
            return

        move24 = (last - dec) / dec * 100
        move4 = ((ep["p4"] or last) - dec) / dec * 100
        mae = (ep["lo"] - dec) / dec * 100
        mfe = (ep["hi"] - dec) / dec * 100
        pullback = (ep.get("lo_before_pump", ep["lo"]) - dec) / dec * 100

        if sym in self.coin_dna and mfe > 0.5:
            old_mfe = self.coin_dna[sym].get("hist_mfe", mfe)
            self.coin_dna[sym]["hist_mfe"] = round(0.7 * old_mfe + 0.3 * mfe, 2)

        mode = ep.get("entry_mode", "sniper")
        key = f"{ep['regime']}|{mode}|{_band(ep['max_score'])}"

        a = self.agg.setdefault(key, {
            "n": 0, "sum4": 0.0, "sum24": 0.0, "pumps4": 0, "pumps24": 0,
            "traded": 0, "sum_mae": 0.0, "sum_mfe": 0.0, "sum_pull": 0.0,
            "sum_pull_pump": 0.0, "sum_mae_pump": 0.0,
            "keys_pump": {}, "keys_all": {},
        })
        a["n"] += 1
        a["sum4"] += move4
        a["sum24"] += move24
        a["sum_mae"] += mae
        a["sum_mfe"] += mfe
        a["sum_pull"] += mae

        if move4 >= PUMP_PCT:
            a["pumps4"] += 1

        did_pump = bool(ep.get("pumped") or mfe >= PUMP_PCT or move24 >= PUMP_PCT)
        if did_pump:
            a["pumps24"] += 1
            a["sum_pull_pump"] = a.get("sum_pull_pump", 0.0) + pullback
            a["sum_mae_pump"] = a.get("sum_mae_pump", 0.0) + mae

            for k in ep["keys"]:
                a["keys_pump"][k] = a["keys_pump"].get(k, 0) + 1
            sv = ep.get("signal_values", {})
            for k, v in sv.items():
                if isinstance(v, (int, float)):
                    a.setdefault("signal_values_pump", {}).setdefault(k, []).append(v)

        for k in ep["keys"]:
            a["keys_all"][k] = a["keys_all"].get(k, 0) + 1
