--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/workers/notification.py ---
import asyncio
from loguru import logger
from bot.core.event_bus import EventBus

class NotificationWorker:
    """Асинхронная очередь уведомлений Telegram с защитой от превышения лимитов."""
    
    def __init__(self, bus: EventBus, send_chat_func):
        self.bus = bus
        self.send_chat = send_chat_func
        self.queue = self.bus.subscribe("NOTIFY", maxsize=200)
        
        # Лимит одного сообщения в Telegram - 4096 символов. 
        # Берем 3800 с запасом на HTML-теги и форматирование.
        self.MAX_MSG_LENGTH = 3800

    async def run(self):
        logger.info("📬 Notification Worker запущен: умный батчинг сообщений")
        buffer = []
        current_length = 0
        
        while True:
            try:
                # Ждем сообщение с таймаутом (2 сек), чтобы периодически сбрасывать буфер
                try:
                    event = await asyncio.wait_for(self.queue.get(), timeout=2.0)
                    payload = event.payload
                    
                    text = payload.get("text", "")
                    urgent = payload.get("urgent", False)
                    
                    if urgent:
                        # Экстренные сообщения (паника/дамп) отправляем немедленно
                        await self._send_chunks(text)
                    else:
                        text_len = len(text) + 2  # +2 для разделителя \n\n
                        
                        # Если буфер переполняется - отправляем то, что накопили
                        if current_length + text_len > self.MAX_MSG_LENGTH:
                            await self._flush(buffer)
                            buffer = []
                            current_length = 0
                            
                        buffer.append(text)
                        current_length += text_len
                        
                        # Батчинг по 5 сообщений для снижения количества запросов к API
                        if len(buffer) >= 5:
                            await self._flush(buffer)
                            buffer = []
                            current_length = 0
                            
                    self.queue.task_done()
                except asyncio.TimeoutError:
                    # Раз в 2 секунды сбрасываем всё, что зависло в буфере
                    if buffer:
                        await self._flush(buffer)
                        buffer = []
                        current_length = 0
                        
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"NotificationWorker error: {e}")

    async def _flush(self, buffer: list):
        """Склеивает и отправляет накопившийся буфер."""
        if not buffer:
            return
        msg = "\n\n".join(buffer)
        await self._send_chunks(msg)

    async def _send_chunks(self, text: str):
        """Безопасная отправка длинных сообщений с разбивкой."""
        while len(text) > 0:
            chunk = text[:self.MAX_MSG_LENGTH]
            
            if len(text) > self.MAX_MSG_LENGTH:
                # Ищем последний перенос строки, чтобы не рвать слова и HTML-теги пополам
                last_newline = chunk.rfind('\n')
                if last_newline > 0:
                    chunk = chunk[:last_newline]
            
            try:
                await self.send_chat(chunk)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Telegram send chunk error: {e}")
                
            text = text[len(chunk):].lstrip()
            # Защита от Flood Limit'ов Telegram API (не более 20 сообщений в минуту в 1 чат)
            await asyncio.sleep(0.2)

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/workers/notification.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/workers/order_manager.py ---
import time
import asyncio
from collections import defaultdict
from loguru import logger

from bot.core.event_bus import EventBus
from bot.exchange.paper_exchange import paper
from bot.strategy.sizing import (
    buy_size, entry_offset, min_order_threshold,
    MAX_POSITIONS_PER_TIER, MAX_ORDERS_PER_TIER
)
from bot.strategy.scanner import get_thresholds
from bot.strategy.learner import learner
from bot.strategy.risk import (
    calc_initial_sl_tp, get_trade_policy,
    evaluate_setup_grade, calc_total_open_risk_pct
)
from bot.news.cmc import get_coin_name
from bot.news.rss_news import fetch_news_cache, check_sentiment
from bot.utils.format import pair_html, corr_txt, usd, fmt_price, fmt_pct, pnl_emoji, grade_badge
from bot.core.state import bot_state


class OrderManagerWorker:
    """Управляет портфелем: Умный Овербукинг, безопасная ротация спящих слотов и защита от покупки шпилек."""

    def __init__(self, bus: EventBus):
        self.bus = bus
        self.signal_queue = self.bus.subscribe("SIGNALS_READY", maxsize=2)

    async def run(self):
        logger.info("💼 Order Manager запущен: Умный Овербукинг, выверенные точки входа и защита от суеты...")
        while True:
            try:
                event = await self.signal_queue.get()
                await self._process_signals(event.payload)
                self.signal_queue.task_done()
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                logger.exception(f"OrderManager error: {e}")

    def _notify(self, text: str, urgent: bool = False):
        self.bus.publish("NOTIFY", {"text": text, "urgent": urgent})

    def _effective_orders_for_risk(self) -> list:
        if not paper.orders:
            return []
        by_tier = defaultdict(list)
        for o in paper.orders:
            by_tier[o.get("tier") or "SMALL"].append(o)

        effective = []
        for t, ords in by_tier.items():
            avail_slots = max(0, MAX_POSITIONS_PER_TIER - paper.count_tier_positions(t))
            if avail_slots > 0:
                ords_sorted = sorted(
                    ords,
                    key=lambda x: (1 if x.get("grade") == "A+" else 0, float(x.get("score", 0.0))),
                    reverse=True,
                )
                effective.extend(ords_sorted[:avail_slots])
        return effective

    def _try_evict_sleeping_position(self, cand: dict, tier: str, tickers: dict, regime: str) -> bool:
        """
        Вызывается ТОЛЬКО тогда, когда новая ракета (score >= 9.2) УЖЕ прошла все проверки стен и RR!
        Не трогает свежие донные позиции (reversal / 1H_DIP_BOUNCE), чтобы не выбрасывать сделки на дне (фикс INJUSDT).
        """
        if cand.get("score", 0.0) < 9.2 or cand.get("entry_mode") not in ("sniper", "rocket"):
            return False

        now_ts = int(time.time())
        sleeping_candidates = []

        for sym, pos in list(paper.positions.items()):
            if (pos.get("tier") or "SMALL") != tier:
                continue
            if pos.get("tp1_done"):
                continue
            pos_age = now_ts - int(pos.get("entry_time", now_ts))
            min_sleep_age = 9000 if (pos.get("entry_mode") == "reversal" or pos.get("gate_1h") == "1H_DIP_BOUNCE") else 6300
            if pos_age < min_sleep_age:
                continue
            last_p = tickers.get(sym, {}).get("last", 0.0)
            avg_p = pos.get("avg", 0.0)
            if last_p <= 0 or avg_p <= 0:
                continue
            pnl_pct = (last_p - avg_p) / avg_p * 100.0
            if -0.05 <= pnl_pct <= 0.35 and (cand["score"] - float(pos.get("score", 6.0))) >= 1.6:
                sleeping_candidates.append((pos_age, sym, pos, last_p))

        if not sleeping_candidates:
            return False

        sleeping_candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)
        _, v_sym, v_pos, v_last = sleeping_candidates[0]
        reason = f"🔄 Ротация слота под ракету {cand['symbol'][:-4]} ({cand['score']:.1f})"
        ex = paper._sell(v_sym, v_last, "🛡🔒 БУ", regime_now=regime)
        paper.log_event(v_sym, "sell_profit", v_last, reason, tp=v_pos.get("tp"), sl=v_pos.get("sl"), sup=v_pos.get("nearest_sup"), res=v_pos.get("nearest_res"))
        bot_state.set_cooldown(v_sym, 600, structural_reset=False, reason="slot_evict")
        logger.info(f"OrderManager: 🔄 Освобождён спящий слот {v_sym} ({ex['pnl_pct']:+.2f}%) ради валидной ракеты {cand['symbol']} ({cand['score']:.1f})")
        self._notify(
            f"🔄 <b>Освобождён спящий слот</b> · продан {pair_html(v_sym, ex)}{grade_badge(ex)} ({pnl_emoji(ex['pnl_pct'])} {fmt_pct(ex['pnl_pct'])})\n"
            f"➡️ под ракету <b>{cand['symbol'][:-4]}</b> (⭐ {cand['score']:.1f})"
        )
        return True

    def _try_rotate_order(self, cand: dict, cand_grade: str, tickers: dict, reason_tag: str = "освобождение USDT", target_tier: str = None) -> bool:
        if not paper.orders:
            return False

        now_ts = int(time.time())
        cand_score = cand.get("score", 0.0)
        eligible = []

        for o in paper.orders:
            if target_tier and (o.get("tier") or "SMALL") != target_tier:
                continue

            o_age = now_ts - o.get("created", now_ts)
            o_grade = o.get("grade", "B")
            o_score = o.get("score", 0.0)

            # Запрещаем дёргать ордера младше 15 минут (900 сек) — убираем пустую суету без сделок (фикс TAUSDT, ARXUSDT)!
            if o_age < 900:
                continue

            last_p = tickers.get(o["symbol"], {}).get("last", 0.0)
            if last_p > 0 and abs(last_p - o["price"]) / o["price"] * 100 <= 0.45:
                continue

            score_diff = cand_score - o_score
            is_upgrade = (
                (score_diff >= 1.4) or
                (cand_grade == "A+" and o_grade != "A+" and score_diff >= 0.8)
            )
            if is_upgrade:
                priority_key = (1 if o_grade == "A+" else 0, o_score, -o_age)
                eligible.append((priority_key, o))

        if not eligible:
            return False

        eligible.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
        victim = eligible[0][1]
        v_sym = victim["symbol"]
        v_last = tickers.get(v_sym, {}).get("last", victim["price"])

        paper.cancel_order(victim["id"])
        paper.log_event(
            v_sym, "cancel", v_last,
            f"Ротация ({reason_tag}) -> {cand['symbol'][:-4]} ({victim.get('score', 0):.1f} -> {cand_score:.1f})",
            tp=victim.get("tp"), sl=victim.get("sl"),
            sup=victim.get("nearest_sup"), res=victim.get("nearest_res")
        )
        logger.info(
            f"OrderManager: 🔄 РОТАЦИЯ ОРДЕРА ({reason_tag}): снят {v_sym} "
            f"(score {victim.get('score', 0):.1f}{grade_badge(victim)}) ради {cand['symbol']} "
            f"(score {cand_score:.1f}{' [💎]' if cand_grade == 'A+' else ''})"
        )
        self._notify(
            f"🔄 <b>Ротация ({reason_tag})</b> · снят {pair_html(v_sym, victim)}{grade_badge(victim)} ({victim.get('score', 0):.1f}) "
            f"➡️ под <b>{cand['symbol'][:-4]}</b>{' [💎]' if cand_grade == 'A+' else ''} ({cand_score:.1f})"
        )
        return True

    async def _process_signals(self, payload: dict):
        candidates = payload["candidates"]
        tickers = payload["tickers"]
        regime = payload["regime"]

        equity = paper.equity(tickers)
        thrs = get_thresholds(regime)
        news_items = await fetch_news_cache()

        risk_mode = getattr(learner, "current_risk_mode", "NORMAL")
        policy = get_trade_policy(risk_mode)
        min_usdt_order = min_order_threshold(equity)

        eff_risk_now = calc_total_open_risk_pct(paper.positions, self._effective_orders_for_risk(), equity)
        logger.info(
            f"💼 OrderManager [{risk_mode}]: кандидатов {len(candidates)}, "
            f"эффективный открытый риск {eff_risk_now:.2f}% / {policy['max_portfolio_risk_pct']}%"
        )

        for cand in candidates:
            sym = cand["symbol"]
            base = sym[:-4]

            if sym in paper.positions or any(o["symbol"] == sym for o in paper.orders):
                continue

            sector = cand.get("sector", "Other")
            tier = cand.get("tier") or "SMALL"
            entry_mode = cand.get("entry_mode", "sniper")
            is_mom = cand.get("is_momentum", False)

            tier_pos_count = paper.count_tier_positions(tier)
            needs_eviction = False
            if tier_pos_count >= MAX_POSITIONS_PER_TIER:
                if cand.get("score", 0.0) >= 9.2 and entry_mode in ("sniper", "rocket"):
                    needs_eviction = True
                else:
                    logger.debug(f"OrderManager: {sym} пропущен -> в тире {tier} уже открыто {tier_pos_count}/{MAX_POSITIONS_PER_TIER} позиций")
                    continue

            cand_corr = float(cand.get("corr") or 0.0)
            if regime in ("bear", "neutral") and cand_corr >= 0.85 and cand.get("score", 0.0) < 9.0:
                max_corr_pos = 3 if regime == "bear" else 4
                high_corr_pos = sum(1 for p in paper.positions.values() if float(p.get("corr") or 0.0) >= 0.85)
                high_corr_ord = sum(1 for o in paper.orders if float(o.get("corr") or 0.0) >= 0.85)
                if high_corr_pos >= max_corr_pos or (high_corr_pos + high_corr_ord) >= (max_corr_pos + 1):
                    logger.info(f"OrderManager: {sym} пропущен -> лимит BTC-клонов ({high_corr_pos} поз., {high_corr_ord} орд., corr {cand_corr:.2f})")
                    continue

            patterns = cand.get("patterns", [])
            is_accum = "vcp" in patterns
            e21_15m = cand.get("e21", 0.0)
            e50_15m = cand.get("e50", 0.0)
            a = cand["atr"]
            if a <= 0:
                continue

            if learner.coin_bias(sym) > -1.0:
                bot_state.check_structural_reset(sym, cand["last"], e21_15m, e50_15m)
            if bot_state.is_on_cooldown(sym):
                continue

            if entry_mode == "reversal":
                can_rev, rev_reason = bot_state.can_enter_reversal(sym, cand["last"], e21_15m)
                if not can_rev:
                    logger.info(f"OrderManager: {sym} reversal заблокирован -> {rev_reason}")
                    continue
            elif entry_mode == "sniper":
                can_snp, snp_reason = bot_state.can_enter_sniper(
                    sym, cand["last"], patterns,
                    ema21_15m=e21_15m, ema50_15m=e50_15m, atr_15m=a,
                    score=cand.get("score", 0.0), gate_1h=cand.get("gate_1h", "")
                )
                if not can_snp:
                    logger.info(f"OrderManager: {sym} sniper заблокирован -> {snp_reason}")
                    continue

            grade = evaluate_setup_grade(cand)
            if policy["require_grade_a_for_alt"] and entry_mode in ("reversal", "rocket"):
                if grade != "A+":
                    logger.info(f"OrderManager: {sym} пропущен -> режим {risk_mode} требует [💎] A+ для {entry_mode}")
                    continue

            avail_tier_slots = max(1 if needs_eviction else 0, MAX_POSITIONS_PER_TIER - tier_pos_count)
            is_elite_cand = (grade == "A+" or cand["score"] >= 8.2)
            if avail_tier_slots == 1:
                tier_orders = [o for o in paper.orders if (o.get("tier") or "SMALL") == tier]
                has_elite_waiting = any(o.get("grade") == "A+" or float(o.get("score", 0.0)) >= 8.0 for o in tier_orders)
                if has_elite_waiting and not is_elite_cand:
                    logger.debug(f"OrderManager: {sym} [B] пропущен -> последний слот тира {tier} забронирован под [💎] A+")
                    continue

            name = await get_coin_name(base)
            neg, pos_news, mentions, _ = check_sentiment(news_items, [base, name])
            if neg >= 2 and neg >= (pos_news * 2) and neg >= (mentions * 0.33):
                logger.info(f"OrderManager: {sym} отклонен -> токсичные новости ({neg}/{mentions})")
                bot_state.set_cooldown(sym, 7200)
                continue

            thr = cand.get("thr") or thrs.get(entry_mode, 6.0)
            off = entry_offset(cand["score"], thr, regime, cand["atr_pct"], entry_mode, tier=tier, is_accumulation=is_accum, symbol=sym)

            t_data = tickers.get(sym, {})
            bid1 = t_data.get("bid1", cand["last"])
            ideal_entry = cand["last"] * (1 + off)
            entry = min(ideal_entry, bid1 * 0.9990)

            # ЗАЩИТА ОТ ВХОДА НА МИНУТНЫХ ШПИЛЬКАХ (фикс TAUSDT):
            # Для любого sniper (кроме старта со дна 1H_DIP_BOUNCE) лимитка ставится не выше 15m EMA21 + 0.30*ATR!
            if entry_mode == "sniper" and cand.get("gate_1h") != "1H_DIP_BOUNCE" and e21_15m > 0:
                ema_pullback_cap = e21_15m + 0.30 * a
                if ema_pullback_cap < entry and ema_pullback_cap >= cand["last"] * 0.986:
                    entry = ema_pullback_cap

            wick_entry = cand.get("wick_entry_price")
            if entry_mode == "reversal" and wick_entry and 0 < wick_entry < entry:
                entry = max(wick_entry, cand["last"] * 0.990)

            sup = cand.get("nearest_sup")
            sup_touches = cand.get("sup_touches", 0)
            if sup and sup_touches >= 2 and not is_mom:
                retest_floor = sup * 1.0012
                if entry < sup and retest_floor <= cand["last"] * 0.9980:
                    entry = retest_floor

            off = (entry - cand["last"]) / cand["last"] if cand["last"] > 0 else off

            all_res = cand.get("signal_values", {}).get("all_res", [])
            res_above_entry = []
            for r_item in all_res:
                r_val, r_tch = r_item if isinstance(r_item, (list, tuple)) else (r_item, 2)
                if r_val > entry * 1.0005:
                    res_above_entry.append((r_val, r_tch))

            if res_above_entry:
                res_above_entry.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
                entry_res, res_touches = res_above_entry[0]
            else:
                entry_res = cand.get("nearest_res")
                res_touches = cand.get("res_touches", 0)

            min_rr = policy["min_rr_sniper"] if entry_mode == "sniper" else policy["min_rr_alt"]

            sl, tp, is_valid, rej_reason = calc_initial_sl_tp(
                symbol=sym, entry=entry, tier=tier, a=a,
                sup=sup, sup_touches=sup_touches,
                res=entry_res, res_touches=res_touches,
                is_mom=is_mom, entry_mode=entry_mode,
                min_rr=min_rr,
                recent_low_6=cand.get("recent_low_6"),
                ema50=e50_15m,
                e21_1h=cand.get("e21_1h"),
                e50_1h=cand.get("e50_1h"),
            )

            if not is_valid:
                logger.info(f"OrderManager: {sym} отклонён -> {rej_reason}")
                continue

            sl_dist_pct = (entry - sl) / entry * 100
            if sl_dist_pct <= 0:
                continue

            # Только ТЕПЕРЬ, когда кандидат 100% валиден и прошёл проверку стен и RR, освобождаем под него спящий слот!
            if needs_eviction:
                if self._try_evict_sleeping_position(cand, tier, tickers, regime):
                    tier_pos_count = paper.count_tier_positions(tier)
                    avail_tier_slots = max(0, MAX_POSITIONS_PER_TIER - tier_pos_count)
                else:
                    continue

            max_tier_orders = 2 if avail_tier_slots == 1 else min(MAX_ORDERS_PER_TIER, avail_tier_slots + 2)
            tier_ord_count = paper.count_tier_orders(tier)
            if tier_ord_count >= max_tier_orders:
                if not self._try_rotate_order(cand, grade, tickers, reason_tag=f"буфер тира {tier}", target_tier=tier):
                    logger.debug(f"OrderManager: {sym} пропущен -> в тире {tier} уже {tier_ord_count}/{max_tier_orders} ордеров")
                    continue

            trade_risk_budget_pct = policy["risk_per_trade_core"] if entry_mode == "sniper" else policy["risk_per_trade_alt"]
            current_risk_pct = calc_total_open_risk_pct(paper.positions, self._effective_orders_for_risk(), equity)
            avail_risk_budget_pct = policy["max_portfolio_risk_pct"] - current_risk_pct

            if avail_risk_budget_pct <= 0.15:
                if self._try_rotate_order(cand, grade, tickers, reason_tag="лимит Portfolio VaR"):
                    current_risk_pct = calc_total_open_risk_pct(paper.positions, self._effective_orders_for_risk(), equity)
                    avail_risk_budget_pct = policy["max_portfolio_risk_pct"] - current_risk_pct

            if avail_risk_budget_pct <= 0.15:
                logger.debug(f"OrderManager: {sym} пропущен -> лимит риска исчерпан")
                continue

            effective_risk_pct = min(trade_risk_budget_pct, avail_risk_budget_pct)
            size_by_risk = (equity * (effective_risk_pct / 100.0)) / (sl_dist_pct / 100.0)

            locked_usdt = paper.effective_locked_usdt()
            free_usdt = max(0.0, paper.usdt - locked_usdt)

            if free_usdt < min_usdt_order:
                if self._try_rotate_order(cand, grade, tickers, reason_tag="нехватка свободного USDT"):
                    locked_usdt = paper.effective_locked_usdt()
                    free_usdt = max(0.0, paper.usdt - locked_usdt)

            size_base = buy_size(
                equity, cand["score"], thr, cand["liquidity"], free_usdt,
                entry_mode=entry_mode, grade=grade, symbol=sym
            )

            size = min(size_base, size_by_risk, free_usdt * 0.98, equity * 0.14)
            size = round(size, 2)

            if size < min_usdt_order:
                logger.debug(f"OrderManager: {sym} пропущен (свободно ${free_usdt:.2f} < мин. порога ${min_usdt_order:.2f})")
                continue

            qty = size / entry
            order = paper.place_limit_buy(sym, qty, entry, tp=tp, sl=sl, score=cand["score"], reason_keys=cand.get("reason_keys", []))

            order.update({
                "sector": sector, "tier": tier, "corr": cand.get("corr"),
                "regime": regime, "risk_mode": risk_mode,
                "thr": thr,
                "gate_1h": cand.get("gate_1h", "1H_TREND_OK"),
                "reason_1h": cand.get("reason_1h", ""),
                "chg_48h": cand.get("chg_48h", 0.0),
                "pos_in_48h": cand.get("pos_in_48h", 0.5),
                "reasons": cand.get("reasons", []),
                "is_momentum": is_mom, "entry_mode": entry_mode,
                "nearest_res": entry_res,
                "res_touches": res_touches,
                "nearest_sup": sup,
                "sup_touches": sup_touches,
                "sl_state": "SHIELD",
                "patterns": patterns,
                "grade": grade,
                "wave_class": cand.get("wave_class", "mid_wave"),
                "wave_amp_pct": cand.get("wave_amp_pct", 3.2),
                "initial_score": cand["score"],
                "last_move_ts": int(order["created"]),
                "_ema21": e21_15m,
                "_ema50": e50_15m,
                "_atr15": a,
                "e21_1h": cand.get("e21_1h"),
                "e50_1h": cand.get("e50_1h"),
            })
            paper.save()

            reasons_html = "<br>".join([f"• {r}" for r in cand.get("reasons", [])])
            paper.log_event(sym, "order_placed", entry, text=reasons_html, mode=entry_mode, tp=tp, sl=sl, sup=sup, res=entry_res)

            tp_pct = (tp - entry) / entry * 100
            sl_pct = (sl - entry) / entry * 100
            g_badge = " [💎]" if grade == "A+" else ""

            logger.info(
                f"OrderManager: 🎯 {sym} ОРДЕР {entry_mode}{g_badge}: entry=${fmt_price(entry)}, "
                f"TP=${fmt_price(tp)}, SL=${fmt_price(sl)} | 1H: {cand.get('reason_1h', '')}"
            )

            self._notify(
                f"📋 <b>Ордер</b> · {pair_html(sym, order)}{g_badge}\n"
                f"💵 {usd(size)} · 📥 {fmt_price(entry)} ({off * 100:+.2f}%){corr_txt(cand)}\n"
                f"🎯 {fmt_price(tp)} ({fmt_pct(tp_pct)}) · 🛡📉 {fmt_price(sl)} ({fmt_pct(sl_pct)})\n"
                f"⭐ {cand['score']:.1f}/{thr:g} · 🕐 {cand.get('reason_1h', '')}"
            )

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/workers/order_manager.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/workers/position_manager.py ---
import time
import asyncio
from loguru import logger

from bot.core.event_bus import EventBus
from bot.exchange.market_data import market_data
from bot.exchange.paper_exchange import paper
from bot.strategy.scanner import live_score, get_thresholds, _returns, is_post_pump_dump
from bot.strategy.indicators import atr, ema, find_sr_levels, get_all_sr_levels
from bot.strategy.learner import learner
from bot.strategy.shadow import shadow
from bot.strategy.risk import (
    calc_initial_sl_tp, calc_runner_target, update_position_sl,
    get_trade_policy, _round_px
)
from bot.news.cmc import get_coin_name
from bot.news.rss_news import fetch_news_cache, check_sentiment
from bot.strategy.sizing import entry_offset
from bot.utils.format import (
    pair_html, format_coin_compact, sl_emoji, grade_badge,
    usd, fmt_price, fmt_pct, pnl_emoji
)
from bot.core.state import bot_state


class PositionManagerWorker:
    """Управление позициями: частые ступеньки SL/TP на прокол, анти-зомби фильтр и ведение ордеров."""

    def __init__(self, bus: EventBus):
        self.bus = bus
        self.regime_queue = self.bus.subscribe("REGIME_UPDATED", maxsize=2)
        self.current_regime = "neutral"

    async def run(self):
        logger.info("🛡 Position Manager запущен: Ступенчатый Коридор SL/TP (на прокол) и ведение ордеров...")
        asyncio.create_task(self._regime_updater())

        while True:
            if bot_state.paused or not bot_state.trading_enabled:
                await asyncio.sleep(10)
                continue

            try:
                await self._maintenance_cycle()
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                logger.exception(f"PositionManager error: {e}")

            await asyncio.sleep(60)

    async def _regime_updater(self):
        while True:
            event = await self.regime_queue.get()
            self.current_regime = event.payload["regime"]
            self.regime_queue.task_done()

    def _notify(self, text: str, urgent: bool = False):
        self.bus.publish("NOTIFY", {"text": text, "urgent": urgent})

    async def _maintenance_cycle(self):
        tickers = await market_data.get_tickers()
        deriv_tickers = await market_data.get_derivatives_tickers()
        if not tickers:
            return

        btc_candles = await market_data.get_kline("BTCUSDT", "15", 120)
        btc_ret = _returns([c["close"] for c in btc_candles])

        m_24 = paper.get_metrics(tickers, hours=24)
        m_48 = paper.get_metrics(tickers, hours=48)
        pf_24 = m_24["profit_factor"] if m_24["profit_factor"] is not None else 1.0
        pf_48 = m_48["profit_factor"] if m_48["profit_factor"] is not None else pf_24
        if pf_24 == float("inf"):
            pf_24 = 2.2
        if pf_48 == float("inf"):
            pf_48 = 2.2
        blended_pf = (0.70 * pf_24 + 0.30 * pf_48) if m_48["total_trades"] > 0 else None
        blended_dd = max(m_24["max_drawdown_pct"], m_48["max_drawdown_pct"] * 0.85)

        old_mode = getattr(learner, "current_risk_mode", "NORMAL")
        learner.update_threshold(blended_pf, blended_dd, m_48["total_trades"])
        new_mode = getattr(learner, "current_risk_mode", "NORMAL")

        if old_mode != new_mode and m_48["total_trades"] > 0:
            mode_icons = {"NORMAL": "🟢", "CAUTIOUS": "🟡", "STRICT": "🔴", "AGGRESSIVE": "🚀"}
            self._notify(f"🎚 <b>Режим риска (ИИ) плавно изменен:</b> {mode_icons.get(new_mode, '⚪')} {new_mode}\n<i>(строгость {learner.threshold_adj:+.2f})</i>")

        news_items = await fetch_news_cache()
        regime = self.current_regime
        thrs = get_thresholds(regime)
        policy = get_trade_policy(new_mode)
        current_time = int(time.time())

        # =====================================================================
        # 1. ПРОВЕРКА ОТКРЫТЫХ ПОЗИЦИЙ И ЧАСТЫЕ СТУПЕНЬКИ SL / TP «НА ПРОКОЛ»
        # =====================================================================
        for sym in list(paper.positions.keys()):
            t = tickers.get(sym)
            if not t or sym not in paper.positions:
                continue

            pos_snapshot = paper.positions.get(sym, {})
            score_pos, candles = await live_score(
                sym, t, regime, btc_ret, news_items,
                deriv_t=deriv_tickers.get(sym),
                is_open_pos=True,
                saved_patterns=pos_snapshot.get("patterns"),
                saved_mode=pos_snapshot.get("entry_mode"),
                btc_candles=btc_candles,
                saved_gate_1h=pos_snapshot.get("gate_1h"),
            )
            pos = paper.positions.get(sym)
            if not pos or score_pos is None or not candles:
                continue
            pos["symbol"] = sym

            closes = [c["close"] for c in candles]
            a = atr(candles)
            if a <= 0:
                continue

            last = t["last"]
            pnl_pct = (last - pos["avg"]) / pos["avg"] * 100 if pos["avg"] else 0
            e21_series = ema(closes, 21)
            e21, e50 = e21_series[-1], ema(closes, 50)[-1]

            base = sym[:-4]
            name = await get_coin_name(base)
            pos = paper.positions.get(sym)
            if not pos:
                continue

            neg, pos_news, mentions, _ = check_sentiment(news_items, [base, name])
            is_toxic = neg >= 2 and neg >= (pos_news * 2) and neg >= (mentions * 0.33)

            if is_toxic:
                bot_state.set_cooldown(sym, 7200)
                if pnl_pct >= 0.8 or pnl_pct <= -0.3:
                    if sym not in paper.positions:
                        continue
                    reason = "✂📰 Новости"
                    ex = paper._sell(sym, last, reason, regime_now=regime)
                    paper.log_event(sym, "sell", last, f"Новости {neg}/{mentions}", tp=pos.get("tp"), sl=pos.get("sl"), sup=pos.get("nearest_sup"), res=pos.get("nearest_res"))
                    self._notify(f"💸 <b>Продажа</b> · {pair_html(sym, ex)}{grade_badge(ex)} · {reason} {neg}/{mentions}\n{pnl_emoji(ex['pnl_pct'])} {fmt_pct(ex['pnl_pct'])} · 💵 {usd(ex['pnl'])}")
                    continue

            entry_mode = pos.get("entry_mode", "sniper")
            e21_falling = len(e21_series) >= 4 and e21 < e21_series[-3] * 0.9995
            closed_2_below = (
                len(candles) >= 3 and
                candles[-2]["close"] < (e50 - 0.55 * a) and
                candles[-3]["close"] < (e50 - 0.55 * a)
            )
            trend_broken = (
                closed_2_below and
                last < (e50 - 0.55 * a) and
                (e21 < e50 * 0.997 or e21_falling)
            )
            pos_age = current_time - pos.get("entry_time", current_time)

            if pos_age >= 2400 and not is_toxic and pnl_pct >= 0.45 and entry_mode != "reversal" and trend_broken and sym in paper.positions:
                reason = "🪫 Слом тренда"
                ex = paper._sell(sym, last, reason, regime_now=regime)
                paper.log_event(sym, "sell_profit", last, reason, tp=pos.get("tp"), sl=pos.get("sl"), sup=pos.get("nearest_sup"), res=pos.get("nearest_res"))
                bot_state.record_trade_exit(sym, ex["pnl_pct"], last, is_sl=False)
                shadow.mark_success(sym)
                self._notify(f"💸 <b>Продажа</b> · {pair_html(sym, ex)}{grade_badge(ex)} · {reason}\n{pnl_emoji(ex['pnl_pct'])} {fmt_pct(ex['pnl_pct'])} · 💵 {usd(ex['pnl'])}")
                continue

            is_stagnant = (not pos.get("tp1_done")) and pos_age >= 7200 and (last < e50 * 0.999 and e21 < e50 * 0.999)
            if is_stagnant and pnl_pct >= 0.25 and sym in paper.positions:
                reason = "⏱ Выход по времени (без импульса)"
                ex = paper._sell(sym, last, reason, regime_now=regime)
                paper.log_event(sym, "sell_profit", last, reason, tp=pos.get("tp"), sl=pos.get("sl"), sup=pos.get("nearest_sup"), res=pos.get("nearest_res"))
                bot_state.record_trade_exit(sym, ex["pnl_pct"], last, is_sl=False)
                self._notify(f"⏱ <b>Разгрузка слота</b> · {pair_html(sym, ex)}{grade_badge(ex)} · {reason}\n{pnl_emoji(ex['pnl_pct'])} {fmt_pct(ex['pnl_pct'])} · 💵 {usd(ex['pnl'])}")
                continue

            pos = paper.positions.get(sym)
            if not pos:
                continue

            max_p = max(pos.get("max_price", last), last)
            pos["max_price"] = max_p
            sl_updated, new_sl, new_state, tag_emoji = update_position_sl(pos, max_p, last_price=last, candles_15m=candles)
            if sl_updated and sym in paper.positions:
                old_sl_str = pos.get("_last_notified_sl_str") or fmt_price(pos.get("sl", 0.0))
                new_sl_str = fmt_price(new_sl)
                old_state = pos.get("_last_notified_sl_state") or pos.get("sl_state", "SHIELD")
                pos["sl"] = max(pos.get("sl", 0.0), new_sl)
                pos["sl_state"] = new_state
                pos["max_sl"] = pos["sl"]
                paper.save()
                paper.log_event(sym, "sl_moved", pos["sl"], tag_emoji, tp=pos.get("tp"), sl=pos["sl"], sup=pos.get("nearest_sup"), res=pos.get("nearest_res"))

                last_notify_ts = float(pos.get("_last_sl_notify_ts", 0.0))
                if new_sl_str != old_sl_str and ((current_time - last_notify_ts >= 180) or new_state != old_state):
                    pos["_last_notified_sl_str"] = new_sl_str
                    pos["_last_notified_sl_state"] = new_state
                    pos["_last_sl_notify_ts"] = current_time
                    coin_compact = format_coin_compact(sym, pos)
                    self._notify(f"🛡 SL поднят по {coin_compact} до {new_sl_str} ({tag_emoji})")

            if pos.get("tp1_done"):
                candles_1h = await market_data.get_kline(sym, "60", 250)
                pos = paper.positions.get(sym)
                if not pos:
                    continue
                next_target, can_step, phase_reason = calc_runner_target(sym, last, candles, candles_1h, pos=pos)
                old_tp = float(pos.get("tp", 0.0))
                if can_step and old_tp > 0 and abs(next_target - old_tp) / old_tp >= 0.0018:
                    pos["tp"] = next_target
                    paper.save()
                    step_dir = "⬇️ под прокол" if next_target < old_tp else "⬆️ по волне"
                    paper.log_event(sym, "tp_runner_raised", pos["tp"], f"TP🏃🏻‍➡️ ступень {step_dir}", mode="TP🏃🏻‍➡️", tp=pos["tp"], sl=pos["sl"], sup=pos.get("nearest_sup"), res=pos.get("nearest_res"))
                    if abs(next_target - old_tp) / old_tp * 100 >= 0.85:
                        sl_tag = sl_emoji(pos.get("sl_state", "TRAILING_RUNNER"))
                        self._notify(f"TP🏃🏻‍➡️ <b>Ступень тейка ({step_dir})</b> · {pair_html(sym, pos)}\n🎯 {fmt_price(pos['tp'])} · {sl_tag} {fmt_price(pos['sl'])}")
            else:
                peak_pct = (max_p - pos["avg"]) / pos["avg"] * 100 if pos["avg"] > 0 else 0.0
                pulled_back = (max_p - last) >= 0.25 * a
                if pos_age >= 1800 and peak_pct >= 0.70 and pulled_back and last >= pos["avg"] * 0.998:
                    step_down_tp1 = _round_px(max(pos["avg"] * 1.0075, last + 0.50 * a, e21 + 0.55 * a))
                    if step_down_tp1 < pos["tp"] * 0.9980:
                        pos["tp"] = step_down_tp1
                        paper.save()
                        paper.log_event(sym, "tp_runner_raised", pos["tp"], "🎯 TP1 снижен под прокол", mode="TP1", tp=pos["tp"], sl=pos["sl"], sup=pos.get("nearest_sup"), res=pos.get("nearest_res"))

        # =====================================================================
        # 2. ПРОВЕРКА ОЖИДАЮЩИХ ОРДЕРОВ
        # =====================================================================
        for order in list(paper.orders):
            if order not in paper.orders:
                continue
            sym = order["symbol"]
            t = tickers.get(sym)
            if not t:
                continue

            base = sym[:-4]
            name = await get_coin_name(base)
            if order not in paper.orders:
                continue
            neg, pos_news, mentions, _ = check_sentiment(news_items, [base, name])

            if neg >= 2 and neg >= (pos_news * 2) and neg >= (mentions * 0.33):
                paper.cancel_order(order["id"])
                paper.log_event(sym, "cancel", t["last"], "Токсичные новости", tp=order.get("tp"), sl=order.get("sl"), sup=order.get("nearest_sup"), res=order.get("nearest_res"))
                bot_state.set_cooldown(sym, 7200)
                logger.info(f"PositionManager: ⚠️ Ордер {sym} снят -> токсичные новости ({neg}/{mentions})")
                self._notify(f"⚠️ Снят · {pair_html(sym, order)} · ☠️⚰️ (новости)")
                continue

            entry_mode = order.get("entry_mode", "sniper")
            patterns = order.get("patterns", [])
            ord_gate_1h = order.get("gate_1h", "")

            score_now, candles = await live_score(
                sym, t, regime, btc_ret, news_items,
                deriv_t=deriv_tickers.get(sym),
                is_open_pos=True,
                saved_patterns=patterns,
                saved_mode=entry_mode,
                btc_candles=btc_candles,
                saved_gate_1h=ord_gate_1h,
            )
            if order not in paper.orders or score_now is None or not candles:
                continue

            closes = [c["close"] for c in candles]
            e21_series = ema(closes, 21)
            e21, e50 = e21_series[-1], ema(closes, 50)[-1]
            a = atr(candles)
            if a <= 0:
                continue

            order_age = current_time - order["created"]
            tier = order.get("tier") or "SMALL"
            is_accum = "vcp" in patterns
            thr = thrs.get(entry_mode, 6.0)
            init_score = order.get("initial_score", order.get("score", thr))

            is_post_pump, pp_reason = is_post_pump_dump(candles)
            if is_post_pump:
                paper.cancel_order(order["id"])
                paper.log_event(sym, "cancel", t["last"], f"Пост-памп слив ({pp_reason})", tp=order.get("tp"), sl=order.get("sl"), sup=order.get("nearest_sup"), res=order.get("nearest_res"))
                bot_state.set_cooldown(sym, 1800)
                logger.info(f"PositionManager: ⚠️ Ордер {sym} снят -> ☠️⚰️ {pp_reason}")
                self._notify(f"⚠️ Снят · {pair_html(sym, order)} · ☠️⚰️ (пост-памп слив)")
                continue

            curr_c = candles[-1]
            closed_c = candles[-2]
            ord_sup = order.get("nearest_sup")
            ord_sl = order.get("sl", 0.0)
            sup_broken = bool(ord_sup and closed_c["close"] < ord_sup * 0.992 and t["last"] < ord_sup * 0.992)

            near_sl_danger = bool(ord_sl > 0 and t["last"] <= (ord_sl + 0.30 * a))
            is_dip_order = (ord_gate_1h == "1H_DIP_BOUNCE")

            # Для 1H_DIP_BOUNCE (вход на дне часовой просадки, где цена заведомо под 15m EMA50)
            # НЕ отменяем ордер из-за last < e50, отменяем только при пробое опоры или угрозе стопа!
            sniper_broken = (
                entry_mode != "reversal" and
                not is_dip_order and
                t["last"] < (e50 - 0.65 * a) and
                e21 < e50 * 0.996 and
                (near_sl_danger or score_now < thr - 1.0)
            )
            dip_or_rev_broken = (
                (entry_mode == "reversal" or is_dip_order) and
                (sup_broken or near_sl_danger or (curr_c["open"] - curr_c["close"]) > 0.95 * a)
            )

            if sniper_broken or dip_or_rev_broken:
                paper.cancel_order(order["id"])
                paper.log_event(sym, "cancel", t["last"], f"Слом структуры ({init_score:.1f}-{score_now:.1f})", tp=order.get("tp"), sl=order.get("sl"), sup=order.get("nearest_sup"), res=order.get("nearest_res"))
                bot_state.set_cooldown(sym, 900)
                logger.info(f"PositionManager: ⚠️ Ордер {sym} снят -> ☠️⚰️ слом структуры/нож ({init_score:.1f} -> {score_now:.1f})")
                self._notify(f"⚠️ Снят · {pair_html(sym, order)} · ☠️⚰️ ({init_score:.1f}-{score_now:.1f})")
                continue

            base_intact = (
                (not sup_broken and not near_sl_danger)
                if is_dip_order
                else (entry_mode != "reversal" and t["last"] >= e50 * 0.996 and e21 >= e50 * 0.997)
            )
            if order_age >= 1500 and not base_intact and score_now < (thr - 1.8):
                paper.cancel_order(order["id"])
                paper.log_event(sym, "cancel", t["last"], f"Ослаб ({score_now:.1f})", tp=order.get("tp"), sl=order.get("sl"), sup=order.get("nearest_sup"), res=order.get("nearest_res"))
                bot_state.set_cooldown(sym, 600)
                logger.info(f"PositionManager: ⚠️ Ордер {sym} снят -> 🪫📉 ослаб сигнал ({score_now:.1f})")
                self._notify(f"⚠️ Снят · {pair_html(sym, order)} · 🪫📉 ({score_now:.1f})")
                continue

            if t["last"] > order["price"] + 2.6 * a:
                paper.cancel_order(order["id"])
                paper.log_event(sym, "cancel", t["last"], "Улетела без нас", tp=order.get("tp"), sl=order.get("sl"), sup=order.get("nearest_sup"), res=order.get("nearest_res"))
                bot_state.set_cooldown(sym, 300)
                logger.info(f"PositionManager: ⚠️ Ордер {sym} снят -> 🚀👋🏻 улетела без нас")
                self._notify(f"⚠️ Снят · {pair_html(sym, order)} · 🚀👋🏻")
                continue

            max_order_age = 3600 if (entry_mode == "reversal" or is_dip_order) else 2700
            if order_age > max_order_age:
                if score_now >= (thr + 0.5) and order.get("extensions", 0) < 1 and base_intact:
                    order["extensions"] = order.get("extensions", 0) + 1
                    order["created"] = current_time - (max_order_age - 1800)
                    paper.save()
                    logger.info(f"PositionManager: ⏳ Ордер {sym} продлён на 30 мин (сильный сигнал {score_now:.1f}/{thr:g})")
                else:
                    paper.cancel_order(order["id"])
                    paper.log_event(sym, "cancel", t["last"], f"Тайм-аут ({init_score:.1f}-{score_now:.1f})", tp=order.get("tp"), sl=order.get("sl"), sup=order.get("nearest_sup"), res=order.get("nearest_res"))
                    bot_state.set_cooldown(sym, 300)
                    logger.info(f"PositionManager: ⚠️ Ордер {sym} снят -> ⌛ тайм-аут ({init_score:.1f}-{score_now:.1f})")
                    self._notify(f"⚠️ Снят · {pair_html(sym, order)} · ⌛ Тайм-аут ({init_score:.1f}-{score_now:.1f})")
                    continue

            last_move_ts = order.get("last_move_ts", order["created"])
            if (current_time - last_move_ts) < 480:
                continue

            atr_pct = a / t["last"] * 100
            off = entry_offset(score_now, thr, regime, atr_pct, entry_mode, tier=tier, is_accumulation=is_accum, symbol=sym)
            ideal_price = min(t["last"] * (1 + off), t.get("bid1", t["last"]) * 0.9992)
            old_price = order["price"]

            candles_1h = await market_data.get_kline(sym, "60", 250)
            if order not in paper.orders:
                continue
            nearest_res, res_touches, nearest_sup, sup_touches = find_sr_levels(candles, candles_1h)
            live_sup = nearest_sup if (nearest_sup and sup_touches >= 2) else order.get("nearest_sup")
            live_sup_touches = sup_touches if (nearest_sup and sup_touches >= 2) else order.get("sup_touches", 2 if order.get("nearest_sup") else 0)

            target_price = old_price
            action_type = None
            price_icon = ""
            dev_pct = abs(ideal_price - old_price) / old_price * 100
            in_freeze_zone = abs(t["last"] - old_price) <= (0.35 * a) or (abs(t["last"] - old_price) / old_price * 100) <= 0.28

            if ideal_price < old_price and dev_pct >= 0.40 and not in_freeze_zone:
                if not (live_sup and ideal_price < live_sup * 1.0015):
                    target_price = ideal_price
                    action_type = "correct"
                    price_icon = "⬇️"
            elif (
                regime == "bull" and
                ideal_price > old_price and
                order_age >= 720 and
                order.get("requotes_up", 0) < 1 and
                score_now >= thr + 1.2 and
                e21 > e50 and
                0.28 <= dev_pct <= 0.65
            ):
                target_price = ideal_price
                action_type = "pull_up"
                price_icon = "⬆️"

            if not action_type:
                continue

            all_res, all_sup = get_all_sr_levels(candles, candles_1h)
            res_above_target = [(r[0], r[1]) for r in all_res if r[0] > target_price * 1.0005]
            if res_above_target:
                res_above_target.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
                entry_res, entry_res_touches = res_above_target[0]
            else:
                entry_res, entry_res_touches = None, 0

            sup_below_target = [(s[0], s[1]) for s in all_sup if s[0] <= target_price * 1.0010]
            if sup_below_target:
                sup_below_target.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)
                entry_sup, entry_sup_touches = sup_below_target[0]
            else:
                entry_sup, entry_sup_touches = live_sup, live_sup_touches

            min_rr = policy["min_rr_sniper"] if entry_mode == "sniper" else policy["min_rr_alt"]
            recent_low_6 = min(c["low"] for c in candles[-6:]) if len(candles) >= 6 else None

            closes_1h = [c["close"] for c in candles_1h] if candles_1h and len(candles_1h) >= 50 else []
            e21_1h_val = ema(closes_1h, 21)[-1] if closes_1h else order.get("e21_1h")
            e50_1h_val = ema(closes_1h, 50)[-1] if closes_1h else order.get("e50_1h")

            sl, tp, is_valid, _ = calc_initial_sl_tp(
                symbol=sym, entry=target_price, tier=tier, a=a,
                sup=entry_sup, sup_touches=entry_sup_touches,
                res=entry_res, res_touches=entry_res_touches,
                is_mom=(entry_mode == "rocket"), entry_mode=entry_mode,
                min_rr=min_rr,
                recent_low_6=recent_low_6,
                ema50=e50,
                e21_1h=e21_1h_val,
                e50_1h=e50_1h_val,
            )

            if is_valid and action_type and order in paper.orders:
                old_notional = order["qty"] * order["price"]
                order["price"] = target_price
                order["qty"] = old_notional / target_price if target_price > 0 else order["qty"]
                order["sl"] = sl
                order["tp"] = tp
                order["nearest_sup"] = entry_sup
                order["sup_touches"] = entry_sup_touches
                order["nearest_res"] = entry_res
                order["res_touches"] = entry_res_touches
                order["last_move_ts"] = current_time
                order["requotes"] = order.get("requotes", 0) + 1
                if action_type == "pull_up":
                    order["requotes_up"] = order.get("requotes_up", 0) + 1
                paper.save()

                msg_desc = f"{dev_pct:.2f}%"
                logger.info(f"PositionManager: 📐 Сдвиг ордера {sym} ({action_type}) -> new_entry=${fmt_price(target_price)}, SL=${fmt_price(sl)}")
                paper.log_event(sym, "order_moved", order["price"], f"Сдвиг: {action_type}", tp=order["tp"], sl=order["sl"], sup=entry_sup, res=entry_res)
                self._notify(f"📐 <b>Сдвиг {price_icon}</b> · {pair_html(sym, order)}\n📥 {fmt_price(order['price'])} ({off * 100:+.2f}%) · 🛡📉 {fmt_price(order['sl'])} · {msg_desc}")

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/workers/position_manager.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/workers/scanner.py ---
import asyncio
from loguru import logger
from bot.core.event_bus import EventBus
from bot.exchange.market_data import market_data
from bot.strategy.scanner import get_regime, scan
from bot.core.state import bot_state

class ScannerWorker:
    """Тяжелый I/O воркер: собирает метрики, анализирует RSI/EMA, генерирует сигналы."""
    
    def __init__(self, bus: EventBus):
        self.bus = bus

    async def run(self):
        logger.info("🔎 Scanner Worker запущен: параллельное сканирование рынка каждые 60с...")
        while True:
            if bot_state.paused or not bot_state.trading_enabled:
                await asyncio.sleep(10)
                continue
                
            try:
                tickers = await market_data.get_tickers()
                deriv_tickers = await market_data.get_derivatives_tickers()
                
                if tickers:
                    regime, info = await get_regime()
                    
                    # ⚡ Уведомление о смене рынка
                    if getattr(bot_state, "last_notified_regime", None) != regime:
                        bot_state.last_notified_regime = regime
                        reg_icon = "🟢" if regime == "bull" else "🔴" if regime == "bear" else "🟡"
                        self.bus.publish("NOTIFY", {"text": f"🧭 <b>Макро-тренд сменился:</b> {reg_icon} {regime.upper()}", "urgent": False})
                    
                    # ⚡ Кэшируем глобально, чтобы /status в Телеге отвечал моментально
                    bot_state.current_regime = regime 
                    self.bus.publish("REGIME_UPDATED", {"regime": regime, "info": info})
                    
                    candidates = await scan(regime, tickers, deriv_tickers, limit=20)
                    
                    if candidates:
                        self.bus.publish("SIGNALS_READY", {
                            "candidates": candidates,
                            "tickers": tickers,
                            "regime": regime
                        })
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                logger.exception(f"ScannerWorker error: {e}")
                
            # БЫЛО: await asyncio.sleep(60)
            # СТАЛО: Сканируем раз в 3 минуты. Идеально для 15m таймфрейма.
            await asyncio.sleep(180)

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/workers/scanner.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/utils/__init__.py ---
def fmt_price(p) -> str:
    """Красивая цена: адаптивное число знаков."""
    p = float(p)
    if p >= 1000:
        return f"{p:,.2f}"
    if p >= 1:
        return f"{p:.4f}"
    if p >= 0.01:
        return f"{p:.6f}"
    return f"{p:.8f}"


def fmt_usdt(x) -> str:
    return f"{float(x):.2f}"


def fmt_pct(x) -> str:
    return f"{float(x):+.2f}%"


def fmt_sym(s) -> str:
    if s.endswith("USDT"):
        return s[:-4] + "/USDT"
    return s

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/utils/__init__.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/utils/format.py ---
import os
from bot.news.cmc import TIER_EMOJI

# ===== ТОЛКОВЫЙ СЛОВАРЬ ЭМОДЗИ (EMOJI LEXICON) =====
SL_STATE_EMOJI = {
    "SHIELD": "🛡📉",          # Первичный стоп от слива
    "BREAKEVEN": "🛡🔒",       # Безубыток (перенос в ноль / защиту)
    "PROFIT_LOCK": "🛡💼",     # Профит-лок (фиксация накопленной прибыли ДО TP1)
    "TRAILING_RUNNER": "🛡📈", # Трейлинг (динамический коридор ПОСЛЕ TP1)
    "ABOVE_TP1": "🛡🔥",       # Стоп выше TP1 (гарантированный супер-профит выше 1-й цели)
}

MODE_EMOJI = {
    "rocket": "🚀",
    "reversal": "🧲",
    "sniper": "🏹",
}


def sl_emoji(state: str) -> str:
    return SL_STATE_EMOJI.get(state or "SHIELD", "🛡📉")


def mode_emoji(data_obj: dict) -> str:
    if not isinstance(data_obj, dict):
        return "🏹"
    emode = data_obj.get("entry_mode", "")
    if emode == "reversal":
        return "🧲"
    if emode == "rocket" or data_obj.get("is_momentum"):
        return "🚀"
    return "🏹"


def grade_badge(data_obj: dict) -> str:
    if isinstance(data_obj, dict) and data_obj.get("grade") == "A+":
        return " [💎]"
    return ""


def fmt_price(p) -> str:
    p = float(p)
    if p >= 1000:
        return f"{p:,.2f}"
    if p >= 1:
        return f"{p:.4f}"
    if p >= 0.01:
        return f"{p:.6f}"
    return f"{p:.8f}"


def usd(x) -> str:
    return f"${float(x):,.2f}"


def fmt_usdt(x) -> str:
    return f"{float(x):.2f}"


def fmt_pct(x) -> str:
    return f"{float(x):+.2f}%"


def fmt_sym(s) -> str:
    return s[:-4] + "/USDT" if s.endswith("USDT") else s


def pnl_emoji(x) -> str:
    return "🟢" if x > 0.25 else ("🔴" if x < -0.15 else "🟡")


def weight_emoji(v) -> str:
    return "🔥" if v >= 1.1 else ("🟢" if v >= 0.9 else ("🟡" if v >= 0.7 else "🔻"))


def funding_line(x) -> str:
    return f"\n🏦 в накопления {usd(x)}" if x > 0 else ""


def corr_txt(d) -> str:
    v = d.get("corr") if isinstance(d, dict) else None
    return f" · ₿ {v:.2f}" if v is not None else ""


def format_coin_compact(sym: str, data_obj: dict = None) -> str:
    """Компактный тег монеты для уведомлений SL: 🧲LINK🐋"""
    data_obj = data_obj or {}
    m_tag = mode_emoji(data_obj)
    tier = data_obj.get("tier")
    t_em = TIER_EMOJI.get(tier, "") if tier else ""
    base_sym = sym[:-4] if sym.endswith("USDT") else sym
    public_url = os.getenv("RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL", "https://captain-rost-bot.onrender.com")
    chart_url = f"{public_url}/chart?symbol={base_sym}USDT"
    return f"{m_tag}<a href='{chart_url}'><b>{base_sym}</b></a>{t_em}"


def format_coin(sym: str, data_obj: dict = None) -> str:
    """Стандартный тег монеты с сектором: 🧲 LINK 🐋 · DeFi"""
    data_obj = data_obj or {}
    m_tag = mode_emoji(data_obj)
    tier = data_obj.get("tier")
    em = TIER_EMOJI.get(tier, "") if tier else ""
    sector = data_obj.get("sector") or "Other"

    base_sym = sym[:-4] if sym.endswith("USDT") else sym
    public_url = os.getenv("RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL", "https://captain-rost-bot.onrender.com")
    chart_url = f"{public_url}/chart?symbol={base_sym}USDT"

    return f"{m_tag} <a href='{chart_url}'><b>{base_sym}</b></a>{' ' + em if em else ''} · <i>{sector}</i>"


pair_html = format_coin

--- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА: ./bot/utils/format.py ---

--- НАЧАЛО ФАЙЛА: ./bot/utils/visualizer.py ---
import pandas as pd
import httpx
import plotly.graph_objects as go
from plotly.subplots import make_subplots
import json
import os
import time
from pathlib import Path
from bot.utils.format import fmt_price
from bot.strategy.indicators import get_all_sr_levels

LOG_TIMEZONE = "Europe/Moscow"


class TradeVisualizer:
    def __init__(self, log_path: str, symbol: str, interval: str = "60"):
        self.state_path = Path(os.getenv("STORAGE_DIR", "storage")) / "paper_state.json"
        self.symbol = symbol.upper()
        self.base_sym = self.symbol.replace("USDT", "")
        self.interval = interval

    def fetch_klines(self, start_ts: int, end_ts: int, interval_override: str = None) -> pd.DataFrame:
        url = "https://api.bybit.com/v5/market/kline"
        all_candles = []
        curr_end_ms = end_ts * 1000
        start_ms = start_ts * 1000
        active_interval = str(interval_override or self.interval)

        with httpx.Client(timeout=15) as client:
            while True:
                resp = client.get(url, params={
                    "category": "spot", "symbol": self.symbol,
                    "interval": active_interval, "start": start_ms,
                    "end": curr_end_ms, "limit": 1000
                })
                data = resp.json()
                if data.get("retCode") != 0 or not data.get("result", {}).get("list"):
                    break
                batch = data["result"]["list"]
                all_candles.extend(batch)
                oldest = int(batch[-1][0])
                if len(batch) < 1000 or oldest <= start_ms:
                    break
                curr_end_ms = oldest - 1

        if not all_candles:
            return pd.DataFrame()

        df = pd.DataFrame(all_candles, columns=["start_time", "open", "high", "low", "close", "volume", "turnover"])
        df["start_time"] = pd.to_numeric(df["start_time"])
        for col in ["open", "high", "low", "close", "volume"]:
            df[col] = df[col].astype(float)
        df["datetime"] = pd.to_datetime(df["start_time"], unit="ms", utc=True).dt.tz_convert(LOG_TIMEZONE).dt.tz_localize(None)
        return df.sort_values("datetime").reset_index(drop=True)

    def build_chart(self, show: bool = True):
        if not self.state_path.exists():
            return None

        data = json.loads(self.state_path.read_text())
        events = [e for e in data.get("chart_events", []) if e.get("sym") == self.symbol]
        market_history = data.get("market_history", [])

        if not events:
            return None

        df_ev = pd.DataFrame(events)
        for col in ['tp', 'sl', 'sup', 'res']:
            if col not in df_ev.columns:
                df_ev[col] = None

        df_ev['datetime'] = pd.to_datetime(df_ev['ts'], unit='s', utc=True).dt.tz_convert(LOG_TIMEZONE).dt.tz_localize(None)

        now_ts = int(time.time())
        min_ev_ts = df_ev['ts'].min()
        start_ts = min(min_ev_ts - 7200, now_ts - 48 * 3600)
        end_ts = now_ts + 3600

        df_kline = self.fetch_klines(start_ts, end_ts)
        if df_kline.empty:
            return None

        df_kline['EMA21'] = df_kline['close'].ewm(span=21, adjust=False).mean()
        df_kline['EMA50'] = df_kline['close'].ewm(span=50, adjust=False).mean()

        delta = df_kline['close'].diff()
        gain = delta.where(delta > 0, 0.0).ewm(alpha=1/14, adjust=False).mean()
        loss = (-delta.where(delta < 0, 0.0)).ewm(alpha=1/14, adjust=False).mean()
        rs = gain / loss
        df_kline['RSI'] = 100 - (100 / (1 + rs))

        df_kline = df_kline.reset_index(drop=True)
        min_time = df_kline['datetime'].min()
        max_time = df_kline['datetime'].max()
        df_ev = df_ev[df_ev['datetime'] >= min_time]

        colors_vol = ['rgba(38, 166, 154, 0.5)' if row['close'] >= row['open'] else 'rgba(239, 83, 80, 0.5)' for _, row in df_kline.iterrows()]

        fig = make_subplots(rows=3, cols=1, shared_xaxes=True,
                            vertical_spacing=0.03, row_heights=[0.6, 0.2, 0.2])

        # === 1 СЛОЙ: Свечи и EMA ===
        fig.add_trace(go.Candlestick(
            x=df_kline['datetime'], open=df_kline['open'], high=df_kline['high'],
            low=df_kline['low'], close=df_kline['close'], name='Цена',
            increasing_line_color='rgba(38, 166, 154, 0.8)', decreasing_line_color='rgba(239, 83, 80, 0.8)'
        ), row=1, col=1)

        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=df_kline['datetime'], y=df_kline['EMA21'], mode='lines', name='EMA 21', line=dict(color='rgba(255, 235, 59, 0.8)', width=1.5)), row=1, col=1)
        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=df_kline['datetime'], y=df_kline['EMA50'], mode='lines', name='EMA 50', line=dict(color='rgba(255, 152, 0, 0.8)', width=1.5)), row=1, col=1)

        # Загружаем 15m и 1H свечи за 48+ часов для построения полной карты часовых и 15m уровней
        sr_start_ts = min(start_ts, now_ts - 72 * 3600)
        df_sr_15m = df_kline if self.interval == "15" else self.fetch_klines(sr_start_ts, end_ts, interval_override="15")
        if df_sr_15m.empty:
            df_sr_15m = df_kline

        df_sr_1h = df_kline if self.interval == "60" else self.fetch_klines(sr_start_ts, end_ts, interval_override="60")
        candles_1h_list = df_sr_1h.to_dict('records') if not df_sr_1h.empty else None

        all_res, all_sup = get_all_sr_levels(df_sr_15m.to_dict('records'), candles_1h_list)

        for idx, (r_val, r_tch) in enumerate(all_res):
            fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                x=[min_time, max_time], y=[r_val, r_val],
                mode="lines", name="Сопротивление",
                line=dict(color="rgba(233, 30, 99, 0.50)", width=1.2 if r_tch >= 4 else 1.0, dash="dot"),
                hoverinfo="text+y", text=[f"Сопротивление ({r_tch}x): {fmt_price(r_val)}"] * 2,
                showlegend=(idx == 0)
            ), row=1, col=1)

        for idx, (s_val, s_tch) in enumerate(all_sup):
            fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                x=[min_time, max_time], y=[s_val, s_val],
                mode="lines", name="Поддержка",
                line=dict(color="rgba(33, 150, 243, 0.55)", width=1.2 if s_tch >= 3 else 1.0, dash="dot"),
                hoverinfo="text+y", text=[f"Поддержка ({s_tch}x): {fmt_price(s_val)}"] * 2,
                showlegend=(idx == 0)
            ), row=1, col=1)

        if market_history:
            history_sorted = sorted(market_history, key=lambda x: x.get("ts", 0))
            for i in range(len(history_sorted)):
                item = history_sorted[i]
                t_start_s = pd.to_datetime(item["ts"], unit='s', utc=True).tz_convert(LOG_TIMEZONE).tz_localize(None)
                if i + 1 < len(history_sorted):
                    t_end_s = pd.to_datetime(history_sorted[i+1]["ts"], unit='s', utc=True).tz_convert(LOG_TIMEZONE).tz_localize(None)
                else:
                    t_end_s = max_time

                if t_end_s < min_time:
                    continue
                x0_val = max(t_start_s, min_time)
                x1_val = min(t_end_s, max_time)

                reg = item.get("regime", "neutral")
                reg_color = "rgba(38, 166, 154, 0.08)" if reg == "bull" else ("rgba(239, 83, 80, 0.08)" if reg == "bear" else "rgba(255, 235, 59, 0.04)")
                fig.add_vrect(x0=x0_val, x1=x1_val, fillcolor=reg_color, opacity=1.0, layer="below", line_width=0, row=1, col=1)

        trade_groups = []
        current_trade = []
        for _, ev in df_ev.sort_values('ts').iterrows():
            current_trade.append(ev)
            if ev['type'] in ['sell', 'sell_loss', 'sell_profit', 'tp_runner', 'cancel']:
                trade_groups.append(pd.DataFrame(current_trade))
                current_trade = []
        if current_trade:
            trade_groups.append(pd.DataFrame(current_trade))

        pos = data.get("positions", {}).get(self.symbol)

        for idx, tr_df in enumerate(trade_groups):
            t_start = tr_df['datetime'].min()
            t_end = tr_df['datetime'].max()

            is_active_trade = (idx == len(trade_groups) - 1) and (pos is not None)
            if is_active_trade:
                t_end = max_time
            elif t_start == t_end:
                t_end = t_start + pd.Timedelta(minutes=30)

            is_real_trade = 'buy' in tr_df['type'].values
            label_tag = f"Сделка {idx+1}" if is_real_trade else f"Ордер {idx+1}"

            sl_points = tr_df[tr_df['sl'].notna() & (tr_df['sl'] > 0)]
            if not sl_points.empty:
                sl_x = list(sl_points['datetime'])
                sl_y = list(sl_points['sl'])
                if is_active_trade and pos.get('sl'):
                    sl_x.append(max_time)
                    sl_y.append(pos['sl'])
                elif len(sl_x) == 1:
                    sl_x.append(t_end)
                    sl_y.append(sl_y[0])

                fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                    x=sl_x, y=sl_y,
                    mode="lines+markers",
                    line=dict(color="rgba(255, 23, 68, 0.90)", width=2.0, shape="hv"),
                    marker=dict(size=4, color="red"), name=f"Стоп ({label_tag})",
                    hoverinfo="text+y", text=[f"🛡 SL: {fmt_price(p)}" for p in sl_y]
                ), row=1, col=1)

            tp_points = tr_df[tr_df['tp'].notna() & (tr_df['tp'] > 0)]
            if not tp_points.empty:
                tp_x = list(tp_points['datetime'])
                tp_y = list(tp_points['tp'])
                if is_active_trade and pos.get('tp'):
                    tp_x.append(max_time)
                    tp_y.append(pos['tp'])
                elif len(tp_x) == 1:
                    tp_x.append(t_end)
                    tp_y.append(tp_y[0])

                fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                    x=tp_x, y=tp_y,
                    mode="lines+markers",
                    line=dict(color="rgba(0, 230, 118, 0.90)", width=2.0, shape="hv"),
                    marker=dict(size=4, color="green"), name=f"Тейк ({label_tag})",
                    hoverinfo="text+y", text=[f"🎯 TP: {fmt_price(p)}" for p in tp_y]
                ), row=1, col=1)

        # 2 СЛОЙ: Объем
        fig.add_trace(go.Bar(x=df_kline['datetime'], y=df_kline['volume'], name='Объем', marker_color=colors_vol), row=2, col=1)

        # 3 СЛОЙ: RSI
        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=df_kline['datetime'], y=df_kline['RSI'], mode='lines', name='RSI', line=dict(color='rgba(156, 39, 176, 0.8)', width=1.5)), row=3, col=1)
        fig.add_hline(y=70, line_dash="dash", line_color="rgba(239, 83, 80, 0.5)", row=3, col=1)
        fig.add_hline(y=30, line_dash="dash", line_color="rgba(38, 166, 154, 0.5)", row=3, col=1)

        colors = {
            "order_placed": ("cyan", "line-ew", "Ордер"),
            "order_moved": ("blue", "diamond-open", "Сдвиг"),
            "buy": ("lime", "triangle-up", "Покупка"),
            "tp1": ("#00e676", "star", "TP1 🎯"),
            "tp_runner": ("#00e5ff", "diamond", "TP Runner 🚀"),
            "sell_profit": ("#00e676", "x", "Профит"),
            "sell_loss": ("#ff1744", "x", "Убыток"),
            "sell": ("red", "x", "Продажа"),
            "sl_moved": ("orange", "circle-open", "Трейлинг"),
            "tp_runner_raised": ("#00e5ff", "triangle-up-open", "TP сдвиг"),
            "cancel": ("silver", "x-open", "Отмена")
        }

        for ev_type, (color, symbol, name) in colors.items():
            mask = df_ev['type'] == ev_type
            if mask.any():
                subset = df_ev[mask]

                def format_hover(row):
                    title = f"<b>{row.get('mode', name) or name}</b>"
                    info = f"<br><span style='color: #aaa;'>{row.get('text', '')}</span>" if row.get('text') else ""
                    return title + info

                hover_text = subset.apply(format_hover, axis=1)

                fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                    x=subset['datetime'], y=subset['price'], mode='markers',
                    marker=dict(symbol=symbol, size=12, color=color, line=dict(width=1, color='white')),
                    name=name, text=hover_text, hoverinfo="text+y"
                ), row=1, col=1)

        fig.update_layout(
            title=f"История торгов: {self.symbol} | Капитан Рост", template="plotly_dark", xaxis_rangeslider_visible=False,
            hovermode="x unified", hoverlabel=dict(bgcolor="rgba(20, 20, 20, 0.9)", font_size=13),
            height=850, margin=dict(t=60, b=40, l=40, r=40)
        )

        fig.update_yaxes(title_text="Цена", row=1, col=1)
        fig.update_yaxes(title_text="Объем", row=2, col=1, showticklabels=False)
        fig.update_yaxes(title_text="RSI", range=[0, 100], row=3, col=1, tickvals=[30, 50, 70])

        if show:
            fig.show()
        return fig
